## 连续语言模型与语言流 [[Back]](#toc)

连续 embedding/latent 空间中的语言建模，包括 ELF、CoLa、扩散语言模型与 flow matching language model。

### [1] [Nemotron-Labs-Diffusion: A Tri-Mode Language Model Unifying Autoregressive, Diffusion, and Self-Speculation Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05722)
*Yonggan Fu, Lexington Whalen, Abhinav Garg, Chengyue Wu, Maksim Khadkevich, Nicolai Oswald, Enze Xie, Daniel Egert, Sharath Turuvekere Sreenivas, Shizhe Diao, Chenhan Yu, Ye Yu, Weijia Chen, Sajad Norouzi, Jingyu Liu, Shiyi Lan, Ligeng Zhu, Jin Wang, Jindong Jiang, Morteza Mardani, Mehran Maghoumi, Song Han, Ante Jukić, Nima Tajbakhsh, Jan Kautz, Pavlo Molchanov*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

Subtopic: 连续语言模型与语言流

Importance: 高优先级 (97.15) - Top 2 精读候选

TL;DR: Nemotron-Labs-Diffusion 是一种三模式语言模型，在单一架构中统一了自回归（AR）、扩散和自推测解码。通过联合 AR-扩散训练，模型可在不同部署场景下切换模式以维持高吞吐量。实验表明 AR 和扩散目标互补：扩散提升前瞻规划能力，AR 提供从左到右的语言先验；在自推测模式下，扩散负责草稿生成而 AR 负责验证，在接受率和实际设备效率上均优于多 token 预测方法。扩展到 3B、8B 和 14B 参数后，该模型族（包括基础、指令和视觉语言模型）在准确率和速度上持续超越最先进的开源 AR 和扩散语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归语言模型在推理时受限于逐 token 生成的低效率，而扩散语言模型虽然能并行生成但缺乏从左到右的语言先验。作者希望将两者的优势结合，在单一架构中实现 AR 和扩散的互补，并通过自推测解码进一步提升推理效率。

Method: 提出 Nemotron-Labs-Diffusion 架构，采用联合 AR-扩散训练目标。模型包含三种推理模式：(1) AR 模式：标准从左到右自回归生成；(2) 扩散模式：基于扩散的并行生成；(3) 自推测模式：扩散模型作为草稿模型生成候选 token，AR 模型作为验证模型进行接受/拒绝。模型在训练时同时优化 AR 损失和扩散损失，共享同一组参数。扩展到视觉语言模型时，将视觉编码器与语言模型结合。

Result: Nemotron-Labs-Diffusion-8B 每次前向传播生成的 token 数量是 Qwen3-8B 的 6 倍，在 GB200 GPU 上使用 SGLang 时，SPEED-Bench 吞吐量提升 4 倍。在自推测模式下，扩散草稿+AR 验证的接受率优于多 token 预测方法。光速分析表明，在最优采样器下，扩散模式每次前向传播可生成比自推测模式多 76.5% 的 token。

Conclusion: AR 和扩散语言模型可以互补地统一在单一架构中，实现高效灵活的推理。三模式设计使模型能够根据部署场景动态切换，兼顾准确率和吞吐量。扩散语言模型在并行生成方面的潜力巨大，未来有望进一步提升推理效率。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Nemotron-Labs-Diffusion，统一了自回归（AR）与扩散（diffusion）两种语言建模范式，属于连续语言模型中的扩散语言模型方向。论文核心贡献是将扩散语言模型与自回归语言模型结合，实现三模式解码（AR、扩散、自推测解码），与 canonical 子主题"连续语言模型与语言流"高度匹配。

Importance reason: Unifies autoregressive and diffusion modes with self-speculation decoding, directly advancing continuous language model and efficient inference priorities. Signals: tri-mode language model, joint AR-diffusion training, self-speculation decoding, 6x tokens per forward vs Qwen3-8B, continuous language model

Abstract: We introduce Nemotron-Labs-Diffusion, a tri-mode language model (LM) that unifies AR, diffusion, and self-speculation decoding within a single architecture. Trained with a joint AR-diffusion objective, Nemotron-Labs-Diffusion can switch modes to sustain high throughput across deployment settings and concurrency levels. Our study shows that (1) AR and diffusion objectives are complementary: diffusion improves lookahead planning, while AR provides left-to-right linguistic priors. (2) In self-speculation mode, diffusion drafts while AR verifies, outperforming multi-token prediction (MTP) methods in both acceptance rate and real-device efficiency. (3) A speed-of-light analysis further demonstrates diffusion's long-term potential, with up to 76.5% more tokens per forward pass than self-speculation under an optimal sampler. Scaling to 3B, 8B, and 14B parameters, our Nemotron-Labs-Diffusion family, including base, instruct, and vision-language models, consistently outperforms state-of-the-art open-source AR and diffusion LMs in both accuracy and speed. For example, Nemotron-Labs-Diffusion-8B decodes 6x more tokens per forward than Qwen3-8B with comparable accuracy, translating to 4x higher throughput on SPEED-Bench with SGLang on a GB200 GPU.

</details>


### [2] [Diffusion Language Model Parallel Decoding via Product-of-Experts Bridge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08048)
*Juntong Shi, Brian L. Trippe, Jure Leskovec, Stefano Ermon, Minkai Xu*

Main category: cs.PF, cs.CL, cs.LG

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

Subtopic: 连续语言模型与语言流

Importance: 高优先级 (95.24) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 PoE-Bridge，一种基于专家乘积（Product-of-Experts）桥接分布的扩散语言模型并行解码框架。通过引入 DLM 与 AR 模型之间的中间分布，结合并行草稿、拒绝采样和重要性采样，在数学推理和代码生成任务上以 5 倍加速恢复 AR 模型 95% 以上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型（DLM）具有并行解码的速度优势，但由于缺乏 token 间依赖关系，生成质量远低于自回归（AR）模型。现有方法通过重要性采样用 DLM 作为提议分布、AR 作为目标分布来弥合差距，但两者分布差异巨大，需要大量粒子，计算成本高昂。因此需要一种更高效的解码框架来兼顾速度与质量。

Method: 提出 PoE-Bridge 框架：(1) 构建 DLM 提议与 AR 目标的专家乘积（PoE）作为中间桥接分布；(2) 先用 DLM 并行草稿多个续写候选；(3) 用拒绝采样将候选向 PoE 分布对齐；(4) 再用重要性采样将 PoE 对齐后的候选进一步校正到 AR 目标分布。此外提出混合温度采样增强多样性、弹性拒绝窗口减少验证浪费等改进技术。

Result: 在数学推理和代码生成任务上，PoE-Bridge 相比标准 DLM 解码方法实现 5 倍加速，同时恢复 AR 目标模型至少 95% 的性能，有效弥合了大部分质量差距。

Conclusion: PoE-Bridge 通过引入 PoE 桥接分布，有效弥合了 DLM 与 AR 模型之间的分布差距，在保持 DLM 并行解码速度优势的同时大幅提升了生成质量，为扩散语言模型的高效高质量解码提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究扩散语言模型（DLM）的并行解码方法，属于连续语言模型范畴。映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "连续语言模型与语言流"（continuous_language_flow），该 subtopic 涵盖扩散语言模型、flow matching language model 等连续语言建模方法，与本论文主题高度吻合。

Importance reason: Directly advances continuous language model decoding with a novel PoE bridge, achieving 5x speedup and 95% AR quality recovery, highly relevant to your top-weighted continuous language model subtopic. Signals: continuous language model, diffusion language model, parallel decoding, product-of-experts, bridge distribution

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLMs) offer substantial speed advantages through parallel decoding, but the lack of token dependencies limits generation quality compared to autoregressive (AR) models. Recent progress attempts to bridge the gap via importance sampling, with DLM being the proposal and AR being the target. However, due to the huge gap between their distributions, the sampling requires a large number of particles and is thus expensive to compute. In this paper, we introduce PoE-Bridge, a novel decoding framework that drastically improves generation speed and accuracy by introducing an intermediate distribution to bridge the gap. The distribution is constructed as a Product-of-Experts (PoE) of the DLM proposal and the AR target. With the intermediate distribution, we first use the DLM to draft multiple continuations in parallel, then apply rejection sampling to verify the drafted tokens and move the resulting candidates toward the PoE. We then use importance sampling to further correct the PoE-aligned candidates toward the AR target. We further propose several improved techniques, including mixed-temperature sampling for enhanced diversity and elastic rejection windows for reducing wasted verification. Empirically, PoE-Bridge achieves significantly improved accuracy with $5\times$ speedup over the standard DLM decoding approach, and recovers at least 95% of the target AR model's performance, efficiently advancing most of the quality gap on challenging mathematical reasoning and coding tasks. Our code is available at .

</details>

## 具身世界模型与规划控制 [[Back]](#toc)

面向机器人、具身智能、动作规划和控制的世界模型。

### [3] [Learning 4D Geometric Priors for Inference-Efficient World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05468)
*Jianjun Zhang, Jian Zhu, Taiyi Su, Chong Ma, Zitai Huang, Yi Xu, Hanli Wang*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 值得关注 (69.27) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: MECo-WAM提出一种多专家协同训练的世界动作模型，通过引入4D几何先验（来自冻结VGGT编码器）来增强视频-动作联合表征，同时保持推理时零额外开销。在LIBERO、RoboTwin 2.0及真实机器人操作任务上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAM）在联合建模视觉未来动态和可执行动作序列时，主要优化面向外观的视频潜变量，未能充分捕捉随时间演化的几何信息，而这对于精确的机器人操作至关重要。因此需要在不增加推理成本的前提下，将动作相关的4D几何先验注入视频-动作表征。

Method: 提出MECo-WAM框架，包含：(1) 视频专家和动作专家联合训练，外加一个轻量级4D专家，由冻结的VGGT编码器提供的关系目标监督；(2) 非对称专家可见性设计，防止辅助几何信息到动作生成的因果捷径；(3) 衰减式4D读掩码注意力，训练早期提供受限的当前帧几何引导，逐步移除依赖；(4) 动作感知时序几何蒸馏，对齐帧内几何关系及其时序演化，同时强调与机器人动作最相关的视觉区域。推理时移除所有辅助4D组件。

Result: 在LIBERO上达到98.2%，RoboTwin 2.0上达到92.6%，并在真实世界机器人操作任务上验证了有效性，在不增加推理成本的前提下显著提升了操作性能。

Conclusion: MECo-WAM通过训练阶段注入4D几何先验有效提升了世界动作模型的操作精度，且推理时无额外开销，为机器人操作中的几何感知建模提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出MECo-WAM，一种面向机器人操作的世界动作模型（WAM），通过多专家协同训练注入4D几何先验来提升操作性能，属于具身智能与机器人规划控制范畴，因此映射到[CANONICAL]具身世界模型与规划控制。

Importance reason: Introduces 4D geometric priors for efficient world action models, aligning with world model and efficient inference priorities. Signals: world model, efficient inference, 4D geometric prior, video-action co-training, robotic manipulation

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) have shown strong potential for robotic manipulation by jointly modeling visual future dynamics and executable action sequences. However, existing video-action co-training methods primarily optimize appearance-oriented video latents, which may insufficiently capture the temporally evolving geometry required for precise manipulation. We propose MECo-WAM, a Multi-Expert Co-Training World Action Model that injects action-relevant 4D geometric priors into video-action representations while preserving the original lightweight inference graph. During training, MECo-WAM combines video and action experts with a lightweight 4D expert supervised by relational targets from a frozen VGGT encoder. Asymmetric expert visibility prevents non-causal shortcuts from auxiliary geometry to action generation. To transfer geometric knowledge into the deployed video-action pathway, we introduce decayed 4D read-mask attention, which provides restricted current-frame geometric guidance early in training and progressively removes this dependency. We further propose action-aware temporal geometric distillation, which aligns within-frame geometric relations and their temporal evolution while emphasizing visual regions most relevant to robot actions. At deployment, all auxiliary 4D components are removed. Experiments on LIBERO (98.2%), RoboTwin 2.0 (92.6%), and challenging real-world manipulation tasks show that MECo-WAM improves manipulation performance without increasing inference cost.

</details>


### [4] [GEM-Occ: From Visual Geometry Evidence to Embodied Semantic Occupancy Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05543)
*Hu Zhu, Bohan Li, Xianda Guo, Hongsi Liu, Baorui Peng, Mingqi Yuan, Xin Jin, Wenjun Zeng, Chang Wen Chen*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 普通相关 (59.89) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出HIOcc层次化室内占据基准和GEM-Occ高斯证据记忆框架，将局部视觉几何预测转化为语义高斯占据证据和自由空间射线证据，通过可见性与不确定性感知的因果更新融合为持久层次化记忆，支持局部预测、房间级在线建图和建筑级跨连接空间建图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有室内占据基准和方法主要关注单视图预测或房间级在线感知，缺乏对跨连接室内空间的长时序语义建图的探索。

Method: 提出GEM-Occ框架，将局部视觉几何预测视为瞬态证据，转化为语义高斯占据证据和自由空间射线证据，通过可见性与不确定性感知的因果更新融合到持久层次化记忆中。记忆组织为局部缓存、房间级子图和建筑级图结构，可通过高斯到占据的泼溅查询。

Result: 在HIOcc基准上，GEM-Occ在局部占据预测、在线地图稳定性、自由空间推理、回访一致性和建筑级可扩展性方面均优于先前的室内占据和高斯建图基线方法。

Conclusion: GEM-Occ通过将视觉几何证据转化为高斯占据记忆，有效实现了长时序、跨空间的层次化语义占据建图，为具身智能体提供了结构化的空间记忆。

Classification reason: 该论文提出GEM-Occ，一种面向具身智能体的语义占据记忆框架，用于室内环境的层次化语义占据建图。这属于具身智能体在室内环境中的空间记忆与感知建模，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]方向高度吻合。

Importance reason: Strong in world model and embodied mapping but lacks direct connection to continuous language/multimodal models or autoregressive generation. Signals: world model, embodied semantic occupancy, Gaussian evidence memory, hierarchical mapping, indoor occupancy benchmark

Abstract: Semantic occupancy provides a structured spatial memory for embodied indoor agents by jointly representing occupied regions, observed free space, unknown areas, and object semantics. However, existing indoor occupancy benchmarks and methods mainly focus on single-view prediction or room-level online perception, leaving long-horizon semantic mapping across connected indoor spaces underexplored. We introduce HIOcc, a hierarchical indoor occupancy benchmark that unifies ScanNet, ScanNet++, and Matterport3D under a common sparse semantic occupancy format while preserving their native observation geometries, including perspective RGB-D frames and pano-centric observation groups. HIOcc supports three complementary evaluation regimes: local semantic occupancy prediction, room-level online occupancy mapping, and building-level mapping across connected panoramic environments. We further propose GEM-Occ, a Gaussian Evidence Memory framework for semantic occupancy mapping. Rather than using pointmaps as persistent map states, GEM-Occ treats local visual geometry predictions as transient evidence, converts them into semantic Gaussian occupancy evidence and free-space ray evidence, and fuses them into a persistent hierarchical memory through visibility- and uncertainty-aware causal updates. The memory is organized into local caches, room-level submaps, and a building-level graph, and can be queried at any time through Gaussian-to-occupancy splatting. Experiments on HIOcc show that GEM-Occ improves local occupancy prediction, online map stability, free-space reasoning, revisit consistency, and building-level scalability over prior indoor occupancy and Gaussian-based mapping baselines.

</details>


### [5] [DexTele: A Dual-Arm Dexterous Teleoperation System Based on Motion Retargeting and Adaptive Force Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05883)
*Yuanchuan Lai, Qing Gao, Ziyan Liang, Xianfeng Cheng, Junjie Hu, Zhaojie Ju*

Main category: cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 普通相关 (54.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出DexTele双灵巧臂遥操作系统，通过基于视觉的运动重定向模块（运动图编码器+潜空间优化）实现跨平台精确运动映射，并结合VLM与MPC的自适应抓取模块实现柔顺抓取力预测与在线优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双灵巧臂遥操作中，跨平台运动重定向的泛化能力和抓取的交互性至关重要，但机器人架构异构性和抓取物体多样性给精确运动重定向和柔顺抓取带来巨大挑战。

Method: 1) 基于视觉的运动重定向模块：从人体图像生成初步机器人运动，采用运动图编码器和潜空间优化实现精确便捷的跨平台运动重定向。2) 自适应抓取模块：结合视觉语言模型(VLM)与模型预测控制(MPC)，预测目标物体所需抓取力并进行基于梯度的在线优化。

Result: 大量实验表明，DexTele在多个机器人平台上实现了精确的运动重定向和柔顺抓取，具有良好的跨平台泛化能力。

Conclusion: DexTele通过运动重定向与自适应力控制相结合，有效解决了双灵巧臂遥操作中的跨平台泛化和柔顺抓取难题，验证了VLM+MPC框架在机器人抓取力控制中的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出双灵巧臂遥操作系统DexTele，涉及运动重定向与自适应力控制，属于机器人具身智能与规划控制范畴，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]（embodied_world_model）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on robotic teleoperation, not directly on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Dual-arm teleoperation, motion retargeting, adaptive force control, VLM+MPC grasping, cross-platform generalization

Abstract: In dual-arm dexterous teleoperation, cross-platform generalization of motion retargeting and interactivity of grasping are crucial. However, the heterogeneity of robotic architectures and the wide variety of grasping objects pose significant challenges to achieving precise motion retargeting and compliant grasping in dual-arm dexterous teleoperation. To address these challenges, a dual-arm dexterous teleoperation system (DexTele) is proposed based on motion retargeting and adaptive force control. First, a vision-based motion retargeting module is designed to generate preliminary robot motions from human images. In this module, a motion-graph encoder and latent optimization are proposed for precise and convenient cross-platform motion retargeting. Second, an adaptive grasping module is designed to achieve compliant grasping. This module combines a vision-language model (VLM) with model predictive control (MPC), allowing the system to predict the required grasping force for a target object and perform gradient-based online optimization. Finally, extensive experiments demonstrate that the DexTele achieves precise motion retargeting and compliant grasping with generalization across multiple robot platforms.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 收集真实世界人机交互数据集，同步记录全身人体运动与双手交互力；提出力感知评估指标 FATS 及辅助诊断指标；建立统一基准协议，在仿真中重放记录的交互力，提供标准化评估接口。

Result: 实验表明，力感知评估能够揭示传统无外力评估下隐藏的显著性能差异，不同全身控制策略在力交互场景下表现差异明显。

Conclusion: ThorArena 提供了一个实用且可复现的力感知人形机器人交互研究框架，为评估接触丰富的人形机器人行为提供了新基准。

Classification reason: 该论文研究人形机器人的物理交互基准测试，涉及全身运动跟踪与交互力感知，属于具身智能中的机器人控制与评估，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]方向高度相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on humanoid robotics force interaction, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: humanoid robot, force-aware evaluation, whole-body control, contact-rich tasks, benchmark

Abstract: Humanoid robots are increasingly expected to perform contact-rich tasks that require not only accurate whole-body motion but also robust physical interaction with surrounding objects and humans. Although recent advances in humanoid motion imitation and whole-body control have achieved remarkable tracking performance, existing datasets and benchmarks primarily focus on kinematic motion while largely overlooking synchronized interaction forces. As a result, current evaluations fail to capture how external interaction forces affect tracking accuracy, stability, and control robustness. In this paper, we present ThorArena, a benchmark for evaluating force-aware humanoid interaction based on human demonstrations with synchronized motion and force measurements. We collect a real-world interaction dataset that simultaneously captures whole-body human motion and forces exerted by both hands across six representative physical interaction tasks. Based on these demonstrations, we propose force-aware evaluation metrics that jointly assess whole-body tracking accuracy, robustness under different force levels, control effort, and episode survival through the Force-Aware Tracking Score (FATS) and complementary diagnostic metrics. We further establish a unified benchmark protocol that replays recorded interaction forces in simulation and provides a standardized evaluation interface for different humanoid control policies. Experiments on representative whole-body control policies demonstrate that force-aware evaluation reveals substantial performance differences that remain largely hidden under conventional no-force evaluation. ThorArena provides a practical and reproducible framework for studying force-aware humanoid interaction and offers a new benchmark for evaluating contact-rich humanoid behaviors.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时域家务任务需要机器人组合多个语言条件技能，但技能之间的边界往往不明确。一个技能可能满足自身后置条件，却使机器人、物体或相机视角处于下一个技能无法可靠启动的状态。现有工作缺乏对这种"语义交接"问题的系统诊断。

Method: 提出一个基于智能体编排的VLA执行框架：使用从清理后的BEHAVIOR-1K演示训练的π₀.₅技能检查点，为每个技能分配类型化参数和步骤预算，利用多视角VLM验证决定执行是否前进、重试或重新规划。通过对比两种初始状态分布（干净技能边界快照 vs. 链式终端状态）来分离单技能能力与长时域组合鲁棒性。

Result: 选定的导航、抓取、放置和开门技能在人工审核验证下从干净快照的成功率为77-100%，但组合执行时频繁因链式状态而停滞。执行轨迹将失败归因于下一技能就绪性、目标定位和底层控制执行三个方面。

Conclusion: 揭示了单技能成功与可靠长时域任务完成之间的显著差距，将近乎为零的端到端任务成功率转化为可操作的诊断信息。未来VLA技能库必须学习对混乱链式状态分布的鲁棒性，而干净演示系统性地低估了这种分布。

Classification reason: 该论文研究机器人长时域任务中的技能组合与语义交接问题，使用VLA（视觉-语言-动作）模型在BEHAVIOR-1K环境中执行导航、抓取、放置等技能，属于具身智能与机器人规划控制范畴，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on robotics skill composition failure diagnosis, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: robotics, skill composition, semantic handoff, BEHAVIOR-1K, VLA

Abstract: Long-horizon household tasks require robots to compose many language-conditioned skills, yet the boundary between consecutive skills is rarely explicit. A skill may satisfy its own postcondition while leaving the robot, objects, or camera views in a state from which the next skill cannot reliably start. We study this semantic handoff problem in BEHAVIOR-1K through an agent-orchestrated vision-language-action execution harness. The harness invokes $\pi_{0.5}$-based skill checkpoints trained from cleaned BEHAVIOR-1K demonstrations, assigns each skill typed arguments and a step budget, and uses multi-view vision-language model verification to decide whether execution should advance, retry, or replan. To separate isolated skill competence from long-horizon compositional robustness, we evaluate the same checkpoints under two initial-state distributions: clean skill-boundary snapshots and chained terminal states produced by previous skills. Selected navigation, grasping, placement, and door-opening skills achieve 77--100% success from clean snapshots under human-reviewed verification, yet composed rollouts still frequently stall from chained states. Execution traces attribute these failures to next-skill readiness, target grounding, and low-level control execution, revealing a substantial gap between single-skill success and reliable long-horizon task completion. These findings turn near-zero end-to-end task success into actionable diagnostics, showing that future VLA skill libraries must learn robustness to the messy chained-state distribution that clean demonstrations systematically underrepresent.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出最优传输Q学习（OTQL），一种通过RL后训练微调并加速次优流策略的方法。OTQL利用优势加权条件最优传输流匹配，在50-60轮交互预算内即可提升单任务策略成功率从36%到86%，预训练VLA从38%到76%，同时减少70%推理步数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散和流策略在机器人应用中表现出色，但高质量策略需要快速推理和高质量演示数据，这些往往难以获得。缺乏这些条件会导致策略行为次优且在分布偏移下失败。因此需要一种利用机器人自身经验通过RL后训练来微调和加速次优流策略的方法。

Method: 提出OTQL（Optimal Transport Q-Learning），结合Q学习与最优传输流匹配。核心是优势加权条件最优传输流匹配（advantage weighted conditional optimal transport flow matching），通过机器人自身交互经验进行RL后训练，无需昂贵的蒸馏过程。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人任务中，OTQL在50-60轮交互预算内将单任务流策略成功率从36%提升至86%，预训练VLA策略从38%提升至76%，同时将每次动作生成的推理步数减少70%。

Conclusion: OTQL提供了一种高效、实用的方法，通过少量机器人交互经验即可显著提升流策略的性能和推理速度，避免了传统蒸馏方法的高计算成本，为机器人流策略的后训练优化提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文关注机器人流策略（flow policy）的微调与加速，使用RL后训练优化具身机器人策略，属于具身世界模型与规划控制范畴，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "具身世界模型与规划控制"。

Importance reason: Novel RL post-training for flow policies with strong efficiency gains, but focuses on robotics rather than core continuous language/multimodal models. Signals: Optimal transport Q-learning, flow policy acceleration, RL post-training, 70% inference step reduction, robotics VLA fine-tuning

Abstract: Diffusion and flow policies have recently demonstrated remarkable performance in robotic applications by accurately capturing multimodal robot trajectory distributions, especially in the context of vision language action (VLA) models. However, high quality policy performance also requires fast inference and high quality demonstrations, which are often hard to get. Lack of these leads to suboptimal policy behaviors and failure under distribution shifts. In this work we address the problem of fine-tuning and accelerating suboptimal flow-based policies using the robot's experience through RL post-training. We introduce Optimal Transport Q-Learning (OTQL), a new method for finetuning flow policies using advantage weighted conditional optimal transport flow matching. OTQL can finetune and accelerate flows with an interaction budget of 50-60 episodes while avoiding computationally expensive distillation in simulation and real-world robot tasks. Our results show that OTQL post-trains flow policies using the robot's own experience, increasing average success percentage of single-task policies from 36% to 86% and of a pre-trained VLA from 38% to 76% while reducing the number of inference steps per action generation by 70%.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出 ActionCache，一种即插即用的外部缓存机制，通过复用过去中间动作来加速流匹配 VLA 模型的推理，在保持高任务成功率的同时实现高达 11.75× 和 34.43× 的推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配 VLA 模型在机器人操作中表现出色，但其动作头的迭代去噪过程是计算瓶颈，严重限制了实时部署能力。现有加速方法（如蒸馏、少步采样）需要额外训练或牺牲质量，亟需一种无需训练、即插即用的加速方案。

Method: ActionCache 是一个外部缓存模块，存储过去推理中的中间动作（intermediate actions）及其紧凑多模态键（compact multimodal keys）。在推理时，通过检索相似历史上下文（跨不同 episode 甚至不同任务）来获取缓存的中间动作，作为当前去噪过程的 warm-start，从而大幅减少所需的去噪步数。该方法是即插即用的，无需额外训练。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境中，ActionCache 在低延迟条件下保持了高任务成功率。对 π₀.₅ 和 GR00T-N1.6 两个代表性流匹配 VLA 模型，分别实现了 11.75× 和 34.43× 的推理加速。

Conclusion: ActionCache 通过缓存和复用历史中间动作，有效解决了流匹配 VLA 模型的推理延迟瓶颈，是一种无需训练、即插即用的高效加速方案，适用于多种机器人操作任务。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 ActionCache，一种用于加速流匹配 VLA（Vision-Language-Action）模型推理的缓存机制，面向机器人操作任务。VLA 模型属于具身智能/机器人领域，且涉及动作生成与规划控制，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制。

Importance reason: Proposes a training-free acceleration method for VLA models, directly addressing the user's high-priority model efficiency and world model subtopics. Signals: efficient inference, flow matching acceleration, world model, action caching, VLA model

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as a promising approach for generalizable robotic manipulations. In particular, flow matching-based VLA models have shown remarkable success due to their capability to generate precise and smooth action sequences and capture multimodal distributions. However, the iterative denoising process in the action head acts as a major computational bottleneck, posing a critical challenge for real-time deployment. To address this challenge, we propose ActionCache, a plug-and-play external cache that opportunistically reuses past intermediate actions to warm-start generations from the vicinity of target actions, thereby drastically reducing the inference latency. Specifically, ActionCache stores the intermediate actions with compact multimodal keys, which enables retrieval from similar past contexts across different episodes or even different tasks. Experimental results in simulation and real-world environments demonstrate that ActionCache maintains high task success rates in a low-latency regime, achieving inference acceleration of up to $11.75\times$ and $34.43\times$ for representative flow-based VLA models, $\pi_{0.5}$ and GR00T-N1.6, respectively.

</details>

TL;DR: WristMimic 提出一种腕部引导的全身控制框架，将无接触的身体/腕部运动与接触丰富的手指操作解耦，通过物体跟踪和接触结果学习抓取行为，无需完整手指姿态监督即可实现跨手部形态的重定向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将人类物体交互演示重定向到物理仿真中，不仅需要复现身体运动，还需要复现物体运动和接触力。然而，仅靠位置手部轨迹无法指定操作物体所需的接触力，直接跟踪完整手部姿态会过度约束接触丰富的指部行为。因此需要一种解耦的全身控制方法。

Method: WristMimic 将全身控制分为两个部分：(1) 无接触的身体和腕部通过运动学姿态目标引导；(2) 手指不直接监督人类手部姿态，而是通过物体跟踪和接触结果学习抓取与操作行为。核心洞察是腕部是这两个区域之间的自然门控——它基本无接触、可运动学跟踪，同时决定全局手部配置并将手指置于可抓取范围内。此外引入腕部特定重置约束和奖励优先级以确保交互中腕部放置的可靠性。

Result: 实验表明，WristMimic 在多种物体操作任务上匹配或超越了使用完整手指姿态监督的方法，同时实现了跨不同手部形态（手指不可知）的重定向能力。

Conclusion: WristMimic 通过腕部引导的解耦控制框架，有效解决了接触丰富场景下的人形机器人全身控制问题，减少了对精确手指姿态监督的依赖，提升了跨手部形态的泛化能力。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 WristMimic，一个面向人形机器人全身控制的框架，通过腕部引导实现物体操作任务的物理仿真重定向。这属于具身智能中的机器人控制与操作规划，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong robotics manipulation paper but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: wrist-guided whole-body control, contact-free vs contact-rich decoupling, finger-agnostic retargeting, physics-based simulation, humanoid manipulation

Abstract: Retargeting human object interaction demonstrations to physics based simulation requires reproducing not only body motion but also the object motion and contacts that make manipulation succeed. However, position only hand trajectories do not specify the contact forces needed to manipulate objects, and directly tracking them can overconstrain contact rich finger behavior. We introduce WristMimic, a wrist guided whole body control framework that explicitly separates contact free body motion from contact rich hand manipulation. The contact free body and wrist are guided by kinematic pose targets, whereas the fingers are not directly supervised by human hand pose. Instead, they learn grasping and manipulation behaviors from object tracking and contact outcomes. Our key insight is that the wrist is the natural gate between these two regimes. It is largely free from contact and can be tracked kinematically, yet it determines the global hand configuration and places the fingers within reachable grasp affordances. To ensure reliable wrist placement during interaction, we introduce wrist specific reset constraints and reward prioritization. Experiments show that WristMimic matches or surpasses methods using full finger pose supervision while enabling finger agnostic retargeting across diverse hand embodiments.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出一个开放世界机器人规划框架，使机器人能够自动生成、验证和更新关于其抽象世界模型的假设。该框架利用基础模型生成初始假设，通过自动规划产生同时验证假设和执行任务的行动序列，并在迭代执行中根据验证反馈扩展知识。实验表明该方法能有效支持机器人在未知环境中自主知识扩展与操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统封闭世界方法依赖预编程知识库，当服务机器人在未知环境中遇到意外情况和任务时无法应对。开放世界规划需要机器人具备自主知识扩展能力，但现有方法缺乏对不确定性感知的知识扩展与假设验证的集成。

Method: 提出一个开放世界规划框架，核心思想是显式维护不确定性感知的知识扩展，并将假设验证集成到目标导向规划中。框架利用基础模型（如VLM）生成关于状态和转移的初始假设，应用自动规划生成同时验证假设和执行任务的行动序列，通过迭代执行和精炼，在假设错误时利用基础模型的验证反馈来扩展知识。

Result: 在模拟和真实环境中的大量实验表明，该框架能够实现自主知识扩展，并在开放世界环境中有效运行。机器人能够成功处理未知物体和动作，通过假设驱动的方式逐步完善世界模型。

Conclusion: 将不确定性感知的模型扩展与机器人基础模型和规划相结合，推动了家用服务机器人在开放世界中的实际部署。该框架为机器人在未知环境中自主学习和适应提供了有效途径。

Classification reason: 该论文研究开放世界机器人规划，核心是机器人通过假设生成与验证来自动扩展其抽象世界模型，并集成到目标导向规划中。这直接对应 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制（embodied_world_model），涉及机器人规划、知识扩展与不确定性建模。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with world_model direction and video generation world model subtopic, but lacks direct connection to continuous multimodal or autoregressive generation priorities. Signals: world model, open-world planning, hypothesis-driven expansion, foundation model integration, robot autonomy

Abstract: We consider an open-world planning setting in which service robots must operate in unknown environments with incomplete knowledge of objects and actions. Traditional closed-world approaches with pre-programmed knowledge bases fail when robots encounter unexpected situations and tasks, posing a fundamental challenge for autonomous knowledge expansion in human environments. In this work, we propose an open-world planning framework that enables robots to automatically generate, verify, and update hypotheses about their abstract world models. Our key insight is to explicitly maintain uncertainty-aware knowledge expansion and integrate hypothesis verification into goal-reaching planning. The framework leverages foundation models to generate initial hypotheses over states and transitions, and applies automated planning to produce action sequences that jointly address hypothesis verification and task execution. Through iterative execution and refinement, the robot expands its knowledge by incorporating verification feedback from the foundation models when hypotheses prove incorrect. Extensive experiments in simulated and real-world environments demonstrate that our framework enables autonomous knowledge expansion and effective operation in open-world settings. These results indicate that integrating uncertainty-aware model expansion from robot foundation models with planning advances the practical deployment of household service robots.

</details>


### [12] [RynnWorld-4D: 4D Embodied World Models for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06559)
*Haoyu Zhao, Xingyue Zhao, Siteng Huang, Xin Li, Deli Zhao, Zhongyu Li*

Main category: cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

TL;DR: RynnWorld-4D 是一个面向机器人操作的 4D 具身世界模型，通过联合生成 RGB、深度和光流（RGB-DF）来捕捉场景的 4D 动态，并利用逆动力学头直接从内部表征输出动作，在真实世界灵巧双臂操作任务上取得 SOTA 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放世界中的机器人操作不仅需要识别场景外观，还需要预测其在交互下的 3D 结构变化。现有 2D 像素视频世界模型与机器人底层动作之间存在较大鸿沟，缺乏对几何结构和时序运动的显式建模。

Method: 提出 RynnWorld-4D，一个三分支扩散架构，联合生成未来 RGB、深度图和光流。通过跨模态注意力与帧级 3D RoPE 确保外观、几何和运动的一致性。进一步提出 RynnWorld-4D-Policy，一个逆动力学头，单次前向传播即可从内部 4D 表征输出机器人动作，避免多步去噪。同时构建了包含 2.54 亿帧的大规模数据集 Rynn4DDataset 1.0。

Result: RynnWorld-4D 能生成时空一致的 4D 预测；RynnWorld-4D-Policy 在真实世界灵巧双臂操作任务上达到 SOTA，尤其在需要空间精度和时序协调的任务中表现优异。

Conclusion: RGB-DF 多模态表征能有效弥合世界预测与策略学习之间的鸿沟，联合生成 4D 动态的世界模型为机器人操作提供了一种有前景的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 RynnWorld-4D，一个面向机器人操作的 4D 具身世界模型，联合预测 RGB、深度和光流，并基于其内部表征直接输出机器人动作。这完全匹配 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 子主题。

Importance reason: Strong world model contribution with 4D embodied prediction and policy integration, but not directly on priority subtopics like continuous language models or autoregressive generation. Signals: world model, robotic manipulation, 4D scene dynamics, RGB-depth-flow generation, inverse dynamics policy

Abstract: Robotic manipulation in the open world requires not only recognizing what a scene looks like, but also anticipating how its 3D structure moves under interaction. We argue that synchronized RGB, depth, and optical flow, namely RGB-DF, provide a physically grounded representation that captures the underlying 4D dynamics of a scene. Compared to 2D pixel videos, this multi-modal synergy aligns visual appearance with geometric structure and temporal motion, creating a representation space significantly closer to the low-level end-effector actions demanded by robotic systems, thereby narrowing the gap between world prediction and policy learning. Building on this insight, we introduce RynnWorld-4D, a generative model that co-produces future RGB frames, depth maps, and optical flow from a single RGB-D image and a language instruction within one unified diffusion process. This 4D world model features a tri-branch architecture that integrates cross-modal attention with frame-wise 3D RoPE, ensuring that appearance, geometry, and motion evolve consistently. To supply training data at scale, we curate Rynn4DDataset 1.0, a massive dataset of over 254.4 million frames across egocentric human and robotic manipulation videos with high-quality pseudo-labels for depth and optical flow. We further propose RynnWorld-4D-Policy, an inverse dynamics head that consumes the internal 4D representations of RynnWorld-4D in a single forward pass, bypassing expensive multi-step denoising, to output robot actions in a closed-loop manner. Experiments show that RynnWorld-4D produces temporally and spatially coherent 4D predictions, and that RynnWorld-4D-Policy achieves state-of-the-art performance on real-world dexterous bimanual manipulation tasks, particularly excelling in tasks demanding spatial precision and temporal coordination.

</details>


### [13] [Lift3D-VLA: Lifting VLA Models to 3D Geometry and Dynamics-Aware Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06564)
*Jiaming Liu, Qingpo Wuwu, Nuowei Han, Hao Chen, Zhuoyang Liu, Fan Fei, Yueru Jia, Chenyang Gu, Yandong Guo, Boxin Shi, Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 高优先级 (93.65) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Lift3D-VLA 是一个统一的 VLA 框架，通过显式 3D 点云推理和时序动作建模，提升了机器人操作任务中的几何理解与物理动态感知能力，在 22 个模拟任务和 8 个真实任务上取得了 SOTA 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型在机器人操作中缺乏有效的 3D 几何理解与空间推理能力，且难以在动态环境中联合建模 3D 几何与时序动作。现有方法受限于数据不足和 3D 编码中的几何信息损失。

Method: 1) 基于 Lift3D 的增强 2D 模型提升策略，将 3D 点与预训练 2D 位置嵌入几何对齐，实现直接点云编码；2) 提出几何中心掩码自编码（GC-MAE），通过双目标自监督学习同时重建当前点云并预测未来几何演化；3) 设计分层时序动作建模，利用 LLM 的多层协同预测动作块，实现时序一致的动作生成。

Result: 在 MetaWorld 和 RLBench 上分别比最优 VLA 方法高出 10.8% 和 11.1% 的平均成功率；在 8 个真实世界操作任务上比最强基线高出 4 个百分点；在分布外扰动下表现出更强的泛化能力。

Conclusion: Lift3D-VLA 通过显式 3D 点云推理、几何动态自监督学习和分层时序动作建模，有效提升了 VLA 模型在机器人操作中的 3D 几何理解与动态感知能力，验证了 3D 信息对具身智能的重要性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Lift3D-VLA，一个面向机器人操作的 VLA 框架，结合 3D 点云推理与动作生成，属于具身智能中的世界模型与规划控制方向，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with world model and continuous multimodal reasoning priorities via 3D point cloud and dynamics-aware VLA. Signals: world model, 3D point cloud reasoning, VLA framework, geometry-dynamics, robotic manipulation

Abstract: Recently, Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated strong generalization across diverse tasks. However, effective robotic manipulation in physical environments fundamentally requires geometric understanding and spatial reasoning. While some VLA approaches attempt to incorporate 3D information, they are constrained by limited data availability and geometric information loss in current 3D encoding pipelines, and fail to jointly capture 3D geometry and temporally structured actions in dynamic environments. To address these limitations, we introduce Lift3D-VLA, a unified VLA framework that equips models with explicit 3D point cloud reasoning and enables temporally coherent action generation. First, building upon our previous work Lift3D, an enhanced 2D model-lifting strategy is proposed to geometrically align 3D points with pretrained 2D positional embeddings. This design enables direct point-cloud encoding within the VLA vision encoder while minimizing spatial information loss. Based on explicit 3D inputs, we propose Geometry-Centric Masked Autoencoding (GC-MAE), a dual-objective self-supervised framework that reconstructs the current point cloud while predicting its future geometric evolution. This formulation allows the 2D vision encoder to internalize both 3D structure and physical dynamics. To fully exploit 3D representations, we further design layer-wise temporal action modeling, which leverages multiple layers of the LLM to collaboratively predict action chunks, enabling temporally consistent predictions. Across 22 simulated tasks and 8 real-world manipulation tasks, Lift3D-VLA achieves 10.8% and 11.1% higher mean success rates on MetaWorld and RLBench than the best-performing prior VLA methods, and outperforms the strongest real-world baseline by 4 percentage points, while exhibiting stronger generalization to out-of-distribution perturbations.

</details>


### [14] [Prior-First, Condition-Second: Scalable and Controllable Hand Motion Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05938)
*Mingyi Shi, Xuelin Chen, Taku Komura*

Main category: cs.GR, cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"先验优先、条件其次"的框架，用于全身运动条件下的手部运动补全。先在大规模无标注运动数据上学习通用的身体-手部运动学先验，再通过轻量适配层注入语义控制信号，实现实时、可扩展且可控的手部运动生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手部运动自由度极高且缺乏语义标注，现有端到端条件生成方法在低资源场景下鲁棒性差、泛化能力弱，难以满足生产级动画管线对实时性和可控性的需求。

Method: 提出"先验优先、条件其次"两阶段框架：1) 在大规模无标注运动数据上预训练流式自回归身体-手部运动学先验模型，捕捉身体动力学与手部关节之间的内在协调关系；2) 在冻结的先验模型上引入语义分层适配器（semantically-layered adapters），在不同运动学层级注入条件信号，支持自监督属性控制和弱监督文本驱动控制，仅需少量标注数据即可适配。

Result: 在多个数据集和低资源设置下，该方法相比端到端条件基线在运动学合理性、鲁棒性和可控性上均有显著提升，支持实时推理和交互式创作工作流。

Conclusion: 将运动学先验学习与条件控制解耦是一种有效策略，能够在保持生成质量的同时提升可控性和数据效率，适用于生产级动画管线。

Classification reason: 该论文研究全身运动条件下的手部运动补全，涉及身体-手部运动学先验建模、实时自回归生成和语义控制，属于具身智能中的动作生成与规划控制范畴，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]方向高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on hand motion completion for animation, not directly aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: autoregressive, world model, controllable generation, efficient inference, real-time inference

Abstract: Synthesizing hand motion that matches the full body motion and the semantic labels is a difficult task due to their high degrees of freedom and the lack of semantic labels. To cope with this issue, we propose a prior-first, condition-second framework for body-conditioned hand motion completion. Our framework first learns a generic body-hand kinematic prior from large-scale unstructured and unlabeled motion data, capturing the intrinsic coordination between global body dynamics and hand articulation. Semantic control is then introduced through lightweight adaptation on top of the frozen prior, avoiding the need to relearn kinematic structure for each control interface. Our framework centers on a streaming, autoregressive body-hand prior that generates coherent, kinematically consistent hand motion from body dynamics in real time, using structured kinematic modeling to maintain mechanical body-hand coupling. To enable practical controllability under limited supervision, we introduce semantically-layered adapters that inject conditioning signals at appropriate kinematic levels, supporting both self-supervised attribute control and weakly supervised text-driven control with only a few hours of labeled data. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that our framework improves kinematic plausibility, robustness, and controllability compared to end-to-end conditioned baselines, particularly in low-resource and cross-dataset settings. We further showcase real-time inference and an interactive authoring workflow, highlighting the applicability to production animation pipelines. Homepage:

### [15] [OrchardBench: A Physically-Grounded, GPU-Parallel Apple-Orchard Simulation Benchmark for Agricultural Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06337)
*Humphrey Munn*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 可交互与物理世界建模

Importance: 普通相关 (59.89) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: OrchardBench 是一个基于物理引擎 Newton 的 GPU 并行苹果果园仿真基准。它使用随机 L-system 生成树木，将树枝建模为符合 Euler-Bernoulli 梁理论的扭转弹簧阻尼器，苹果通过茎绳连接并在特定拉力下脱落，并包含密度可控的 foliage 层。该仿真支持多环境并行、域随机化，并集成了移动机械臂与深度相机进行闭环采摘。基线方法仅能收获约 1/8 的可达果实，为新型自主方法留下了空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人果树采摘的田间实验成本高、不可复现（果园每年仅数周可用、每棵树不同、控制错误会损坏作物），现有图形学树木模型缺乏物理属性，而机器人学习的 GPU 并行仿真器中缺乏合理的树木模型。因此需要一种物理真实、可并行、可复现的果园仿真环境。

Method: 基于 Newton 物理引擎构建 GPU 并行仿真。每棵树由随机 L-system 生长，实例化为全刚体系统：树枝为符合 Euler-Bernoulli 梁理论的扭转弹簧阻尼器，在木材断裂模量下断裂并自由铰链下落；苹果通过茎绳连接，在文献支持的拉力下脱离； foliage 层密度可控地遮挡树冠。所有物理参数均有文献来源。支持每环境域随机化，集成移动机械臂与腕部深度相机进行几何水果感知与自主采摘基线。

Result: 在笔记本 GPU 上以交互速率运行多个并行环境。基线方法在检测到的果实上成功约 40%，仅收获约 1/8 的可达果实。提供了涵盖收获完整性、吞吐量和植物损伤的指标套件，并按树冠区域细分。

Conclusion: OrchardBench 提供了首个物理真实、GPU 并行、可复现的果园仿真基准，填补了现有仿真器在农业机器人领域的空白。基线结果展示了显著改进空间，为新型自主采摘方法提供了测试平台。

Classification reason: 该论文构建了一个基于物理的果园仿真模拟器（OrchardBench），用于农业机器人采摘任务。它属于世界模型方向中的可交互与物理世界建模（interactive_physical_world），因为其核心是物理一致的交互式模拟器，包含刚体动力学、柔性树枝模型、果实采摘力学等物理建模，并支持机器人闭环控制与规划。

Importance reason: Strong physical world model for agricultural robotics, but niche domain outside user's priority subtopics. Signals: GPU-parallel physics simulation, physically-grounded tree model, agricultural robotics benchmark, world model for manipulation, low personal relevance due to domain mismatch

Abstract: Robotic tree-fruit harvesting is a flagship problem for agricultural automation, but progress is bottlenecked by the cost and irreproducibility of field experiments: an orchard is available only weeks a year, every tree is different, and a control error can permanently damage the crop or the plant. The tree models used in graphics and agronomy are geometrically detailed but physically inert, while the GPU-parallel simulators used in robot learning contain no plausible trees. We present OrchardBench, a physically-grounded, GPU-parallel simulation of apple-orchard trees on the Newton engine. Each tree is grown by a stochastic L-system and instantiated as a fully articulated body: branches are compliant torsional spring-dampers whose stiffness follows Euler-Bernoulli beam theory, they break at a wood modulus of rupture and fall as free hinges, and apples are independent bodies on stem tethers that detach at literature-grounded pull forces and load the branch when pulled. A moving, density-controllable foliage layer occludes the canopy as real leaves do. Every physical parameter is tied to a published source. Per-environment domain randomization makes each batched world a distinct tree, and a mobile manipulator with a wrist depth camera closes the loop with geometric fruit perception and an autonomous harvesting baseline. Careful engineering of the solver and the model lets OrchardBench run many parallel environments at interactive rates on a laptop GPU. We define the tasks and a metric suite spanning harvest completeness, throughput, and plant damage (with a per-canopy-zone breakdown), and report baseline results across foliage, fruit load, terrain, canopy zone, and parallelism. The analytic baseline succeeds on about 40% of the fruit it detects and harvests only about an eighth of the reachable fruit on a tree, leaving clear headroom for novel autonomy approaches.

</details>


### [16] [Image2Sim: Scaling Embodied Navigation via Generative Neural Simulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05765)
*Zihan Wang, Seungjun Lee, Yinghao Xu, Gim Hee Lee*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 可交互与物理世界建模

TL;DR: Image2Sim 是一个实时神经仿真框架，能从带位姿的 RGB-D 图像序列构建高质量可交互 3D 场景，并作为自动化数据引擎生成海量导航训练样本，在多个导航基准上取得显著提升并有效迁移到真实世界零样本场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身导航研究受限于缺乏可扩展、高保真且物理真实的可交互环境。真实扫描数据集视觉真实但规模有限，合成模拟器易扩展但存在巨大的 sim-to-real 差距。需要一种既能大规模扩展又能保持物理真实性的仿真方案。

Method: Image2Sim 将 3D 空间锚定与逼真观测合成解耦。场景构建使用前馈特征高斯模型（feed-forward feature Gaussian model），单次前向将带位姿的 RGB-D 观测提升为 3D 特征-高斯表示。渲染阶段提出几何感知单步像素流模型（Geometry-Aware One-Step Pixel Flow），将稀疏有噪声的高斯投影转换为高质量全景 RGB-D 观测。该框架还作为自动化数据引擎，将大量视频和图像转换为近 20K 可交互场景，合成超过 1000 万导航训练样本。

Result: 在多个主要导航基准上，完全在神经环境中训练的导航模型取得了显著改进，并能有效零样本迁移到真实世界场景。Image2Sim 成功将大规模视频/图像数据转化为可交互训练环境，生成了近 20K 场景和超过 1000 万训练样本。

Conclusion: 可扩展的神经仿真可以作为具身导航大规模训练的有效基础，Image2Sim 证明了从真实世界图像数据构建高质量可交互仿真环境的可行性，为缩小 sim-to-real 差距提供了新路径。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Image2Sim，一个从 RGB-D 图像序列构建可交互神经仿真环境的框架，用于具身导航训练。这属于可交互与物理世界建模（interactive_physical_world）这一 [CANONICAL] 子主题，因为其核心是构建可交互的神经模拟器作为具身智能的训练环境。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with world model and neural simulator priorities, offering a scalable generative simulation pipeline for embodied navigation. Signals: world model, neural simulator, generative simulation, embodied navigation, scalable data engine

Abstract: Embodied navigation aims to build agents that interpret multimodal goals, reason in 3D space, and reach target destinations reliably in the real world. However, progress remains constrained by the lack of scalable, high-fidelity, and physically grounded interactive environments. Although real-world scanned datasets offer visual realism, they are limited by scale. In contrast, synthetic simulators scale more easily but often exhibit large sim-to-real gaps. We introduce Image2Sim, a real-time neural simulation framework that constructs high-quality interactive environments from posed RGB-D image sequences. The central idea is to decouple 3D spatial anchoring from photorealistic observation synthesis. For scene construction, Image2Sim uses a feed-forward feature Gaussian model that lifts posed RGB-D observations into a 3D feature-Gaussian representation in a single pass. For rendering, we propose a Geometry-Aware One-Step Pixel Flow model that transforms sparse and noisy Gaussian projections into high-quality panoramic RGB-D observations. Image2Sim also serves as a fully automated embodied data engine that generates high-fidelity observations, executable actions, and diverse navigation instructions at scale. It converts large collections of videos and images into nearly 20K interactive scenes and synthesizes more than 10 million navigation training samples. Navigation models trained entirely in these neural environments achieve strong improvements on major benchmarks and transfer effectively to real-world zero-shot settings. These results suggest that scalable neural simulation can serve as a practical training substrate for embodied navigation at scale.

</details>


### [17] [MoWorld: A Flash World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06216)
*Team Moxin, Deyi Ji, Tianrun Chen, Xin Zhang, Jiale Yang, Qi Zhu, An Zhao, Zihao Xie, Han Wang, Xuanyi Liu, Yixiang Zhou, Pei Liu, Yi Tan, Cheng Chen, Dayi Zhu, Mingyu Wei, Hanjie Xu, Jun Liao, Siqi Li, Lingyu Lu, Hongye Fang, Hongming Tan, Youjiang Zhu, Taiyu Zhang, Zejian Li, Chaotao Ding, Lanyun Zhu, Yunhe Pan, Lingyun Sun*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型在推理效率和部署成本上存在瓶颈，难以在真实自主系统中实现高帧率实时感知、规划与控制。作者旨在构建一个端到端的高效世界模型，兼顾模型能力与部署成本，实现大规模实际应用。

Method: MoWorld 采用端到端框架，包含：(1) 可扩展的 3D 原生数据引擎，利用大规模 3D 视觉与生成建模管线构建几何一致的训练数据；(2) 课程式跨帧预训练策略，实现稳定可扩展的世界模型学习；(3) 高效去噪步蒸馏算法，降低扩散训练成本；(4) 混合精度并行推理框架，支持低成本的实时部署。

Result: MoWorld 在多种评测中达到领先性能，平均推理成本仅为现有世界模型的 30%-50%，在 NPU 上实现高达 50 FPS 的实时交互，无需高端 GPU。

Conclusion: MoWorld 证明了通过联合优化模型能力与部署成本，可以在低端设备上实现高质量实时交互世界模型，为世界模型的大规模实际应用提供了实用基础。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 MoWorld，一个实时可交互的世界模型，支持 50 FPS 实时交互，属于世界模型方向。其核心贡献在于构建可交互的物理世界模拟器，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "可交互与物理世界建模"（interactive_physical_world）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances world model efficiency and real-time interaction on NPU, aligning with top user priorities. Signals: world model, efficient inference, real-time interaction, NPU deployment, 3D-native data engine

Abstract: The future of World Models depends not only on scaling model capability, but also on scaling practicality and inference efficiency. High-frame-rate inference enables responsive perception, planning, and control in real-world autonomous systems. To this end, we present MoWorld, a cost-effective yet high-performance Flash World Model with an end-to-end framework spanning data generation, pre-training, distillation, and efficient inference, enabling up to 50 FPS real-time interaction with cinematic visual quality without the need of high-end GPUs. To enable large-scale real-world deployment, MoWorld jointly optimizes model capability and cost throughout the entire development pipeline. Specifically, unlike existing approaches that primarily rely on large-scale video corpora, MoWorld is built upon a scalable 3D-native data engine accumulated from our large-scale 3D vision and generative modeling pipeline, enabling the efficient construction of geometrically consistent training data across diverse real-world and synthetic environments. Based on this foundation, a curriculum cross-frame pre-training strategy for stable and scalable World Model learning, an efficient denoising-step distillation algorithm to reduce diffusion training cost, and a mixed-precision parallel inference framework for low-cost real-time deployment. MoWorld is the first real-time interactive World Model built on the Neural Processing Unit (NPU) and can achieves up to 50 FPS in such the devices, enabling practical and efficient deployment at scale. Comprehensive evaluations demonstrate that MoWorld achieves leading performance; notably, its average inference cost is only 30\%-50\% of that of existing World Models, providing a practical foundation for large-scale real-world applications of World Models. We also demonstrate diverse applications of MoWorld.

</details>

### [18] [TRIG: Trajectory-Rig Decoupled Metric Geometry Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05801)
*Lizhou Liao, Wentao Xu, Handong Wang, Lirong Yang, Shuai Yang, Weiwei Liu, Chang Huang*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 空间 3D/4D 世界模型

TL;DR: TRIG 提出了一种面向视觉自动驾驶的轨迹-刚体解耦度量几何学习框架，将多相机位姿分解为自车轨迹和相机刚体组件分别建模，并引入稀疏时序-空间注意力机制，在五个自动驾驶基准上实现了位姿估计、度量深度预测和3D重建的最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉几何模型在自动驾驶场景中通常将相机位姿编码为纠缠表示，同时建模时变的自车运动与静态相机刚体几何，未能充分利用车辆侧的几何先验，限制了度量几何估计的精度。

Method: TRIG 将多相机系统的位姿分解为自车轨迹（ego-trajectory）和相机刚体（camera-rig）两个独立组件，分别进行解耦的位姿编码与监督学习。同时提出稀疏时序-空间注意力（Temporal-Spatial attention），将跨相机交互与时序聚合分离，降低全局注意力计算成本的同时保持几何推理能力。

Result: 在五个自动驾驶基准数据集上，TRIG 在位姿估计、度量深度预测和3D重建任务上均达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: 轨迹-刚体解耦的几何学习范式能够有效利用车辆侧几何先验，提升自动驾驶多相机系统的度量几何估计精度，且稀疏注意力机制在保持性能的同时降低了计算开销。

Classification reason: 该论文面向自动驾驶中的度量几何与位姿估计，将相机位姿分解为自车轨迹与相机刚体结构，属于空间3D场景中的世界建模，与 canonical subtopic "空间 3D/4D 世界模型" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong in world model direction but lacks direct connection to priority subtopics like continuous language models or autoregressive generation. Signals: world model, autonomous driving, metric geometry, multi-camera pose estimation, sparse attention

Abstract: Vision-centric autonomous driving requires accurate metric geometry and ego-motion estimation from synchronized multi-camera observations. Recent visual geometry models show strong performance in pose estimation, depth prediction, and 3D reconstruction, but are not tailored to rigid multi-camera driving systems. They often encode camera poses as entangled representations, in which time-varying ego-motion and static camera-rig geometry are jointly modeled, limiting the utilization of vehicle-side geometric priors. We propose Trajectory-Rig Decoupled Metric Geometry Learning (TRIG), a geometry perception framework for autonomous driving. TRIG factorizes camera poses into ego-trajectory and camera-rig components, enabling separate modeling of ego-motion and static multi-camera topology. We introduce decoupled pose encoding and supervision, which separately constrain trajectory evolution and rig geometry for metric-consistent learning. Moreover, sparse Temporal--Spatial attention separates cross-camera interaction from temporal aggregation, reducing global attention cost while preserving geometric reasoning. Experiments on five autonomous driving benchmarks show that TRIG achieves state-of-the-art performance in pose estimation, metric depth prediction, and 3D reconstruction.

</details>

### [19] [Imagined Rollouts are Kinematic, Not Dynamic: A Diagnosis of Long-Horizon World-Model Failure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05966)
*Finn Rasmus Schäfer, Korbinian Moller, Yuan Gao, Christian Oefinger, Sebastian Schmidt, Johannes Betz*

Main category: cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 视频世界模型

Importance: 高优先级 (91.37) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出世界模型的长时序失败并非简单的累积误差，而是模型倾向于生成运动学一致但动力学不真实的想象轨迹。作者定义了想象运动学一致性误差（iKCE）作为诊断指标，并在 DreamerV3 训练的 DMC walker-walk 任务上验证：模型的 iKCE 比真实物理 rollout 高两个数量级，且在摩擦系数变化导致步态崩溃时 iKCE 保持平坦，揭示了"运动学而非动力学"的失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献将世界模型的长时序失败笼统归因于"累积误差"，但未区分误差的类型。本文旨在揭示世界模型在长时序 rollout 中究竟犯了什么类型的错误，从而为改进世界模型提供更精确的诊断工具。

Method: 提出运动学-动力学重框架，将世界模型的想象 rollout 与封闭形式的运动学零假设（如恒定速度/加速度外推）进行比较。定义想象运动学一致性误差（iKCE）作为每步诊断指标，并设计扰动协议（如摩擦系数扫描）来测试模型在物理条件跨越动力学边界时 iKCE 是否响应。在 DreamerV3 训练的 DMC walker-walk 检查点上实例化该诊断。

Result: 在 DMC walker-walk 任务上，世界模型的 iKCE 比匹配的真实物理 rollout 高约两个数量级。在摩擦系数扫描实验中，当摩擦系数从 0.2 变化到 1.0 导致步态崩溃时，模型的 iKCE 保持统计平坦，而训练策略的奖励在同一范围内崩溃，提供了"运动学而非动力学"的明确证据。该诊断在超过步态周期的长时序上能有效区分运动学与动力学想象。

Conclusion: 世界模型的长时序失败本质上是动力学建模的失败而非简单的累积误差——模型学会了生成运动学上合理的轨迹，但未能捕捉底层物理动力学。iKCE 诊断为评估和改进世界模型的动力学建模能力提供了有效工具。

Classification reason: 该论文研究世界模型在长时序 rollout 中的失败原因，提出运动学-动力学重框架诊断方法，属于世界模型中的视频世界模型（video_world_model）子主题，关注长时序预测与动作条件生成。

Importance reason: Directly diagnoses world model failure in long-horizon video generation, a core challenge for your world model and video generation priorities. Signals: World model failure diagnosis, Kinematic vs dynamic error, iKCE diagnostic metric, Long-horizon video generation, DreamerV3 analysis

Abstract: Long-horizon failure in world models is conventionally attributed to compounding error, a generic framing that does not distinguish what kind of error compounds. We propose a kinematic-vs-dynamic reframing: world models tend to imagine kinematically rather than dynamically. We operationalize this as the imagined Kinematic-Consistency Error, a per-step diagnostic that measures how far a rollout departs from a closed-form kinematic null, paired with a perturbation protocol that tests whether iKCE responds when physical conditions cross a regime boundary. We instantiate the diagnostic on a released DreamerV3 checkpoint trained on DMC walker-walk, where imagined iKCE runs roughly two orders of magnitude above that of matched real-physics rollouts. Across a friction sweep that crosses the gait-collapse boundary, the model's iKCE stays statistically flat even as the trained policy's reward collapses through the same range, providing the kinematic-not-dynamic signature. The diagnostic distinguishes kinematic from dynamic imagination at horizons longer than the embodiment's gait period.

</details>

*Haoyu Zhao, Xingyue Zhao, Hangyu Li, Biao Gong, Kehan Li, Siteng Huang, Xin Li, Deli Zhao, Zhongyu Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RynnWorld-Teleop 提出数字遥操作范式，用动作条件视频世界模型替代真实机器人进行数据采集。系统通过手部姿态流驱动视频 Diffusion Transformer，从单张参考图像合成高保真第一人称视频，实现 40+ FPS 实时交互生成，生成的轨迹数据可直接用于模仿学习，并实现零样本 Sim2Real 迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习需要海量、多样化的轨迹数据，但当前数据采集受限于物理遥操作——每个演示都绑定操作员时间和特定硬件。为突破这一瓶颈，作者提出数字遥操作范式，用生成式世界模型替代真实机器人，实现与物理约束解耦的数据采集。

Method: 系统集成深度感知骨骼条件控制、渐进式人形到机器人训练（在视频 Diffusion Transformer 上）、以及流式自回归蒸馏。将生成过程压缩为单次推理，在单张 H100 GPU 上实现 40+ FPS 的实时交互生成。手部姿态流作为与具体形态无关的动作标签，可通过标准重定向迁移到任意目标机器人。

Result: 仅使用 RynnWorld-Teleop 生成数据训练的策略在灵巧和多样双臂任务上实现了有效的零样本 Sim2Real 迁移。用数字遥操作数据增强真实数据集能持续提升任务成功率。

Conclusion: RynnWorld-Teleop 作为一种高保真、可扩展的数据引擎，为下一代机器人智能体提供了有效的训练数据生成方案，证明了动作条件视频世界模型在机器人数据采集中的巨大潜力。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于动作条件视频生成的世界模型用于数字遥操作，属于视频世界模型（video_world_model）这一[CANONICAL]子主题，核心是动作条件视频生成与未来预测。

Importance reason: Directly advances world model and video generation for robotics, aligning with high-priority world_model and video generation subtopics. Signals: world model for teleoperation, action-conditioned video diffusion, real-time 40+ FPS generation, zero-shot Sim2Real transfer, scalable data engine for robotics

Abstract: Scaling robot learning requires massive, diverse trajectory data, yet collection is currently bottlenecked by physical teleoperation, where every demonstration binds operator time to specific hardware and workspaces. We introduce digital teleoperation, a paradigm that decouples data collection from physical constraints by replacing the real robot with a generative world model. In this framework, an operator's hand-pose stream drives a robot-centric generative world model to synthesize high-fidelity egocentric videos from a single reference image. The recorded pose stream serves as an embodiment-agnostic action label transferable to any target robot via standard retargeting, yielding complete state-action trajectories for imitation learning independent of physical hardware. We instantiate this paradigm in RynnWorld-Teleop, a system that integrates depth-aware skeletal conditioning, progressive human-to-robot training on a video Diffusion Transformer, and streaming autoregressive distillation. This pipeline compresses the generative process into a single-pass inference, enabling 40+ FPS, real-time interactive generation on a single H100 GPU. Policies trained exclusively on RynnWorld-Teleop-generated data achieve effective zero-shot Sim2Real transfer across dexterous and diverse bimanual tasks. Moreover, augmenting real-world datasets with our digitally teleoperated data consistently improves success rates, demonstrating that RynnWorld-Teleop serves as a high-fidelity, scalable data engine for the next generation of robotic agents.

</details>

Conclusion: 世界模型是AI领域一个核心但定义模糊的概念，本文通过提供统一的定义和路线图，为未来世界模型的研究提供了指导框架，有助于推动该领域的系统化发展。

Classification reason: 该论文对世界模型进行了科学定义、技术讨论和发展路线图规划，属于世界模型方向的综述/视角类文章。虽然不聚焦于视频生成，但其核心讨论世界模型的定义、预测目标与构建方法，与[视频世界模型]这一规范子主题紧密相关（世界模型的核心议题之一），且该子主题是CANONICAL条目，优先复用。

Importance reason: Provides a foundational definition and roadmap for world models, directly aligning with your high-priority world model and video generation interests. Signals: world model definition, roadmap, video world model, neural simulator, high priority subtopic

Abstract: World models -- internal simulators that learn the structure and dynamics of an environment -- have become one of the most actively debated concepts in AI. From model-based reinforcement learning and video generation to embodied robotics and ultimately, physical AI, researchers across AI subfields are building systems that they call "world models", yet there is no consensus on what a world model fundamentally is, what it should predict, or how it should be built. This perspective article provides a scientific definition of world models, discussions of their key technical aspects, and a staged roadmap for developing effective world models.

</details>

Importance reason: Directly addresses top-priority world model and video generation with a full-stack interactive framework, strongly aligned with user's focus on autoregressive video generation and world models. Signals: world model, interactive video generation, autoregressive, open-source framework, real-time interaction

Abstract: Game worlds have traditionally been built through labor-intensive production pipelines, making them costly to develop, difficult to customization, and expensive to modify after deployment. Recent advances in video world models offer a fundamentally different paradigm. Rather than explicitly authoring every component of a virtual environment, these models autoregressively synthesize future observations conditioned on the current world state and user interactions, enabling playable worlds to be generated online. Trained on both gameplay recordings and real-world videos, they can capture diverse visual appearances and physical dynamics, opening new opportunities for interactive applications beyond gaming, including embodied intelligence. In this paper, we present \textbf{AlayaWorld}, a full-stack open-source framework for building interactive generative worlds. AlayaWorld enables open-ended real-time interaction, allowing users to freely navigate and perform diverse actions such as combat, spell casting, and monster summoning. The framework unifies the complete development-from data preparation model architecture, model training, inference acceleration, and deployment-within a modular and extensible architecture. Alongside the framework, we release reproducible pipelines, reference implementations, evaluation tools, and comprehensive documentation, establishing a practical foundation for future research and real-time applications of generative world models.

</details>

*J de Curtò, Victoria Guillén, I. de Zarzà*

TL;DR: 本文提出LLMForge框架，利用LLM/VLM从自然语言描述自动生成参数化3D CAD模型。包含IterTracer（基于光线追踪渲染的几何评分）和IterVision（基于VLM语义批评）两种迭代优化机制，在97个工程设计问题的基准上评估了7个基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CAD建模需要专业知识和大量手动操作，自动化程度低。利用大语言模型和视觉语言模型的能力，实现从自然语言描述直接生成参数化3D机械设计，可大幅降低设计门槛、加速工业设计流程。

Method: 提出LLMForge框架，集成JSON-schema验证、分析特征评分、网格合成和多轮迭代优化。IterTracer使用Phong着色光线追踪渲染器，通过轮廓IoU、孔可见性、边缘间隙、长宽比一致性等视觉指标提供轻量级几何反馈。IterVision用Qwen2.5-VL-72B作为VLM语义批评器，通过思维链视觉推理评估空间一致性和设计意图。在包含四类几何族（带孔板、多特征盒、法兰圆柱、L型支架）的97问题基准上评估7个基础模型。

Result: IterTracer下前四名模型紧密聚类（总体均值[0.885,0.890]），网格生成成功率98.97%，表明紧凑指令微调模型可匹敌更大系统。IterVision下领先模型实现100%水密网格生成，但在旋转对称几何体（如圆柱）上视觉与语义评分分歧最大。

Conclusion: 基础模型在自动CAD生成方面展现出巨大潜力，紧凑模型通过指令微调可达到与大型系统相当的性能。VLM语义批评可提升水密性但面临旋转对称几何体的挑战。该工作对工业工作流和可扩展自动化机械设计具有重要启示。

Importance reason: Focuses on CAD generation, not aligned with user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: CAD generation, LLM/VLM for 3D, iterative refinement, benchmark study, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) and Vision-Language Models (VLMs) enable the automatic generation of parametric 3D designs from natural-language specifications. This chapter presents an empirical study of foundation models for automatic Computer-Aided Design (CAD) generation of mechanical parts, using a unified evaluation pipeline and a curated benchmark of 97 engineering design problems. We introduce LLMForge, a multi-model text-to-CAD framework integrating JSON-schema validation, analytic feature scoring, mesh synthesis, and multi-round iterative refinement, studied under two critique regimes. IterTracer uses a Phong-shaded ray-trace renderer with analytic visual metrics (silhouette IoU, hole visibility, edge clearance, aspect-ratio conformance) for lightweight geometry-aware feedback across rounds. IterVision replaces the analytic scorer with a VLM semantic critic (Qwen2.5-VL-72B) that evaluates rendered views via chain-of-thought visual reasoning, assessing spatial coherence and design intent. On a benchmark spanning four canonical geometry families (plates with holes and bolt circles, multi-feature boxes, flanged cylinders, and L-brackets), we evaluate seven foundation models: DeepSeek-V3.2, Qwen3-235B-A22B, Llama-3.3-70B, Gemma-3-27B, GLM-4.5, MiniMax-M2.1, and INTELLECT. Under IterTracer, the four highest-ranked models form a tight cluster (overall mean in [0.885, 0.890]) with 98.97% mesh success, showing that compact instruction-tuned models can match substantially larger systems. VLM-based critique in IterVision yields 100% watertight mesh generation on the leading model while surfacing systematic difficulty on rotationally symmetric geometries such as cylinders, where visual and semantic scoring diverge most. We discuss benchmark design, failure modes, CAD-oriented prompting, and implications for industrial workflows and scalable automated mechanical design.

Abstract: 3D Gaussian splatting (3DGS) is a strong representation for real-time novel-view synthesis, but its standard training pipeline relies on point estimates and hand-tuned heuristics, providing no native uncertainty or principled complexity control. This is most limiting under sparse views or fixed acquisition budgets, where a model must identify weakly supported geometry and select informative views. We introduce a rendering-aware Bayesian 3DGS framework that tracks Gaussian geometry with a Normal-Inverse-Wishart posterior over means and covariances using renderer-derived surrogate summaries. An optional Dirichlet-process extension adds a probabilistic component-usage signal, and the training schedule makes the closed-form versus approximate inference boundary explicit. Re-rendering posterior geometry samples yields native predictive uncertainty for interval calibration and active view selection. In a fixed-budget 16-to-32 active-view task, native NIW acquisition improves PSNR by +0.453 dB and LPIPS by -0.0146 over a scoring-only 3-member standard-ensemble baseline, winning 29/39 scene-seed pairs and 10/13 scene means; it also improves over PPU-style (+0.355 dB) and NIW-proxy (+0.401 dB) acquisition. NIW native intervals reduce 95% coverage error by about 17x relative to a shared proxy (0.046 vs. 0.796) and are about 10x closer to nominal coverage than a 3-member deep ensemble (0.047 vs. 0.454) at roughly one-third the training cost. As a reconstruction compatibility check, paired NIW-vs-standard analysis over 39 scene-seed runs yields +0.030 dB PSNR with 1.6% additional training time. These results position Bayesian 3DGS as a practical probabilistic scene representation for decision-facing tasks such as active view selection.

Classification reason: 该论文从多视图图像中提取几何基元（superquadric）进行3D形状抽象，属于3D几何重建与表示范畴，与[3D 生成与重建]方向高度匹配。

Signals: training-free, primitive shape abstraction, generative image model, 3D reconstruction, category-agnostic

Abstract: Representing 3D shapes as compact sets of geometric primitives is fundamental to robotics, simulation, and scene understanding. Generative image models trained at scale have recently emerged as generalist visual learners that can identify and segment object parts directly in the image domain, across arbitrary categories and without task-specific training. Adapting such models to downstream tasks typically requires fine-tuning; we ask whether their pretrained capability can instead be harnessed directly, without any training, and answer affirmatively with a training-free harness. Our pipeline renders multi-view images of a 3D object, uses a vision-language model to analyze its semantic parts, prompts a generative image model to paint a color-coded part segmentation mask, reprojects it onto the geometry, and fits a superquadric primitive to each part via parameter optimization. The approach contains no learned parameters: it is category-agnostic and orientation-invariant, properties that previous learning-based models struggled with. Its accuracy ceiling rises with future generative-model improvements, which we confirm with a ground-truth segmentation study showing that part segmentation, not primitive fitting, is the current accuracy bottleneck. On HumanPrim and Toys4K, our method achieves the lowest Chamfer distance among all evaluated methods, using 5--9 primitives per object on average.

</details>

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting provides an explicit representation that jointly models geometry and appearance, serving as a scalable foundation for 3D representation learning. Existing pre-training methods for Gaussian representations, such as masked Gaussian reconstruction, primarily capture local structures but offer limited semantic supervision. In this paper, we propose GaussFusion, a multimodal pre-training framework for 3D Gaussian representations. GaussFusion integrates image and text supervision into masked Gaussian modeling through cross-modal semantic alignment, enabling the Gaussian encoder to learn both visual and language-level semantic information during pre-training. To better adapt masked modeling to the non-uniform distribution of Gaussian primitives, we further propose Gaussian Salience-guided Multi-scale Hole Masking (GSHM). GSHM constructs spatially continuous masked regions based on Gaussian salience. By applying hole masks at multiple scales, GSHM encourages the encoder to capture both fine-grained local patterns and broader structural dependencies. Extensive experiments on downstream tasks demonstrate that GaussFusion improves the transferability of Gaussian representations. Notably, GaussFusion outperforms Gaussian-MAE on ModelNet40 and ScanObjectNN (PB-T50-RS) by 0.61\% and 3.85\%, respectively.

Result: 在image-to-3D生成、text-to-3D生成和3D captioning三项任务上均达到SOTA，超越最强统一基线模型，同时FLOPs和推理延迟约为非弹性版本的50%。

Conclusion: ELSA3D通过弹性语义锚定实现了统一3D理解与生成的高效跨模态对齐，验证了稀疏、尺度感知的交互机制在3D多模态任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Unified 3D foundation models aspire to generate 3D assets and reason about them in language within a single backbone, but their text-3D interaction remains largely implicit. Existing methods concatenate text and 3D tokens into a flat sequence and rely on self-attention, collapsing coarse structural cues and fine geometric details into one undifferentiated representation. We introduce ELSA3D, a unified 3D model that addresses this with elastic semantic anchoring, structuring language and geometric reasoning jointly along matched abstraction scales. ELSA3D represents geometry with a scale-aware octree tokenizer and introduces Anchor Tokens, sparse cross-modal units that select semantic cues, route them to the most relevant 3D scale, retrieve scale-specific geometric evidence, and write the fused signal back into the unified representation, keeping interaction sparse yet precise. A lightweight per-block router makes both computation and reasoning elastic, choosing which text tokens instantiate anchors at which geometric scale so that cross-modal capacity concentrates where alignment is most needed. ELSA3D achieves state-of-the-art performance across image-to-3D generation, text-to-3D generation, and 3D captioning, outperforming the strongest unified baseline while roughly halving FLOPs and inference latency relative to the non-elastic version of the same model.

Conclusion: UltraDiffEdit 成功将潜在扩散模型扩展到超高分辨率图像编辑，无需微调即可处理 8K 图像，弥合了现有方法与实际需求之间的差距。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 UltraDiffEdit，一种无需微调的超高分辨率图像编辑框架，属于图像编辑方向，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "visual_editing"（图像与视频编辑）。

Abstract: Recent diffusion-based generative models have shown impressive performance in image generation and editing. However, due to memory limitations and the high cost of collecting high-resolution training images, existing methods are typically restricted to inputs with linear resolutions below 1K. In contrast, photos captured by modern mobile devices often reach linear resolutions up to 8K, revealing a significant gap between current capabilities and real-world demands. Simply upscaling low-resolution edited results often results in visually enlarged but blurry images that lack fine details. This paper introduces UltraDiffEdit, a novel, tuning-free image editing framework that extends off-the-shelf latent diffusion models (LDMs) to ultrahigh resolutions. UltraDiffEdit employs a multi-scale progressive editing strategy, iteratively blending high-resolution edited content with unedited areas in a coarse-to-fine manner. We employ multi-patch encoding to preserve both edited and unedited visual details within the latent space. To mitigate editing artifacts, our global-local consistency denoising technique consistently integrates edited and unedited latent features, ensuring smooth transition at editing boundaries from the latent representation to the final image. We also introduce a patch-based hybrid sampling approach that captures local, intermediate, and global features, ensuring semantic coherence and enhancing fine detail during denoising. We conduct extensive experiments demonstrating UltraDiffEdit's superior editing quality and flexibility: it can handle image resolutions up to 8K using only a single NVIDIA GeForce RTX 3090 GPU. The source code is publicly available at .

</details>

Conclusion: 生成式干预结合跨变体自监督学习能有效学习背景不变表示，显著提升模型在分布偏移下的鲁棒性。

Importance reason: Addresses spurious correlations via generative intervention, but focuses on background invariance rather than core user priorities like continuous multimodal models or autoregressive generation. Signals: generative intervention, background-invariant representations, contrastive learning, distribution shift, not aligned with priority subtopics

Abstract: Deep neural networks trained with Empirical Risk Minimization (ERM) often fail under distribution shifts because they exploit spurious correlations between object labels and background context. Recent generative approaches address this issue by creating counterfactual images with altered contexts, but typically use these samples as standard data augmentation, leaving the model free to retain background-sensitive representations. We propose a two-stage framework that uses generative intervention to explicitly learn background-invariant visual representations. First, we isolate the foreground object using zero-shot segmentation and generate context-shifted variants with a structure-preserving diffusion model, preserving object identity while varying the surrounding environment. We then introduce Cross-Variant Self-Supervised Learning, where variants of the same object under different backgrounds form positive pairs in a contrastive objective. This encourages the encoder to align object-centric representations while suppressing background-specific cues. Then, we fine-tune the pretrained encoder using an ERM warm-up followed by GroupDRO with layer-wise learning rates. Experiments on distribution-shift benchmarks demonstrate best worst-group performance, achieving 92.5% on Waterbirds, 81.7% on MetaShift, and 87.4% on NICO++. Code:

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出一种全局蓝图引导的两阶段扩散框架，用于高分辨率艺术画作外推（outpainting）。第一阶段生成低分辨率全局蓝图并注入布局条件（边界框），第二阶段并行合成高分辨率局部块，利用蓝图初始化和低频保持特性消除顺序依赖，实现高效、布局可控且全局一致的高分辨率图像外推。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高分辨率图像外推方法存在三个关键问题：(1) 缺乏可靠的全局规划机制，导致高分辨率下结构不稳定和误差累积；(2) 空间可控性有限，难以在用户指定位置放置物体；(3) 顺序逐块生成导致推理延迟高。本文旨在解决这些问题。

Method: 提出两阶段扩散框架：Stage 1 使用布局适配器（layout adapter）将边界框条件注入 Stable Diffusion inpainting 骨干网络，生成低分辨率全局蓝图并提取全局引导特征；Stage 2 利用前向扩散的低频保持特性，从蓝图初始化各局部块，并行合成高分辨率局部块，注入蓝图全局引导特征，消除顺序依赖。

Result: 在大规模艺术画作数据集上的实验表明，该方法相比基线方法在视觉保真度、语义一致性上显著提升，同时大幅减少推理时间，并支持显式的布局控制。

Conclusion: 本文提出的全局蓝图引导两阶段扩散框架有效解决了高分辨率艺术画作外推中的结构不稳定、空间可控性差和推理延迟高的问题，为高分辨率图像外推提供了新的高效范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究高分辨率艺术画作的图像外推（outpainting），属于图像编辑的范畴。Outpainting 是图像编辑（inpainting/outpainting）的一个子任务，与 canonical 子主题 "图像与视频编辑"（visual_editing）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on artwork outpainting with layout control, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks strong alignment with user's configured direction weights. Signals: diffusion-based outpainting, layout control, two-stage generation, high-resolution, artwork domain

Abstract: Image outpainting extends an image beyond its original borders, requiring seamless style integration and globally coherent scene completion. Building on the success of diffusion models, recent methods have achieved substantial improvements in visual quality. In practice, however, high-resolution outpainting is commonly performed via progressive expansion around a fixed source image, particularly in artwork scenarios. Despite this progress, existing approaches still suffer from three key limitations: (i) the absence of a reliable global planning mechanism, which leads to structural instability and error accumulation at high resolutions; (ii) limited spatial controllability beyond text prompts, making it difficult to place objects at user-specified locations; and (iii) high inference latency caused by inherently sequential patch generation. To address these issues, we propose a global blueprint-guided two-stage diffusion framework for layout-controllable high-resolution outpainting with efficient parallel synthesis. In Stage 1, we generate a low-resolution global blueprint using a layout adapter that injects bounding-box conditions into a Stable Diffusion inpainting backbone, producing a globally consistent structural plan while extracting global guidance features. In Stage 2, we synthesize high-resolution local patches in parallel by injecting the blueprint-derived global guidance and initializing each patch from the blueprint using the low-frequency preservation property of forward diffusion. This design eliminates sequential dependency while maintaining global coherence. Extensive experiments on large-scale artwork datasets demonstrate improved visual fidelity, stronger semantic consistency, and substantially reduced inference time compared to prior baselines, while uniquely supporting explicit layout control for artwork outpainting.

</details>

TL;DR: FADRA 提出了一种频率感知的扩散框架，结合迭代残差适配，用于视频人脸修复。该方法利用预训练文生视频扩散模型，通过轻量 LoRA 适配器和低质量像素对齐特征融合模块进行高效适配，并引入重复残差适配头（RRAH）在流匹配步骤中反复利用低质量线索进行残差细化，同时使用频率感知损失对多个频谱带进行显式监督，以恢复高质量且时序一致的人脸细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频人脸修复方法在复杂退化条件下难以平衡空间保真度和时序一致性，亟需一种既能恢复精细面部细节又能保持时序连贯性的方法。

Method: 1) 利用预训练文生视频扩散模型的强时序一致性，引入轻量 LoRA 适配器和低质量像素对齐特征融合模块（LQ Pixel-Alignment Feature Fusion）进行高效适配；2) 提出重复残差适配头（RRAH），在扩散骨干网络之后进行逐步残差细化，RRAH 将低质量潜变量与当前速度预测作为输入，在每个流匹配步骤中反复利用低质量线索预测残差更新；3) 引入频率感知损失（Frequency-Aware Loss），对多个频谱带进行显式监督，强调对感知质量重要且易产生时序抖动的频率成分。

Result: 大量实验表明，FADRA 在恢复面部结构和生成时序一致性视频方面优于现有方法，在定量指标和视觉感知上均有明显提升。

Conclusion: FADRA 通过频率感知扩散框架和迭代残差适配，有效解决了视频人脸修复中空间保真度与时序一致性的平衡问题，为视频修复任务提供了新的有效范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究视频人脸修复（Video Face Restoration），属于视频质量增强与恢复任务，与"图像与视频编辑"（visual_editing）这一[CANONICAL]子主题高度相关，因为视频修复是视频编辑的一个重要子方向。

Importance reason: Applies diffusion to video face restoration, a niche application not aligned with user's priority subtopics. Signals: video face restoration, frequency-aware loss, LoRA adaptation, residual refinement, diffusion-based video enhancement

Abstract: Video face restoration (VFR) aims to recover high-quality and temporally consistent facial details from severely degraded video sequences; however, existing methods still struggle to balance spatial fidelity and temporal coherence under complex degradations. To address this, we propose FADRA, a frequency-aware diffusion framework with iterative residual adaptation specifically tailored for robust VFR. We first leverage the strong temporal consistency of a pre-trained text-to-video diffusion model and introduce lightweight LoRA adapters together with a Low-Quality (LQ) Pixel-Alignment Feature Fusion module to efficiently adapt the frozen generative prior to the VFR task. To further adapt the frozen diffusion backbone to the downstream VFR task beyond LoRA-based adaptation, we introduce a Repeated Residual Adaptation Head (RRAH) for step-wise residual refinement after the diffusion backbone. To make this refinement explicitly guided by the degraded observation, RRAH further takes the LQ latent together with the current velocity prediction as input, allowing the model to repeatedly revisit LQ cues and predict residual updates at each flow-matching step. This LQ-guided repeated residual adaptation helps recover fine facial details while preserving the inherent temporal priors of the pre-trained model. Furthermore, to ensure the structural integrity of perceptually important details, we introduce a Frequency-Aware Loss that provides explicit supervision across multiple spectral bands, emphasizing visually sensitive frequency components that are crucial for perceptual quality and prone to temporal jittering. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FADRA recovers better facial structures and produces more temporally consistent videos than state-of-the-art methods, leading to clear gains in both quantitative metrics and visual perception.

</details>
