<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM在扩散图像编辑中作为条件编码器使用时，编辑管线在复杂多实体场景中难以保持VLM本身具备的强定位能力。作者假设这一性能差距源于VLM被限制为单次前向传播（而非其优化的自回归生成过程），导致其定位能力无法充分展现。

Method: 提出Analysis-by-Proxy框架：在VLM的中间表征上训练一个轻量、可解释的代理模型，执行辅助定位任务（如分割）。通过分析代理模型的表现，揭示VLM中编码定位信息的具体表征层，并发现定位信号在单次前向传播约束下不会可靠地传播到编辑管线常用的预设层配置中，而是隐藏在随输入提示变化的中间表征位置。

Result: 实验表明，VLM作为条件编码器时，定位信号存在于中间表征中，但其位置随输入提示动态变化，现有编辑管线使用的固定层配置无法可靠捕获该信号。Analysis-by-Proxy框架成功揭示了这一根本性失败原因。

Conclusion: 现有条件提取策略存在根本性缺陷，定位信号在单次前向传播中无法可靠到达预设层。该发现为更合理设计条件编码架构（如动态层选择或多层融合）打开了新方向。

Classification reason: 论文研究VLM作为扩散图像编辑的条件编码器时的定位信号问题，核心应用场景是图像编辑，因此映射到多模态生成方向下的[CANONICAL] subtopic "图像与视频编辑"（visual_editing）。同时论文涉及VLM的内部表征分析，也与多模态理解与生成方向相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on VLM conditioning for image editing, which is not a top priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to continuous language models or video generation. Signals: VLM condition encoder, diffusion image editing, localization signal analysis, proxy model, autoregressive generation mismatch

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are increasingly utilized as the conditioning backbone for diffusion-based image editing due to their remarkable multimodal reasoning capabilities. While standalone VLMs demonstrate strong localization capabilities, editing pipelines frequently struggle to maintain this accuracy, particularly in complex, multi-entity scenes. In this work, we investigate this performance gap, hypothesizing that it stems from treating the VLM as a condition encoder. In this role, the model is restricted to a single forward pass, preventing the autoregressive generation process for which it was optimized, thereby failing to fully expose its capabilities. To investigate whether this spatial understanding persists when the VLM is used as a condition encoder, we introduce Analysis-by-Proxy. In this framework, we train a lightweight, interpretable proxy model on the VLM's intermediate representations using an auxiliary localization task. By analyzing the VLM through this proxy, we uncover the specific VLM representations that encode localization information. Our findings expose a fundamental mismatch between how spatial knowledge is represented within a VLM condition encoder and how it is extracted by current editing pipelines. We reveal that under single-pass constraints, the localization signal does not reliably propagate to the predefined layer configurations commonly used for conditioning. Instead, this crucial signal remains hidden within intermediate representations, at locations that vary depending on the input prompt. Using our introduced Analysis-by-Proxy framework, we reveal the fundamental failures of existing condition extraction strategies in editing pipelines, opening the door to more principled design of conditioning architectures.

</details>


### [33] [From RGB Generation to Dense Field Readout: Pixel-Space Dense Prediction with Text-to-Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06553)
*Zanyi Wang, Xin Lin, Haodong Li, Dengyang Jiang, Yijiang Li, Pengtao Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ReChannel方法，利用预训练文生图DiT模型（FLUX-Klein）进行密集预测。核心思想是：不将密集预测视为目标生成（即不把标注编码到VAE潜空间再解码为图像），而是直接利用DiT的patch-to-token-to-patch格点结构，在每个token上通过一个极轻量的共享线性头（约33K参数）直接预测对应patch的像素级任务场。在六项密集预测任务上达到SOTA或竞争力水平，速度比编辑+潜解码方案快2.48倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将密集预测（深度、法线、抠图等）视为目标生成任务，即把标注编码到RGB预训练的VAE潜空间再解码回图像格式。作者认为这种方式继承了生成模型的输出接口，但密集预测并不需要生成新的RGB内容，而是需要像素正确的任务原生场。因此希望设计一种更简洁、高效的接口来复用文生图模型的先验知识。

Method: ReChannel方法：保留VAE编码器以匹配DiT的输入分布，但丢弃目标侧的VAE解码器；使用冻结的DiT（FLUX-Klein）加任务LoRA进行适配；每个token通过一个共享的token局部线性头（约33K参数，无空间混合）映射到对应的p×p×K_t像素空间patch，直接输出任务原生场（如深度、法线等）。

Result: 在六项密集预测任务（抠图、深度、法线、显著性、姿态、指代分割）的十余个基准上评估。在无trimap抠图、KITTI深度和指代分割上达到新SOTA；在法线、显著性和姿态上具有竞争力。在匹配的4B参数设置下，比编辑+潜解码方案更准确且快2.48倍。

Conclusion: 密集预测可以从生成式预训练中受益，而无需继承其输出接口。通过极简的token-to-patch线性映射头，ReChannel在多项任务上取得了优异性能，验证了生成先验在密集预测中的有效性，并为利用文生图模型进行像素级理解任务提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文利用预训练文生图模型（FLUX-Klein/DiT）进行密集预测（深度、法线、抠图、分割等），通过LoRA微调+token-to-patch线性映射头实现像素级预测，属于将生成模型重用于密集预测任务，与图像编辑/可控生成（visual_editing）中利用生成先验进行下游任务适配的思路高度一致。同时也可归入多模态理解与生成方向下的统一多模态模型（unified_multimodal_model），因其将生成模型用于理解型任务。

Importance reason: Proposes a novel, efficient method for dense prediction using pretrained DiT, directly relevant to continuous multimodal and efficient inference priorities. Signals: ReChannel method, FLUX-Klein DiT, dense prediction from generative models, 33K parameter linear head, 2.48x speedup

Abstract: Large-scale text-to-image models are attractive backbones for dense prediction because RGB generation pretraining learns rich semantic, structural, and geometric priors. Existing generative and editing approaches reuse these priors by casting dense prediction as target generation: annotations such as depth, normals, alpha mattes, masks, and heatmaps are encoded into an RGB-trained VAE latent space and decoded back as image-like targets. We argue this inherits more of the generative output interface than dense prediction requires: unlike RGB synthesis, dense prediction asks for pixel-correct, task-native fields on the same image plane, not new RGB content to be rendered. Our key observation is that a pretrained DiT already organizes RGB inputs through a patch-to-token-to-patch lattice on the image plane, so each token indexes a fixed output patch whose channels can carry task-native quantities instead of RGB appearance. We instantiate this as ReChannel: we keep the VAE encoder for the DiT's input distribution but drop the target-side decoder, adapt the frozen DiT with task LoRA, and map each token to its p x p x K_t pixel-space patch through a shared token-local linear head--about 33K parameters, no spatial mixing. Using FLUX-Klein, we evaluate on six dense prediction tasks and over a dozen benchmarks. This minimal interface sets new state-of-the-art on trimap-free matting, KITTI depth, and referring segmentation, and stays competitive on normals, saliency, and pose. In a matched 4B setting it is more accurate and 2.48x faster than an edit-plus-latent-decode counterpart--dense perception can benefit from generative pretraining without inheriting its output interface.

</details>

*Kristof Overdulve, Lode Jorissen, Nick Michiels*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.GR

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

TL;DR: SSA-3DGS提出一种无监督框架，联合优化3D场景与可学习的2D覆盖层，从3D高斯泼溅重建中去除屏幕空间伪影（如传感器缺陷、污渍、水印等），无需任何监督或人工标注，在合成和真实数据上提升PSNR最高达9dB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS等新视角合成方法假设输入图像干净且多视图一致，但真实拍摄中常出现屏幕空间伪影（如镜头污渍、雨滴、水印等），这些伪影会被错误地烘焙到3D几何中形成"漂浮物"，严重降低新视角渲染质量。现有方法缺乏无监督去除这类伪影的方案。

Method: 提出SSA-3DGS框架，在标准3DGS优化基础上引入一个可学习的2D覆盖层（overlay），该覆盖层与3D场景联合优化。通过多视图几何一致性约束，网络自动区分哪些是固定在2D图像平面上的伪影（屏幕空间伪影），哪些是真实的3D场景内容，从而无监督地分离两者。

Result: 在多种合成伪影（污点、水印、UI元素等）和自采真实数据集上，SSA-3DGS相比直接在污染数据上训练的3DGS，PSNR提升最高达9dB，同时能忠实保留伪影本身作为覆盖层输出。

Conclusion: SSA-3DGS有效解决了3DGS中屏幕空间伪影的无监督去除问题，通过联合优化3D场景与2D覆盖层实现了伪影与场景的干净分离，为真实世界3D重建中的鲁棒性提供了实用方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出无监督去除3D高斯泼溅中的屏幕空间伪影（如污点、水印、UI元素等），属于3D场景重建后的图像/渲染质量修复与编辑任务，与[图像与视频编辑]（visual_editing）主题高度相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on artifact removal in 3DGS, which is not aligned with user's priority subtopics on continuous models, video/image generation, or world models. Signals: 3D Gaussian Splatting, unsupervised artifact removal, screen-space artifacts, novel view synthesis, no priority subtopic match

Abstract: Novel View Synthesis (NVS) methods, such as 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS), rely heavily on the assumption of clean, multi-view consistent, posed input images. Real-world captures can violate this assumption due to screen-space artifacts-static occlusions fixed to the 2D image plane rather than to the 3D world. Common examples include physical sensor defects, environmental obstructions (such as rain or mud on the lens enclosure), capture obstructions (such as a thumb over the camera sensor or a dashboard visible in dashcam footage), and digital overlays (such as watermarks or UI elements). When present, they are erroneously baked into the 3D geometry as "floaters" or near-camera artifacts, degrading the quality of novel-view rendering. In this work, we propose SSA-3DGS, an unsupervised framework that jointly optimizes a 3D scene and a learnable 2D overlay to recover a clean 3D scene and the corrupting artifacts. By exploiting geometric consensus across views, our method effectively disentangles static artifacts from the 3D scene geometry without supervision or manual input. Across diverse synthetic corruptions and a self-captured real-world dataset, SSA-3DGS improves reconstruction fidelity by up to 9 dB PSNR over 3DGS trained on the same corrupted inputs, while faithfully preserving the corrupting artifact.

</details>

*Jiakun Li, Zhe Li, Wenqiang Wu, Zheng Chang, Mingqi Gao, Jinyu Yang, Feng Zheng*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型在合成人体-场景交互时存在"语义-几何纠缠"问题——空间约束与特定动作在训练数据中强相关，导致模型学习到捷径偏差，在严格几何约束下会激进地覆盖语义意图，同时产生身体-场景穿透等物理幻觉。

Method: 提出DeSeG层次化框架：(1) 残差语义规划器（Residual Semantic Planner），将文本指令和规范化目标体素编码到紧凑潜空间，实现独立于空间轨迹的细粒度语义控制；(2) 物理正则化扩散执行器（Physics Regularized Diffusion Executor），将可微排斥势场直接融入扩散目标函数，强制执行碰撞感知的运动生成。

Result: 在Lingo数据集上，DeSeG相比SOTA方法平均场景穿透减少47%，语义对齐提升29%，达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: 显式解耦语义意图与几何约束能有效缓解语义-几何纠缠问题，物理正则化扩散目标函数可显著提升人体-场景交互的物理合理性，为生成物理可信的人体动作提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究人体-场景交互（HSI）的物理合理动作合成，属于人体动作生成范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "数字人与动作生成"（avatar_motion_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on human-scene interaction, not aligned with user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Human-scene interaction, motion generation, semantic-geometric decoupling, diffusion model, physical plausibility

Abstract: Synthesizing physically plausible human-scene interactions (HSI) remains a critical challenge in computer vision and the development of human avatars. Although recent generative models enable diverse motion synthesis, they suffer from an inductive bias referred to as semantic-geometric entanglement. Because spatial constraints often strongly correlate with specific actions in training data, monolithic models will learn the shortcut bias, aggressively overriding the semantic intent when faced with strict geometric cues. Furthermore, this entanglement exacerbates physical hallucinations, such as body-scene penetrations. To address these limitations, we propose DeSeG, a hierarchical framework that explicitly decouples semantic intent from geometric constraints. First, we introduce a Residual Semantic Planner that encodes textual instructions and canonicalized goal voxels into a compact latent space, enabling fine-grained semantic control independent of spatial trajectories. Second, we propose a physics regularized diffusion executor that incorporates differentiable repulsive potential fields directly into the diffusion objective, enforcing collision-aware motion generation. Extensive experiments on the Lingo dataset demonstrate that DeSeG achieves state-of-the-art performance, reducing mean scene penetration by 47% and improving semantic alignment by 29% over the SOTA baselines.

</details>

Abstract: Human-Object Interaction (HOI) video generation aims to synthesize realistic videos of humans manipulating diverse objects, serving as a promising avenue for AI-driven live streaming e-commerce. A primary obstacle in this domain lies in the complexity of modeling fine-grained physical dynamics and the intricate spatial-temporal coordination between human hands and objects. Existing approaches to this problem typically rely on dense temporal guidance, e.g., frame-wise hand-object pose sequences, to strictly control the interaction process. However, such dense guidance incurs high annotation costs and affects motion synthesis diversity. To overcome these limitations, we introduce SparseCtrl-HOI, a novel sparse temporal control framework for HOI video generation. It requires only a few keyframes that capture interaction states at designated timestamps. Specifically, we employ a Time-Controlled Rotary Positional Embedding (TiRoPE) mechanism to temporally anchor these keyframes while preserving their spatial integrity. Subsequently, to govern the dynamics across intermediate frames, we propose a Motion Prior Injection Module that leverages Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) to extract high-level motion priors. This empowers the model to hallucinate logically and physically plausible transitions. Furthermore, we build SparseHOI-5K, a high-quality and richly annotated dataset for HOI video generation with sparse temporal control. Comprehensive evaluations confirm that our method substantially reduces annotation overhead while synthesizing superior live-streaming e-commerce videos. Both our code and dataset are publicly available at .

</details>

Conclusion: 紧凑的提示路由和轻量级时序适配提供了足够的可控容量来实现稳定的长视频外推，验证了参数高效方法在多镜头长视频生成中的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于冻结文生视频扩散Transformer的低秩时序适配器框架，用于多镜头长视频外推，核心属于文生视频与视频扩散方向，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "文生视频与视频扩散" (text_to_video_diffusion)。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with user's priority on video generation and efficient inference via parameter-efficient adapters and prompt routing. Signals: video generation, parameter-efficient, long video extrapolation, diffusion transformer, temporal adapter

Abstract: We present PACR-Video, a parameter-efficient framework for multi-shot long video extrapolation that preserves recurring entities, scene structure, visual style, and causal progression without full generator fine-tuning. PACR-Video keeps a text-to-video diffusion transformer frozen and augments it with low-rank temporal adapters conditioned by learned shot-role prompt tokens. To maintain long-horizon coherence, it builds a recursive prompt bank that stores compact entity, location, action, and style prompts from previous shots, then routes them through adapter gates according to predicted narrative dependencies. A Shot-Local/Story-Global tuning objective combines next-shot reconstruction, cross-shot identity contrast, and prompt sparsity regularization, while an adapter composition schedule balances early-shot visual consistency with later-shot event progression and viewpoint change. Across six multi-shot and long-video benchmarks, PACR-Video outperforms text-to-video, tuning-based, memory-augmented, streaming, and recursive-context baselines on distributional quality, semantic alignment, identity consistency, temporal smoothness, motion stability, transition coherence, and human preference. These results show that compact prompt routing and lightweight temporal adaptation provide sufficient controllable capacity for stable long video extrapolation.

</details>

Conclusion: 本文提出的点云骨架条件生成框架有效解决了闭环驾驶仿真中视觉保真度和交互性的权衡问题，为高保真闭环自动驾驶仿真提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出自回归驾驶视频生成框架，属于视频生成范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 文生视频与视频扩散 子主题相关（尽管是驾驶场景视频生成，但核心是视频扩散/自回归生成方法）。同时涉及闭环仿真，与 world_model 方向下的视频世界模型也有交叉。

Abstract: Evaluating end-to-end autonomous driving (E2E-AD) remains challenging, as existing driving simulation methods often trade off closed-loop interactivity (e.g., CARLA) and real-world visual fidelity (e.g., nuScenes). We present \textbf{\emph{Point as Skeleton}}, a generative sensor simulation framework for state-updated autoregressive driving video generation, in which an autoregressive generator synthesizes visual observations from step-wise updated ego states, actor states, scene maps, and point-cloud skeleton conditions. To support closed-loop rollout, we introduce Reset-and-Roll, which adapts rolling diffusion inference to simulation by preventing future-conditioned latent states from being committed across simulation steps. To stabilize error accumulation during step-wise autoregressive rollout, we introduce point-cloud skeletons that decouple foreground and background assets and project them into camera-view painted-point and template-depth conditions, providing appearance and geometric cues. We further implement a nuPlan-based renderer-level closed-loop generative interface for evaluating generation under ego deviations from the original log. Experiments on nuScenes and nuPlan show that \textit{Point as Skeleton} improves autoregressive generation quality during closed-loop rollout, demonstrating its potential for visually faithful closed-loop driving simulation. The code is available at .

*Bowen Xue, Zihan Min, Xingyang Li, Zhekai Zhang, Haocheng Xi, Lvmin Zhang, Maneesh Agrawala, Jun-Yan Zhu, Song Han, Yujun Lin, Muyang Li*

TL;DR: FourTune提出了一种基于W4A4G4全4-bit量化的扩散模型高效后训练框架，通过三分支混合架构（含冻结数值稳定器）和硬件高效块级量化核，在FLUX.1-dev等大模型上实现2.25倍内存降低和2.27倍训练吞吐提升，同时保持全精度微调的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型后训练（微调、RL、蒸馏）面临巨大的内存开销和缓慢的训练速度，现有PEFT方法（如LoRA）只能部分缓解这些问题，无法从根本上解决大模型后训练的效率瓶颈。

Method: 提出FourTune框架，采用端到端W4A4G4（权重4-bit、激活4-bit、梯度4-bit）量化范式。核心设计包括：(1) 三分支混合架构——在标准LoRA分支基础上增加一个冻结的数值稳定器分支，隔离量化敏感的异常值，实现稳定训练；(2) 硬件高效的块级量化和定制融合核，支持高效的量化反向传播并减少内存带宽开销。

Result: 在定制化、强化学习和蒸馏任务上，FourTune匹配全精度微调的质量。在FLUX.1-dev（12B参数）上，相比BF16 LoRA，内存开销降低2.25倍，端到端训练吞吐提升2.27倍。

Conclusion: FourTune证明了全4-bit量化后训练在扩散模型上的可行性和有效性，为大规模扩散模型的高效下游适配提供了实用方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出面向扩散模型的4-bit量化后训练框架FourTune，核心贡献是生成模型的高效训练（量化、加速、内存优化），属于多模态生成方向下的[生成模型加速与高效化]（efficient_generation）规范子主题。

Importance reason: Directly addresses model efficiency for diffusion models, a high-priority subtopic, with strong practical gains. Signals: W4A4G4 quantization, 2.25x memory reduction, 2.27x training throughput, matches full-precision quality, hardware-efficient kernels

Abstract: Diffusion models have become a dominant paradigm for high-quality generative modeling, while post-training is essential for adapting them to diverse downstream applications. However, post-training of large diffusion models is still challenging due to the prohibitive memory footprints and slow training speed, which existing parameter-efficient fine-tuning methods only partially address. To overcome these limitations, we propose FourTune, an efficient post-training framework for diffusion models based on an end-to-end W4A4G4 paradigm. FourTune introduces a triple-branch hybrid pipeline that augments the standard LoRA architecture with a frozen numerical stabilizer to isolate quantization-sensitive outliers, enabling stable training under native 4-bit computation. In addition, FourTune employs hardware-efficient block-wise quantization and customized fused kernels to support efficient quantized backpropagation and reduce memory bandwidth overhead. Across customization, reinforcement learning, and distillation tasks, FourTune matches the quality of full-precision fine-tuning. On FLUX.1-dev (12B), FourTune reduces memory overhead by 2.25$\times$ and increases end-to-end training throughput by 2.27$\times$ compared to BF16 LoRA.

</details>


### [40] [x-Prediction Is All You Need:Training-Free Accelerated Generation via Endpoint Decodability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06114)
*Xin Peng, Ang Gao*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

Importance: 高优先级 (93.17) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出Truncated Jump Sampling (TJS)，利用扩散/流匹配模型中中间状态可解码出干净样本（端点可解码性）的特性，在ODE采样的早期阶段提前停止并返回解码结果，无需重新训练、蒸馏或架构修改即可实现20-70%的NFE减少，且生成质量几乎无损。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散和流匹配模型的ODE采样器通常需要数十到数百步神经函数评估（NFE），现有加速方法（蒸馏、重新设计轨迹等）需要额外的训练成本和架构修改，对于已发布的检查点不友好。作者希望探索一种无需训练、无需蒸馏、无需架构修改的推理加速方案。

Method: 作者形式化了"端点可解码性"（endpoint decodability）性质：在标准仿射概率路径下，中间状态x_t和路径速度v_t可以确定一个对干净样本x_0的最小MSE估计（即E[x_0|x_t]）。基于此提出Truncated Jump Sampling (TJS)：在ODE采样的早期退出时间t*处停止，直接返回解码出的x_0估计值，跳过后续所有采样步骤。该方法无需拉直轨迹，适用于任何基于仿射概率路径的扩散/流匹配模型。

Result: 在SDXL、SD3.5M、Z-Image-Turbo以及三个类别条件基准上，TJS将NFE减少20-70%，生成质量与完整采样几乎匹配。实验表明端点预测在不拉直轨迹的情况下也能有效工作，为无需轨迹重新设计的推理加速提供了新思路。

Conclusion: TJS是一种简单、通用且无需训练的扩散/流匹配模型加速方法，利用端点可解码性在早期退出时直接解码出干净样本，显著减少采样步数。该方法适用于多种现有模型，为实际部署中的推理加速提供了实用方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出Truncated Jump Sampling (TJS)，一种无需重新训练或蒸馏的扩散/流匹配模型加速采样方法，属于生成模型加速与高效化（efficient_generation）这一[CANONICAL]子主题。

Importance reason: Directly addresses model efficiency (weight 1.2) with a training-free acceleration method for diffusion/flow models, highly relevant to continuous language/multimodal models (weight 2.0) via flow matching. Signals: Training-free acceleration, 20-70% NFE reduction, Endpoint decodability theory, Applicable to flow matching (key for continuous models), No architecture change needed

Abstract: Diffusion and flow matching models generate high-quality samples, but their ODE samplers often need tens to hundreds of neural function evaluations (NFEs). This remains a practical challenge for released checkpoints, since many accelerators require additional design choices and training cost through retraining, distillation, or trajectory redesign. We investigate a different route based on $x$-prediction. During sampling, standard affine probability paths already expose $x_0$ information: an intermediate state and its path velocity determine a principled estimate of the clean sample. We formalize this property as \textbf{endpoint decodability} and show that the decoder is the minimum-MSE estimator $\mathbb{E}[x_0\mid x_t]$ under the usual $\ell_2$ objective. This yields \textbf{Truncated Jump Sampling} (TJS): stop the ODE at an early-exit time $t^*$ and return the decoded $x_0$. TJS requires no retraining, distillation, or architecture change. Across SDXL, SD3.5M, Z-Image-Turbo, and three class-conditional benchmarks, it reduces NFEs by 20--70\% with near-matched quality. The analysis also shows why endpoint prediction can work without straightening the trajectory, providing inference acceleration without trajectory redesign.

</details>

*Mohsen Ghafoorian, Denis Korzhenkov, Adil Karjauv, Ioannis Lelekas, Noor Fathima, Spyridon Stasis, Hanno Ackermann, Boris van Breugel, Markus Nagel, Fatih Porikli, Animesh Karnewar, Amirhossein Habibian*

Conclusion: 证明了服务器级大模型可以通过系统级优化高效部署到移动设备上，无需牺牲模型容量，为移动端高质量视频生成开辟了新路径。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient inference and mobile deployment of large video diffusion models, aligning with model efficiency and video generation priorities. Signals: 5B video diffusion on mobile, recurrence distillation, causal linear attention RNN, learnable attention head pruning, sampling-step distillation

Abstract: Recent advances in video diffusion have been driven by scaling transformer-based architectures to billions of parameters, substantially improving visual fidelity and motion coherence. In contrast, existing mobile video diffusion models remain limited to relatively small parameter budgets, typically 0.4-1.8B, restricting generation quality. In this work, we show that high-quality mobile video generation does not require small models. Instead, we demonstrate that a server-scale 5B-parameter video diffusion transformer can be deployed efficiently on memory-constrained mobile hardware through recurrent reformulation and structured compression. Starting from Wan2.2-5B, we rely on a recurrence distillation framework that converts video generation into a chunk-wise autoregressive process with constant-memory attention computation. Combined with causal linear attention, the model operates as an RNN at inference time while preserving temporal coherence across chunks. We further propose a learnable attention head pruning method based on binary per-head gates optimized end-to-end using a noise-biased sparsity objective and distillation-based finetuning. Together with sampling-step distillation and memory-optimized VAE decoding, MobileWan becomes the first 5B-scale video diffusion model deployable on a commercial mobile device. Our system generates 5-second 480x832 videos at 16 FPS in 20 seconds end-to-end latency, achieving a VBench score of 83.79 and establishing a new state of the art in mobile video generation. Project page:

*Jincheng Ying, Li Wenlin, Minghui Xu, Yinhao Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

Importance: 值得关注 (70.79) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出在扩散模型剪枝与步蒸馏之间引入一个短期的教师对齐修复阶段（teacher-alignment repair），作为桥梁解决直接替换后蒸馏失败的问题。在 ImageNet-512 上，20% 剪枝的单步生成器以更少参数和单步推理达到比原始 EDM2-XS 更优的 FID（3.12 vs 3.53）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型推理成本来自两方面：大网络和多次去噪步。现有压缩方法通常分别处理剪枝（减少网络）和步蒸馏（减少步数），但剪枝后需要长时间重训练恢复多步采样器，而步蒸馏又假设学生模型已足够好。本文探索能否用步蒸馏替代剪枝后的重训练，发现直接替代失败，因此需要一种桥接方案。

Method: 提出 Teacher-Aligned Repair (TAR) 阶段：在剪枝后的模型与教师模型之间，对含噪真实图像潜变量进行对齐匹配，使剪枝模型能跟上教师输出，然后再进行一步蒸馏（SiDA）。整个流程为：剪枝 → TAR 修复 → 一步蒸馏。

Result: 在 ImageNet-512 上，原始 EDM2-XS（124.713M 参数，63 步推理，FID 3.53）。20% 剪枝 + TAR + 一步蒸馏后（98.826M 参数，1 步推理，FID 3.12），优于基线。30% 剪枝后（88.029M 参数，1 步推理，FID 4.26）。

Conclusion: 短期的教师对齐修复阶段可以有效桥接剪枝与步蒸馏，使得剪枝后的模型能够成功进行一步蒸馏，在显著降低参数量和推理步数的同时保持甚至提升生成质量。

Classification reason: 该论文研究扩散模型的剪枝（pruning）与步蒸馏（step distillation）结合，旨在减少模型参数量和推理步数，属于生成模型加速与高效化的典型工作，因此映射到 canonical 的 efficient_generation 子主题。

Importance reason: Proposes a practical bridge between pruning and step distillation for diffusion models, directly addressing model efficiency. Signals: diffusion model acceleration, pruning-distillation bridge, teacher-alignment repair, single-step generation, ImageNet-512 results

Abstract: Diffusion models generate high-quality images, but their inference cost comes from two sources: large denoising networks and repeated denoising steps. Existing compression pipelines usually attack these costs separately. Pruning reduces the network, but most pruning methods still rely on a long post-pruning retraining stage to recover a many-step sampler. Step distillation reduces the number of denoising steps, but it usually assumes a student that can already follow the teacher well enough to receive useful distillation gradients. This paper asks whether post-pruning retraining can be replaced by step distillation. We find that the direct replacement fails: after pruning an EDM2-XS teacher, starting SiDA from the pruned checkpoint produces unusable samples. We introduce a short teacher-alignment repair stage as a bridge between pruning and step distillation. The bridge matches the pruned generator to the teacher on noisy real-image latents, then hands the repaired checkpoint to one-step distillation. On ImageNet-512, the original EDM2-XS baseline uses 124.713M parameters and 63 network evaluations, reaching an FID of 3.53. With a suitable distillation objective, our 20% pruned one-step generator uses 98.826M parameters and one network evaluation, reaching an FID of 3.12. With 30% pruning, the model uses 88.029M parameters and one network evaluation, with an FID of 4.26.

</details>

### [43] [AEGIS: A Mechanism-Guided Defense against Visual Synonym Jailbreaks in Text-to-Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06120)
*Yuanmin Huang, Zhenfei Zhang, Mi Zhang, Geng Hong, Qinqin He, Jialing Tao, Hui Xue, Min Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型对齐与奖励

Importance: 值得关注 (65.44) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 AEGIS，一种基于机制分析的推理时防御方法，通过识别和引导稀疏的语义注入注意力头，有效防御文生图模型中的视觉同义词越狱攻击，同时保持良性概念生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文生图模型的安全对齐方法主要针对显式不安全概念进行过滤、编辑或剪枝，无法有效防御视觉同义词攻击（VSA）——即通过看似良性的提示词隐式触发违规图像生成。核心挑战在于 VSA 在文本层面隐藏了不安全目标，而在生成过程中通过视觉-语义收敛暴露出来。因此需要从静态抑制转向动态追踪不安全语义的生成过程。

Method: 通过机制分析发现，VSA 与显式不安全提示在生成过程中通过稀疏的语义注入注意力头（semantic-injecting attention heads）收敛，这些注意力头是禁止视觉语义的推理时瓶颈。基于此，提出 AEGIS（Adaptive Evasion Guard via Identification and Steering），一种推理时防御方法，仅在识别出的脆弱注意力头上应用相似度感知的排斥（similarity-aware repulsion），从而阻断不安全语义的注入。

Result: 在 SD 1.4 上，AEGIS 将域内暴力/色情 VSA 的攻击成功率降至 0.00/0.03，域外显式攻击和对抗攻击的 ASR ≤ 0.09。同时保持良性生成保真度，避免抑制硬负例概念，并可迁移至 SD 2.1 和 FLUX.1。

Conclusion: AEGIS 通过机制引导的推理时防御，有效解决了文生图模型在视觉同义词攻击下的安全-效用困境，为生成模型的安全对齐提供了新的思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究文生图扩散模型的安全对齐问题，提出针对视觉同义词越狱攻击的推理时防御方法，属于生成模型的安全对齐与可控性范畴，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型对齐与奖励" (generation_alignment)。

Importance reason: Focuses on safety defense for text-to-image models, not on core generation or world model advances. Signals: defense against visual synonym attacks, attention head steering, safety-utility tradeoff, diffusion model alignment

Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion models have achieved high visual fidelity and broad adoption, but remain vulnerable to safety violations when adversaries exploit them to synthesize illicit content. Existing alignment paradigms, from input sanitization to structural feature pruning, are largely organized around unsafe concepts explicitly exposed during filtering, editing, or localization. This leaves a blind spot for visual synonym attacks (VSA), a jailbreak where benign-looking prompts elicit prohibited imagery through implicit visual associations. As a result, current defenses face a safety-utility dilemma: they may either under-mitigate VSA threats or over-suppress visually similar benign concepts. The core challenge is that VSA hides the unsafe target at the textual surface while revealing it through generation-time visual-semantic convergence. In this work, we therefore shift from static suppression of pre-specified unsafe concepts to dynamic tracing of how unsafe semantics emerge during generation. Our mechanistic analysis shows that VSA and explicit unsafe prompts converge through sparse semantic-injecting attention heads, which serve as inference-time bottlenecks for prohibited visual semantics. Based on this insight, we propose AEGIS (Adaptive Evasion Guard via Identification and Steering), an inference-time defense that applies similarity-aware repulsion only at the identified vulnerable heads. Evaluated against 16 baselines, AEGIS improves both safety and utility. On SD 1.4, it reduces ASR to $\mathbf{0.00}/\mathbf{0.03}$ for in-domain violence/nudity VSA and achieves ASRs $\le \mathbf{0.09}$ on out-of-domain explicit and adversarial attacks. It preserves benign fidelity, avoids suppressing hard-negative concepts, and transfers to SD 2.1 and FLUX.1 after re-identifying the critical heads for each backbone.

</details>

*Paul Koch. Adem Karakurt, André Sers*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.RO

TL;DR: GraspIT 是一个桥接仿真与真实世界的机器人抓取数据集，包含超过 2.3M 个候选抓取姿态的物理滑移测试标注、1035 个仿真场景和 100 个真实场景的 RGB-D 帧，支持 6-DoF 抓取姿态生成与策略学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人抓取数据集无法同时提供逼真的 RGB-D 观测、物理验证的抓取质量标注以及仿真与真实世界之间的原则性桥梁，GraspIT 旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在 NVIDIA Isaac Sim 中构建桌面场景，通过四阶段物理滑移测试对 Franka Panda 机械臂的抓取候选进行标注，产生轨迹可达性检查和连续质量分数。通过 Real↔Sim 循环将标注反向投影到 100 个真实场景中，提供实例掩码、6-DoF 姿态、物理属性及评分抓取姿态。

Result: 超过 2.3M 个候选抓取中 83% 被判定为"好"(s≥0.50)，17% 通过力闭合但未通过滑移测试的样本作为分级难例。最终提供约 316k 标注 RGB-D 帧，覆盖 1035 个仿真场景和 100 个真实场景。

Conclusion: GraspIT 提供了一个兼具物理验证标注和仿真-真实桥梁的抓取数据集，可支持抓取姿态生成、策略学习与行为克隆等任务，所有工具均已开源并 Docker 容器化。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 GraspIT 数据集，包含仿真和真实场景的 RGB-D 观测、物理验证的抓取质量标注，属于生成模型评测与数据集方向，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型评测与数据集" (generation_benchmark)。

Importance reason: Focuses on robotic grasping dataset, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or video/image generation. Signals: Robotic grasping dataset, Sim-to-real transfer, Physical slip-test validation, Not in priority subtopics, No boost keywords matched

Abstract: Robust robotic grasping of novel objects requires datasets that simultaneously provide photorealistic RGB-D observations, physically validated grasp quality annotations, and a principled bridge between simulation and the real world, which existing datasets lack to provide jointly. \textbf{GraspIT} addresses this gap: tabletop scenes in NVIDIA Isaac Sim are annotated via a four-stage physical slip-test on parallel Franka Panda instances, producing trajectory-reachability checks and continuous quality scores beyond ${\sim}$2.3M candidates, 83% pass as \emph{good} ($s{\geq}0.50$); the 17% that passed force-closure but failed the slip-test provide graded hard negatives. A Real$\leftrightarrow$Sim loop back-projects these labels onto 100 real-world scenes. The release provides ${\sim}$316k annotated RGBD frame sets across 1035 sim and 100 real scenes, with instance masks, 6-DoF poses, physical object properties, and scored 6-DoF grasps. All tools are open-source and Docker-containerized. The trajectory planning within Isaac Sim further allows streaming of high resolution demonstrations for tabletop manipulation policy learning and behavior cloning.

</details>

*Sharayu N. Deshmukh, Md Rashidunnabi, Nelton Tiago Gemo, Kurundkar G. D., Mahamune M. R., Nilesh K. Deshmukh*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

TL;DR: VendorBench-100 是一个统一的跨范式深度伪造图像检测基准，使用 100 张对抗性图像评估了 36 个模型（商业 API、零样本视觉 LLM、开源检测器），发现商业 API 中位性能最强，但关键发现是 ROC-AUC（排序能力）与 MCC（操作点质量）之间存在持续分歧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前深度伪造图像检测存在三种不同范式（商业 API、零样本视觉语言模型、开源检测器），但这些范式很少在统一协议下进行评估，导致直接比较困难。现有基准往往追求数据集规模而非挑战性场景，且缺乏对类不平衡问题的适当评估指标。

Method: 构建了一个包含 100 张对抗性图像的语料库，涵盖 8 个边缘案例类别（换脸、文生视频静帧、AI 照片编辑、头像合成、不透明来源图像、压缩研究帧等）。采用统一输出模式和评估框架，使用 MCC 作为主要排名指标（应对类不平衡），辅以 ROC-AUC 作为阈值无关的排序能力指标。

Result: 商业 API 取得最强中位性能，其次是视觉 LLM 和开源检测器。但个别开源模型可与最佳视觉 LLM 竞争。更重要的是，发现 ROC-AUC（排序能力）与 MCC（操作点质量）之间存在持续分歧——强评分区分能力并不一定能产生可靠的默认阈值决策。

Conclusion: 指标分歧（而非单一排行榜排名）是基准的核心发现。该工作强调了在深度伪造检测评估中需要同时关注排序能力和操作点质量，并发布了完整的评估框架和结果以支持可重复研究。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个跨范式的深度伪造图像检测基准（VendorBench-100），属于生成模型评测范畴。在 multimodal_generation 方向下有 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型评测与数据集"（generation_benchmark），该论文完全匹配此主题。同时该基准涉及视觉语言模型（VLM）的评估，也与 multimodal_understanding_generation 方向相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on deepfake detection benchmarking, not aligned with user's priority on generative models and continuous representations. Signals: Deepfake detection benchmark, cross-paradigm evaluation, metric divergence finding, no generative model contribution, no continuous token or world model focus

Abstract: Deepfake image detection is currently served by three fundamentally different paradigms: commercial APIs, zero-shot vision-language models (LLMs), and open-source detectors. Despite their widespread use, these paradigms are rarely evaluated under a common protocol, making direct comparison difficult. We introduce VendorBench-100, a cross-paradigm benchmark that evaluates 36 representative models using a single adversarial 100-image corpus, a unified output schema, and a common evaluation framework. To ensure reliable assessment under the corpus's intentional class imbalance, models are ranked primarily by the Matthews correlation coefficient (MCC), with ROC-AUC reported as a threshold-independent measure of ranking ability. Rather than maximizing dataset size, VendorBench-100 emphasizes challenging real-world scenarios through a curated taxonomy of eight edge-case families, including face swaps, text-to-video stills, AI photo edits, avatar compositing, opaque-provenance images, and compressed research frames. Our evaluation shows that commercial APIs achieve the strongest median performance, followed by vision LLMs and open-source detectors. However, individual open-source models remain competitive with the best vision LLMs. More importantly, we identify a consistent divergence between ranking ability (ROC-AUC) and operating-point quality (MCC), demonstrating that strong score discrimination does not necessarily produce reliable default-threshold decisions. This metric disagreement, rather than any single leaderboard ranking, is the central finding of the benchmark. We release the complete evaluation framework and benchmark results to support reproducible future research. The source code and data are available at:

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的不完整多视图聚类方法使用随机噪声到数据的轨迹，初始化于与聚类无关的噪声，且随机去噪动力学并非为聚类任务专门设计，导致聚类一致性不足。

Result: 在标准IMVC基准数据集上，该方法在所有评估指标上均达到新的SOTA性能，验证了确定性ODE流比扩散随机轨迹更有利于聚类目标。

Conclusion: 确定性ODE概率流比扩散随机轨迹更适用于聚类任务，直路径流匹配为不完整多视图聚类提供了一种更有效的生成式补全框架。

Classification reason: 论文提出了一种基于直路径流匹配的不完整多视图聚类方法，属于多模态生成方向中的生成模型方法。虽然论文核心是聚类，但其方法基于flow matching生成缺失视图，属于生成模型范畴。最接近的现有子主题是"生成模型评测与数据集"（generation_benchmark），因为论文在标准IMVC基准上进行了评测并报告了SOTA结果。但更准确地说，该论文的方法论核心是flow matching生成，与"生成模型加速与高效化"或"文生图与图像扩散"不完全匹配。考虑到论文主要贡献是flow matching框架用于视图补全，且现有子主题中没有专门针对"flow matching用于视图补全/聚类"的类别，但论文在标准基准上评测，故归入generation_benchmark作为最接近的现有子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on incomplete multi-view clustering, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks strong alignment with user's key directions. Signals: Flow matching for view completion, Deterministic ODE vs diffusion, Clustering-level alignment, Not autoregressive or video/image generation, No continuous language/multimodal model

Abstract: Incomplete Multi-View Clustering addresses the problem of clustering multi-modal data when certain views are missing. Recent end-to-end generative approaches leverage diffusion models to recover missing views via stochastic noise-to-data trajectories. While expressive, such mechanisms are not explicitly designed for clustering, as they initialize from cluster-agnostic noise and rely on stochastic denoising dynamics. In this work, we revisit probability path design in end-to-end generative IMVC. We introduce a flow-matching framework with a linear interpolation path between paired view representations, that replaces diffusion with probability flows between observed and missing views. We provide a formal analysis showing that deterministic ODE flows are inherently better aligned with clustering objectives than diffusion-based stochastic trajectories, especially in terms of transport mechanisms that respect class-conditional data distributions and maintain cluster consistency in finite-step regimes. Building upon this insight, we develop an end-to-end IMVC architecture that integrates straight-path flow-matching view completion with cluster-level and entropy-based alignment to enforce cross-view clustering consistency. Extensive experiments on standard IMVC benchmarks demonstrate that the proposed framework establishes new state-of-the-art performance.

</details>

<details>

Motivation: 现有文生图模型（如 DALL-E 3）能遵循多样化提示，但无法感知用户的个性化审美偏好。个性化图像生成需要模型根据用户少量历史偏好图像和简短提示来对齐输出，但缺乏统一的评测基准来评估这类方法。

Method: 提出 PIPBench 评测框架，包含：(1) 利用心理学和人口统计学维度构建用户画像的数据构建流水线；(2) 真实用户数据收集与可扩展的基于智能体的数据生成两种模式；(3) 对代表性个性化图像生成方法进行系统评测。

Result: 通过 PIPBench 对现有方法进行评测，揭示了它们在个性化图像生成任务中的关键局限性，包括对用户偏好的捕捉能力不足、个性化程度有限等问题。

Conclusion: PIPBench 为个性化图像生成提供了首个标准化评测框架，揭示了现有方法的不足，为未来个性化文生图研究指明了新挑战和方向。

Abstract: Recent text-to-image models such as DALLE-3 excel at following diverse prompts yet remain blind to individual aesthetic preferences. We study personalized image generation, where models must align outputs with a user's implicit visual preferences based on a few historically preferred images and a short prompt. To this end, we introduce PIPBench, the first profile-inclusive benchmark for evaluating personalized image generation. We further propose a novel data construction pipeline that leverages psychological and demographic profiling dimensions for both real-user data collection and scalable agent-based data generation. Using PIPBench, we conduct a thorough evaluation of representative line of methods. Our experiments reveal key limitations in existing methods, suggesting new challenges and opportunities for personalized text-to-image synthesis. Project page:

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 户型图在不同地区因建筑文化、空间约束和施工实践差异而广泛变化，但获取新标注数据集成本高昂且具有领域特异性。现有工作未研究条件化户型图生成模型对域迁移的鲁棒性，因此需要探索如何以最小目标域监督来缓解域偏移问题。

Method: 提出一种程序化合成数据生成方法，通过强制严格的物理约束（房间不重叠、门位置有效、图一致性）并有意引入高度不规则的空间排列和激进的几何扰动来牺牲建筑真实感，生成大规模合成户型图数据集。在三个公开数据集（RPLAN、MagicPlan、Swiss Dwellings）上评估了两种不同生成范式（自回归和扩散）的SOTA模型，进行零样本跨域测试和低数据微调实验。

Result: 现有模型对域迁移高度敏感，跨域时性能下降可达一个数量级。合成数据预训练显著提升零样本跨域性能，在MagicPlan上甚至优于域内训练。作为微调初始化，合成数据预训练在低数据场景下比真实数据初始化基线提升高达40%。

Conclusion: 合成数据预训练是一种数据高效且有效的域适应策略，能够显著提升户型图生成模型在跨域场景下的鲁棒性，为实际部署中应对数据稀缺问题提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究条件化户型图生成模型在不同数据集间的域迁移鲁棒性，评估了多种生成范式在跨域场景下的性能退化，并提出了一种大规模合成数据集预训练策略来缓解域偏移。论文的核心贡献包括跨域评测和合成数据集构建，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型评测与数据集"（generation_benchmark）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on domain shift in floor plan generation, which is a niche application not aligned with user's priority subtopics. Signals: domain shift, floor plan generation, synthetic pre-training, data-efficient adaptation, low-data regime

Abstract: Robustness to domain shift is a key requirement for floor plan generative models to be applicable beyond the single dataset they were trained on, as floor plans vary widely across regions due to distinct architectural cultures, spatial constraints, and construction practices, while acquiring new annotated datasets remains costly and domain-specific. Yet, no prior work has studied this robustness in the context of conditioned floor plan generation. In this paper, we evaluate state-of-the-art models from two fundamentally different generative paradigms across three public datasets (RPLAN, MagicPlan and Swiss Dwellings) and show that they are highly sensitive to domain shift, with up to an order of magnitude performance degradation when transferred across domains. To mitigate this with minimal target-domain supervision, we introduce a procedural method to generate a large-scale synthetic training dataset that enforces strict physical constraints (non-overlapping rooms, valid door placement, graph consistency) while intentionally sacrificing architectural realism through highly irregular spatial arrangements and aggressive geometric perturbation of room shapes. We show that pre-training on this synthetic data considerably improves zero-shot cross-domain performance, outperforming in-domain training on MagicPlan. Furthermore, it provides a highly effective initialization for fine-tuning, accelerating target domain adaptation and outperforming real-world initialization baselines by up to 40% in a low-data regime.

*Thanh V. T. Tran, Ngoc-Son Nguyen, Luong Tran, Long-Khanh Pham, Paarth Neekhara, Shezheen Hussain, Van Nguyen*

TL;DR: 本文提出 Flowley，一个端到端单阶段训练的视频到音频生成架构，通过渐进式软掩码交叉注意力机制实现音视频同步，并引入 SoundCap 管道生成声音感知描述以提升音频质量，在 VGGSound 上达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 V2A 方法要么依赖多阶段训练导致高计算成本，要么将视觉输入转为文本以利用预训练文生音频模型，从而丢失细粒度时序信息。此外，现有 V2A 基准缺乏面向声音的描述性标注，限制了生成质量。

Method: 提出 Flowley 端到端单阶段训练架构，结合视觉特征与文本提示生成音频。核心创新是 Progressive Soft-masked Cross-Attention（渐进式软掩码交叉注意力），将音视频同步嵌入注意力机制中，不增加额外计算开销。同时提出 SoundCap 管道，为视频生成详细的声音感知描述以引导模型。

Result: 在 VGGSound 基准上，Flowley 在多个指标上达到 SOTA，无需任何预训练的音视频对齐模块。结合 SoundCap 后，在零样本设置下音频质量甚至超过最强的闭源方法。

Conclusion: Flowley 证明了端到端单阶段训练在 V2A 任务中的有效性，渐进式软掩码交叉注意力机制能够高效实现音视频同步，SoundCap 管道进一步提升了生成音频的质量和语义一致性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究视频到音频生成（V2A），属于音视频联合生成任务，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "音频与多模态联合生成"（audio_av_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel cross-modal alignment in latent space for video-to-audio, but outside user's priority subtopics. Signals: Video-to-audio generation, Cross-modal alignment, Progressive soft-masked cross-attention, SoundCap pipeline, Single-stage training

Abstract: Video-to-audio (V2A) generation aims to synthesize realistic audio that is both semantically consistent with and temporally synchronized to a silent video. Despite recent progress, many methods still rely on multi-stage training, resulting in high computational costs and long runtimes, or transform visual input into text to leverage pretrained text-to-audio models, sacrificing fine-grained temporal cues. To overcome these limitations, we propose Flowley, an end-to-end, single-stage training architecture that produces soundtracks by combining visual features with textual prompts. Crucially, we introduce Progressive Soft-masked Cross-Attention, which embeds audio-visual synchronization directly within its attention mechanism, adding zero additional computational cost compared to standard attention layers. We further observe that existing V2A benchmarks lack sound-oriented descriptive captions, which can potentially degrade the quality of the synthesized audio. To remedy this, we propose SoundCap, a plug-and-play pipeline for creating detailed, sound-aware captions that guide the model. Remarkably, without integrating any pretrained audio-visual alignment modules, Flowley achieves state-of-the-art performance on VGGSound across multiple metrics. Moreover, by incorporating SoundCap, we further exceed the performance of the strongest existing close-sourced methods in terms of audio quality in the zero-shot setting.

</details>


### [50] [WordVoice: Explicit and Decoupled Multi-Dimensional Word-Level Control for LLM-Based TTS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06461)
*Sihang Nie, Jinxin Ji, Xiaofen Xing, Deyi Tuo, Chengbin Jin, Jialong Mai, Xiangmin Xu*

Main category: eess.AS, cs.SD, cs.CL

Importance: 普通相关 (53.74) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: WordVoice 是一个基于LLM的TTS框架，通过构建4.7k小时的双语词级标注数据集WordVoice-5A，并引入bound-token机制实现显式的词级声学规划，以及细粒度声学调制模块弥合离散token与连续波形之间的分辨率差距，实现了对时长、边界、能量、音高和语调五个维度的解耦词级控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的TTS系统虽然自然度出色，但依赖隐式端到端生成范式，控制粒度粗糙。在有声书旁白、视频配音等需要精确风格干预和严格时间对齐的场景中，无法显式操控词级声学属性。这一瓶颈主要源于细粒度标注数据的严重缺乏，以及将多维控制信号集成到离散自回归生成中的架构挑战。

Method: 1) 构建WordVoice-5A数据集：通过语言学引导的pipeline，对4.7k小时中英文语音数据进行五维词级标注（时长、边界、能量、音高、语调）。2) 提出WordVoice框架：在LLM中引入bound-token机制，形成显式的"声学规划"过程，实现自适应多任务韵律规划和灵活的人工干预；在token到波形阶段增加细粒度声学调制模块，弥合高压缩离散token与连续波形之间的分辨率差距。

Result: 实验表明WordVoice在多个声学维度上实现了优越的解耦控制，同时保持了具有竞争力的零样本合成稳定性。

Conclusion: WordVoice通过显式词级控制机制和高质量标注数据集，有效解决了LLM-based TTS系统中细粒度声学属性控制不足的问题，为需要精确时间对齐和风格干预的语音合成应用提供了可行的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究基于LLM的文本到语音（TTS）系统，属于音频生成方向，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "音频与多模态联合生成"（audio_av_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on TTS control, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or video/image generation. Signals: TTS, word-level control, LLM-based, audio generation, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: While recent Large Language Model (LLM)-based Text-to-Speech (TTS) systems have achieved remarkable naturalness, they predominantly rely on implicit end-to-end generation paradigms, resulting in coarse-grained control. In scenarios demanding precise stylistic interventions and strict temporal alignment, such as audiobook narration and video dubbing, the inability to explicitly manipulate word-level acoustic attributes remains a critical bottleneck. This limitation is primarily amplified by the severe scarcity of fine-grained annotated datasets and the architectural challenge of integrating multi-dimensional control signals into discrete autoregressive generation. To address this, we propose a unified framework for highly precise word-level control. First, we construct WordVoice-5A, a massive 4.7k-hour bilingual dataset featuring five-dimensional word-level annotations (duration, boundary, energy, pitch and tone) developed through a rigorous linguistically-guided pipeline. Second, we introduce WordVoice to transform the implicit generation process into an explicit, highly controllable paradigm. Specifically, we introduce a bound-token mechanism within the LLM to formulate an explicit ``acoustic planning'' process, enabling adaptive multi-task prosodic planning and flexible manual intervention. Furthermore, we augment the token-to-waveform stage with a fine-grained acoustic modulation module, bridging the resolution gap to strictly align word-level attributes between highly compressed discrete tokens and continuous waveforms. Extensive experiments demonstrate that WordVoice achieves superior, decoupled control over multiple acoustic dimensions while maintaining competitive zero-shot synthesis stability. The code and audio samples are publicly available at .

</details>

### [51] [Controlling Tool Use with Heading-Specific Activation Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05790)
*Yuqi Chen, Vincent Siu, Yang Liu, Dawn Song, Chenguang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 普通相关 (49.29) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型工具调用行为的内部表征，发现从特定位置（heading-anchors）提取的引导向量可以对工具调用行为进行双向因果控制，有效抑制不必要的工具调用。但几何分析表明，这种因果有效性并不对应清晰的线性结构，工具调用步骤的激活模式是弥散且双峰的，不同工具类型具有不同的内部特征，与参数化概念的表征存在本质区别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具增强的大语言模型虽然能通过外部工具扩展能力，但往往会不必要地调用工具。作者希望探究工具调用决策是否存在稳定的内部表征可以被提取和操控——这是一个非平凡的问题，因为工具完全存在于推理时的上下文中，没有直接编码在模型权重中。

Method: 从模型特定位置（heading-anchors）提取引导向量（steering vectors），对工具调用行为进行双向因果控制。在五个开源模型和三个领域上进行实验，并通过几何分析（包括线性探测、方向对齐分析等）研究工具调用步骤的内部表征结构。

Result: 引导向量能有效抑制不必要的工具调用，尤其在参数化推理足以解决问题的领域效果最好。但几何分析显示：工具调用步骤的激活模式是弥散且双峰的，与抑制向量的对齐并非一致的负向对齐；不同工具类型（如计算器、搜索）具有不同的内部特征，跨工具特征重叠度低。

Conclusion: 工具调用行为的内部表征具有非参数化性质，与参数化概念的表征存在本质区别。引导向量的因果有效性与几何不规则性之间的关系仍是一个开放问题。这些发现有助于理解LLM中工具使用的内部机制，并为更精确地控制模型行为提供指导。

Classification reason: 该论文研究通过激活引导（activation steering）控制大语言模型的工具调用行为，属于模型行为控制与安全对齐范畴。虽然论文主要关注工具调用而非多模态，但其方法（activation steering）与多模态安全攻防中的对抗性控制、行为操控高度相关，且该方向下的"多模态安全与攻防"子主题涵盖模型行为控制与防御。论文涉及对模型内部表征的因果操控，与安全攻防主题契合。

Importance reason: Focuses on tool-use control via activation steering, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: Tool-use control, activation steering, heading-anchors, causal intervention, non-parametric representation

Abstract: Tool-augmented large language models extend their capabilities beyond parametric knowledge through external tools, but tend to invoke them unnecessarily. We investigate whether tool-use decisions have any stable internal representation that can be extracted and manipulated, a question that is non-trivial given that tools exist entirely in context at inference time and have no direct encoding in model weights. We show that steering vectors extracted from heading-anchors positions exert bidirectional causal control over tool-invocation behavior across five open-source models and three domains, suppressing unnecessary tool use most effectively in domains where parametric reasoning suffices. However, geometric analysis reveals that this causal effectiveness does not correspond to clean linear structure: tool-invocation steps exhibit diffuse, bimodal alignment with the suppression vector rather than the consistent negative alignment a linear encoding account would predict, and different tool types recruit largely distinct internal signatures with low cross-tool feature overlap. We hypothesize these geometric properties are indicative of the non-parametric nature of tools, and distinguish tool-use steering vectors from those extracted for parametrically grounded concepts. The relationship between this geometric irregularity and the observed causal effectiveness remains an open question.

</details>


### [52] [PolicyShiftGuard: Benchmarking and Improving Policy-Adaptive Image Guardrails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05910)
*Mingyang Song, Luxin Xu, Haoyu Sun, Minzhou Pan, Yu Cheng, Bo Li*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 普通相关 (57.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 PolicyShiftBench 基准和 PolicyShiftGuard 方法，研究图像安全护栏在动态安全策略下的自适应能力。现有 VLM 和专用护栏在策略变化时表现脆弱，而 PolicyShiftGuard 通过两阶段训练（RP-SFT + BP-Adapt）显著提升了策略敏感性能，7B 模型在 PolicyShiftBench 上达到 76.9 Avg. F1 和 72.1 Avg. PSS 的 SOTA 结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像安全护栏通常在固定安全策略下训练和评测，隐含地将安全性视为图像的固有属性。然而实际部署中，同一张图像在不同产品、不同策略边界下可能被允许或禁止。现有模型无法适应策略变化，缺乏策略自适应能力。

Method: 提出 PolicyShiftGuard，采用两阶段训练：1) Randomized Policy SFT (RP-SFT)：随机采样策略进行监督微调，让模型学习策略条件化判断；2) Boundary-Pair Policy Adaptation (BP-Adapt)：对同一图像和风险类别构建匹配的通过/阻止策略对，使用标准标签监督和成对对比损失进行训练，使模型区分不同策略边界下的决策。

Result: PolicyShiftGuard 7B 模型在 PolicyShiftBench 上达到 76.9 Avg. F1 和 72.1 Avg. PSS 的 SOTA 性能，在 UnSafeBench 和 SafeEditBench 上也有良好的迁移表现，且输出格式简洁，改善了延迟-性能权衡。

Conclusion: 策略自适应的图像安全护栏是实际部署中的关键需求，匹配的通过/阻止边界对（BP-Adapt）是实现稳定策略适应的关键组件。PolicyShiftGuard 为动态策略环境下的图像安全提供了有效方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究图像安全护栏（image guardrails）在策略变化下的鲁棒性，属于多模态安全与攻防方向。论文提出了策略自适应图像护栏的评测基准（PolicyShiftBench）和改进方法（PolicyShiftGuard），核心是多模态安全防御与评测，因此映射到 canonical 子主题"多模态安全与攻防"。

Importance reason: Focuses on safety guardrails, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to user's core interests in continuous multimodal models or video/image generation. Signals: Novel benchmark for policy-adaptive guardrails, strong empirical results, no overlap with priority subtopics, negative keywords absent, safety-focused rather than generation or world models

Abstract: Image guardrails are typically trained and evaluated under a fixed safety policy, implicitly treating safety as an intrinsic property of an image. Real deployments are different: the same image may be allowed in one product, restricted in another, and newly disallowed when a policy boundary changes. We study policy-adaptive image guardrailing, where a model must decide whether an image violates the currently supplied policy and generalize to held-out policy definitions. We introduce PolicyShiftBench, a comprehensive benchmark with 2,000 policy-discriminative instances over 265 images, where each image is paired with 7.55 policy-conditioned prompts on average to test whether models adapt to the active policy rather than relying on image-level safety priors. We then propose PolicyShiftGuard, a compact policy-conditioned guardrail trained with a two-stage training recipe that combines Randomized Policy SFT (RP-SFT) with Boundary-Pair Policy Adaptation (BP-Adapt). BP-Adapt trains matched prompts for the same image and risk category using standard label supervision and a pairwise comparison loss that separates blocking policies from passing policies. Experiments show that existing VLMs and specialized guardrails remain brittle under policy shifts, while PolicyShiftGuard substantially improves policy-sensitive performance. The 7B model achieves SOTA performance of 76.9 Avg. F1 and 72.1 Avg. PSS on PolicyShiftBench, transfers well to UnSafeBench and SafeEditBench, and improves the latency-performance trade-off with a concise output format. Ablations confirm that matched pass/block boundary pairs are essential for stable policy adaptation.

</details>


### [53] [AirflowAttack: Thermal-Airflow Adversarial Perturbations against Infrared Remote-Sensing Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06485)
*Cong Su, Jiaju Han, Xuemeng Sun, Chengyin Hu, Qike Zhang, Jiujiang Guo, Yiwei Wei, Jiahuan Long*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 普通相关 (44.11) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: AirflowAttack 是首个针对红外遥感 VLM 的对抗攻击方法，利用热空气流扰动先验生成输入无关的通用扰动，在多个 CLIP 骨干和 SOTA VLM 上实现了显著的攻击成功率，并揭示了模型会将扰动误判为真实热证据的漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 红外遥感 VLM 在安全关键场景中部署日益增多，但其对抗鲁棒性尚未被研究。现有对抗攻击方法未考虑红外物理特性，缺乏针对红外遥感 VLM 的攻击手段。

Method: 提出 AirflowAttack，使用轻量级生成器合成单个输入无关的通用扰动，通过正则化约束使其符合物理可行的热空气流模式。在单个代理 CLIP 模型上优化后，可直接迁移攻击其他模型。

Result: 在五个不同 CLIP 骨干上平均零样本场景分类攻击成功率达 48.5%，远超四种红外物理基线（27.7-37.0%）。应用于六个 SOTA VLM 后，场景分类准确率相对下降最高达 38.2%。消融实验表明气流先验在不牺牲攻击性能的前提下提高了物理合理性。

Conclusion: AirflowAttack 揭示了红外遥感 VLM 生态系统中存在的严重安全漏洞，模型甚至会将对抗扰动误判为真实热证据（如温度梯度、对流），亟需针对红外物理特性的防御机制。

Classification reason: 该论文研究红外遥感视觉语言模型（VLM）的对抗攻击，属于多模态安全与攻防范畴，与 canonical 子主题 multimodal_safety_attack 完全匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on adversarial attacks for infrared remote-sensing VLMs, which is outside your priority subtopics and includes negative keywords. Signals: adversarial attack, infrared remote sensing, vision-language model, thermal airflow perturbation, security vulnerability

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly deployed on infrared (IR) remote sensing imagery in security-critical settings, yet their adversarial robustness remains unexamined. We present AirflowAttack, to our knowledge the first adversarial attack for IR remote-sensing VLMs and the first to weaponize thermal-airflow turbulence as the perturbation prior. A lightweight generator synthesizes a single input-agnostic perturbation regularized toward physically plausible airflow patterns. Optimized on one surrogate CLIP model, it attains a mean zero-shot scene-classification attack success rate (ASR, the fraction of samples whose top-1 class changes) of 48.5% across five diverse CLIP backbones, far exceeding four IR-specific physical baselines (27.7--37.0%). Applied to six state-of-the-art VLMs, it cuts scene-classification accuracy by up to 38.2% relative, yet paradoxically makes some models more confident in their IR analysis, confabulating the perturbation as genuine thermal evidence such as temperature gradients and convection. Ablations show the airflow prior raises physical plausibility at no measurable cost to attack success. Together with a benchmark spanning eleven models and four tasks, these findings expose critical vulnerabilities in the rapidly expanding IR VLM ecosystem.

</details>

### [54] [Mitigating Factual Hallucination in Large Reasoning Models via Mixed-Mode Advantage Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05861)
*Kaishen Wang, Tong Zheng, Xuehao Cui, Ruibo Chen, Tianyi Xiong, Heng Huang*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (52.44) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文发现大型推理模型（LRM）在事实性问答中，显式思维链虽然整体提升性能，但会在实例层面导致"思维诱导幻觉"——即思维过程将原本正确的直接答案推翻。作者将显式思维建模为直接答案倾向上的残差，并提出MARGO框架，通过混合模式（思维/非思维）轨迹组的强化学习，用非思维轨迹作为同模型参考来评估思维是否带来事实增益，从而抑制易幻觉思维、保留有益思维。实验表明MARGO在多个事实性QA基准上提升了事实可靠性，同时保持数学推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型（LRM）通过显式思维链提升了整体性能，但作者发现这种提升在实例层面并不均匀——显式思维有时会将原本正确的直接答案推翻，导致"思维诱导幻觉"。现有方法缺乏对这种失败模式的系统理解和针对性缓解。

Method: 提出MARGO（Mixed-Mode Advantage Regularization for Grounded Optimization）框架。核心思想是将显式思维建模为直接答案倾向上的残差（thinking residual），该残差可能恢复缺失知识或引入无依据关联。MARGO使用强化学习，构造包含思维轨迹和非思维轨迹的混合模式轨迹组，以非思维轨迹作为同模型参考基线来计算优势函数，从而评估显式思维是否带来事实增益，抑制易幻觉思维行为。

Result: 在多个事实性QA基准上，MARGO相比强基线方法提升了事实可靠性；同时在数学推理基准上的评估表明，该方法不会损害通用推理能力。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了LRM中思维诱导幻觉这一新现象，并提出了有效的缓解方法MARGO。通过混合模式优势正则化，能够在保留有益思维的同时抑制有害思维，为提升推理模型的事实可靠性提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究大型推理模型（LRM）中的事实幻觉问题，提出混合模式优势正则化方法缓解thinking-induced hallucination。这与多模态幻觉与可靠性（multimodal_hallucination）高度相关，该[CANONICAL]子主题涵盖幻觉缓解、事实性与可靠性提升，论文虽聚焦文本QA但方法可推广至多模态场景，且幻觉缓解是该子主题的核心内容。

Importance reason: Focuses on factual hallucination in reasoning models, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: Factual hallucination, Large reasoning models, Reinforcement learning, Mixed-mode advantage, Not aligned with user's priority subtopics

Abstract: Large reasoning models (LRMs) improve language model capabilities by generating explicit thinking traces before final answers. In factuality-oriented question answering (QA), such thinking often improves overall performance by helping the model recover relevant knowledge and refine its answers. However, we find that this benefit is not uniform at the instance level: explicit thinking can also overturn correct non-thinking answers and lead to factual drift. We refer to this failure mode as \emph{thinking-induced hallucination}. To explain this phenomenon, we formulate explicit thinking in factuality QA as a thinking residual over the model's direct-answer tendency, which can either recover missing knowledge or introduce unsupported associations. Based on this formulation, we propose MARGO, \underline{\textit{M}}ixed-Mode \underline{\textit{A}}dvantage \underline{\textit{R}}egularization for \underline{\textit{G}}rounded \underline{\textit{O}}ptimization, a reinforcement learning framework that uses non-thinking rollouts as same-model references in advantage estimation. By constructing mixed-mode rollout groups with both thinking and non-thinking trajectories, MARGO evaluates whether explicit thinking adds factual value beyond direct answering, thereby suppressing hallucination-prone thinking while preserving beneficial thinking behaviors. Experiments across multiple factuality-oriented QA benchmarks demonstrate that MARGO improves factual reliability over strong baselines, while evaluations on mathematical benchmarks show that it preserves general reasoning ability.

</details>


### [55] [Propose and Attend: Training-free MLLM Grounding Confidence via Multi-Token Localized Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05978)
*Daniel Shalam, Emanuel Ben Baruch, Avi Ben Cohen, Tal Remez*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 值得关注 (76.55) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出Multi-Token Localized Attention (MTLA)，一种无需训练的后验置信度评分方法，通过衡量预测token对声称区域的注意力强度来检测MLLM的定位幻觉。该方法在图像目标检测、视频和音频时间定位上显著优于现有无训练基线，将零样本COCO检测AP从20.4提升至37.0。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLM）在输出定位预测（如目标框、时间窗口）时存在严重的幻觉问题，而模型自身的token对数概率几乎无法反映定位质量，因为它混淆了定位质量与输入歧义，且坐标token一旦模型做出决策就变得近乎确定性。因此需要一种无需训练、能有效衡量定位置信度的方法。

Method: 提出Multi-Token Localized Attention (MTLA)，核心思想是：对于一个定位预测（如边界框或时间窗口），计算该预测的所有token对声称区域内视觉/音频token的注意力总和，作为置信度分数。与之前仅读取单个响应token且对整个输入模态求和注意力的方法不同，MTLA在声称区域内求和注意力并聚合所有预测token，从而恢复更强的定位信号。该方法适用于图像目标检测、视频时间定位和音频时间定位等多种模态和任务。

Result: 在多个MLLM家族和三种模态上，MTLA将幻觉AUROC相比最佳无训练基线提升+7到+38。作为重排置信度分数，它将开源8B通用模型的零样本COCO检测AP从20.4提升至37.0，几乎翻倍，缩小了与有监督检测器的差距。

Conclusion: MTLA是一种简单、通用且有效的无训练MLLM定位幻觉检测方法，通过利用注意力机制中蕴含的定位信号，显著提升了MLLM在多种模态下定位预测的可靠性，且无需任何任务特定训练。

Classification reason: 该论文提出MTLA方法，通过注意力机制检测MLLM在目标检测、视频/音频时间定位中的幻觉区域，属于多模态幻觉缓解与可靠性提升方向，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态幻觉与可靠性"。

Importance reason: Addresses MLLM hallucination with a training-free attention method, but is not aligned with user's priority on continuous models, generation, or world models. Signals: Training-free confidence scoring, Multi-token localized attention, MLLM grounding hallucination, Zero-shot detection improvement, Applies to image, video, audio

Abstract: Multimodal large language models can emit localized predictions, bounding boxes for objects and temporal windows for video and audio events, but they hallucinate these regions prolifically. The model's own token log-probabilities are nearly uninformative: they conflate grounding quality with input ambiguity, and coordinate tokens become near-deterministic once the model commits. We propose Multi-Token Localized Attention (MTLA): a training-free, post-hoc score that measures how strongly a prediction's tokens attend to the region they claim. Prior attention-based detectors, which sum attention over the entire input modality and read a single response token, are weaker special cases; we show that summing only within the claimed region and aggregating across all prediction tokens recovers a stronger grounding signal. The same recipe applies almost trivially to other modalities and tasks: object detection in images and temporal localization in video and audio. Across multiple MLLM families and three modalities, MTLA improves hallucination AUROC by +7 to +38 over the best prior training-free baseline. Used as a confidence score for re-ranking, it nearly doubles the zero-shot COCO detection AP of an open-source 8B generalist (from 20.4 to 37.0), narrowing the gap to supervised detectors without any task-specific training.

</details>

### [56] [More Convincing, Not More Correct: Self-Play Reward Hacking of Reference-Free LLM Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05904)
*Chenyu Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 高优先级 (91.86) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文揭示了自对弈（self-play）和自奖励（self-rewarding）范式中一个结构性缺陷：LLM裁判基于似真性（plausibility）而非正确性（correctness）评分，导致策略模型可以学习利用假阳性区域进行奖励黑客。通过隐藏锚点审计（hidden-anchor audit）在GSM8K上验证，自对弈使裁判通过率从0.72提升至0.94，但真实准确率仅0.20。该问题跨模型家族和规模迁移，三裁判集成仍接受55%错误。关键解决方案是让裁判在评估前先给出自己的答案（committing first），可将假阳性率从0.719降至0.012。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自奖励、自对弈和LLM-as-a-judge等范式假设模型对候选答案的判断能反映正确性。本文旨在揭示这一假设的结构性失败：裁判评分的是似真性而非正确性，从而产生策略可利用的假阳性区域。

Method: 提出隐藏锚点审计（hidden-anchor audit）方法，使用跨来源精确匹配检查作为裁判从未见过的真实正确性基准。在GSM8K上使用Qwen3策略进行自对弈训练实验，并跨Qwen、Llama、Gemma等裁判家族验证。提出"先承诺后评估"（committing first）的解决方案，即裁判在查看候选答案前先给出自己的答案。

Result: 自对弈使裁判通过率从0.72提升至0.94，但真实准确率仅0.20（三颗种子）。错误跨裁判家族和规模迁移，三裁判集成仍接受55%错误。似真性评分防御无法消除假阳性区域。先承诺策略将假阳性率从0.719降至0.012，盲解将判别力提升至0.96，作为训练奖励时假阳性保持为零。

Conclusion: 自对弈奖励黑客是参考自由裁判范式的结构性缺陷，源于裁判评分似真性而非正确性。先承诺策略可以有效预防而非仅检测该问题。论文还给出了一个可证伪的界（差距最多为1-准确率）来预测哪些场景存在风险，并在代码和竞赛数学任务以及Gemma策略上验证了结果的普适性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究自对弈奖励黑客问题，即LLM作为裁判时基于似真性而非正确性评分，导致奖励信号被利用。这属于多模态/语言模型的对齐与奖励学习问题，映射到[多模态强化学习与对齐]（multimodal_rl_alignment）这一规范主题。

Importance reason: Reveals a fundamental flaw in self-play reward hacking for LLM judges, with a simple fix that directly impacts alignment in multimodal models. Signals: self-play reward hacking, reference-free judge flaw, hidden-anchor audit, committing-first fix, cross-model transfer

Abstract: Training a language model against its own reference-free judgments (the premise of self-rewarding, self-play, and LLM-as-a-judge pipelines) assumes a model's verdict on a shown answer tracks correctness. We show it fails structurally: conditioned on a candidate, a judge scores plausibility, not correctness, leaving false-positive basins a policy learns to exploit. We measure this with a hidden-anchor audit: a held-out, cross-source exact-match check the judge never sees. On GSM8K with Qwen3 policies, self-play drives the judge's pass rate from 0.72 to 0.94 while true accuracy stays at 0.20 (three seeds). This reward hacking is not white-box gaming: the errors transfer across judge families (Qwen, Llama, Gemma) and scales, a strict three-judge ensemble still accepts 55% of them, and no plausibility-scoring defense closes the basin. The decisive variable is whether the judge commits an answer of its own before using the candidate: committing first drops the false-positive rate from 0.719 to 0.012, blind solving lifts discrimination to 0.96, and used as the training reward the de-anchored channel keeps false positives at zero, preventing the basin rather than only detecting it. A falsifiable bound (the gap is at most 1 - accuracy) predicts which regimes are exposed. The full arc replicates without training under best-of-N selection in code and competition math, and with a Gemma policy.

</details>

*Akshay Arora, Ishan Nigam, Ashutosh Aggarwal, Shefali Bansal, Krishna Singh, Sweta Kumari, Nikhil Mittal, Shariq Farhan, Siddarth Malreddy*

<details>

Conclusion: AgenticAI-Supervisor 为 LLM agent 提供了可扩展的 RL 训练与评测平台，未来将支持 Computer Use、Tool Use、自动化边缘用例生成等高级功能。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 AgenticAI-Supervisor，一个基于 API 和 UI 的 RL Gym 环境，用于 LLM agent 的多步决策训练与评估，涉及奖励塑造、状态验证等强化学习技术。这与 [CANONICAL] 多模态强化学习与对齐 (multimodal_rl_alignment) 主题高度相关，因为该主题涵盖多模态 RL、RLHF、偏好学习等方向，而本文的 agentic RL 环境正是该方向的典型工作。

Importance reason: Focuses on agentic RL infrastructure rather than core multimodal generation or continuous representation priorities. Signals: Agentic RL platform, reward shaping for LLM agents, decouples environment from execution, customer support case study, no direct connection to continuous language/multimodal models or video generation

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) evolve into autonomous agents, traditional static evaluation fails to capture multi-step decision-making. We introduce AgenticAI-Supervisor, an API and UI-driven RL Gym environment that decouples environment creation from scalable execution. By moving to verifiable execution outcomes, the platform generates high-fidelity traces and applies multi-dimensional reward shaping. Critically, our framework mitigates reward hacking through rigorous internal state validation and testing. This work provides a first look at our platform's core capabilities through a Customer Support Agent case study demonstrating a consistent closed-loop feedback for model optimization. Future work will focus on advanced features such as Computer Use, Tool Use, automated "stumping", and edge-case generation.

</details>


### [58] [SAMPLe: SAM-based Optimizer for Prompt Learning in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05727)
*Hossein Rajoli, Fatemeh Lotfi, Niloufar Alipour Talemi, Hossein Kashiani, Xiaolong Ma, Fatemeh Afghah*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 值得关注 (79.09) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SAMPLe 是一种即插即用的锐度感知优化器，通过考虑损失景观的锐度来增强 VLM 中 prompt learning 的泛化能力，在多个 prompt learning 框架上一致优于现有优化器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM 中的 prompt learning 面临性能-泛化困境：在已见分布上微调 prompt 可获得高准确率，但往往损害对未见数据的泛化能力。现有方法缺乏对损失景观锐度的考虑，导致过拟合。

Method: 提出 SAMPLe（Sharpness-Aware Minimization Prompt Learning），一种基于锐度感知最小化的优化器。它在每一步优化中通过考虑局部曲率和梯度特性来平衡探索与利用，动态适应当前优化状态，从而减少对已见分布的过拟合并提升对未见数据的适应性。

Result: 将 SAMPLe 集成到 CoOp、CoCoOp、MaPLe、TCP、Co-Prompt 等多个 prompt learning 框架中，实验表明 SAMPLe 在多种设置下一致优于现有优化器，显著提升了 prompt 的泛化能力。

Conclusion: SAMPLe 是一种鲁棒且模型无关的 prompt learning 优化方案，有效缓解了 VLM prompt learning 中的性能-泛化困境，具有良好的通用性和实用性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究VLM中prompt learning的泛化性问题，提出基于锐度感知最小化（SAM）的优化器来缓解过拟合，属于多模态模型训练中对齐与优化方法。映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态强化学习与对齐"（multimodal_rl_alignment），因为该子主题涵盖多模态模型的对齐、偏好学习与训练优化方法。

Importance reason: Focuses on VLM prompt learning optimization, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: Prompt learning, Sharpness-aware minimization, VLM generalization, Plug-in optimizer, CoOp/CoCoOp/MaPLe/TCP/Co-Prompt

Abstract: Pre-trained Vision-Language Models (VLMs) like CLIP have proven highly effective as foundation models for various downstream applications. However, prompt learning in VLMs encounters a performance-generalization dilemma: while prompts can be tuned to achieve high accuracy on seen distributions, this tuning process often undermines their generalizability to unseen data. The limited set of learnable prompts, which contextualize and condition the input to steer it toward the task within the pretrained VLM, tends to overfit the training data, leading to a trade-off between task-specific performance and preserving generalization. To address this dilemma, we introduce SAMPLe (Sharpness-Aware Minimization Prompt Learning), a plug-in sharpness-aware optimizer that enhances prompt generalizability by accounting for loss landscape sharpness. Unlike conventional methods, SAMPLe balances exploration and exploitation by satisfying objective function constraints at each step, dynamically adapting to the current optimization state based on the local curvature and gradient properties. This approach reduces overfitting on seen distributions and improves adaptability to unseen data, preserving the generalization potential of pre-trained VLM models. We integrate SAMPLe into multiple prompt learning frameworks, including CoOp, CoCoOp, MaPLe, TCP, and Co-Prompt, demonstrating its effectiveness across diverse methods. Experiments show that SAMPLe elevates prompt learning frameworks and consistently outperforms existing optimizers across diverse settings, establishing itself as a robust, model-agnostic solution for prompt learning.

</details>

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 高优先级 (86.93) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出VAORA（Visual Action Outcome Reasoning Alignment），一种新颖的奖励设计方法，通过视觉对齐奖励和视觉-动作对齐奖励来抑制VLM在交互式物理推理中的幻觉CoT，并缩小推理与行为之间的差距，在PHYRE和Virtual Tool基准上实现了对未见任务和未见环境的泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM在交互式物理推理中面临两个关键失败模式：一是与物理现实矛盾的幻觉CoT推理，二是模型推理与实际行动之间的错位。现有方法无法同时解决这两个问题，导致模型在未见任务和未见环境下泛化能力不足。

Method: VAORA引入两种互补奖励：1) 视觉对齐奖励（Visual Alignment Reward），将VLM推理锚定到视觉上下文，独立于智能体动作本身；2) 视觉-动作对齐奖励（Visual-Action Alignment Reward），将推理基于模型动作所引发的视觉结果。两者共同抑制幻觉CoT并缩小推理与行为差距。为提升训练稳定性，使用预训练的领域内专家智能体估计成功概率，提供平滑密集奖励。

Result: 在PHYRE和Virtual Tool基准上，VAORA在未见任务和未见环境设置下均取得了优于基线方法的性能，验证了通过VAORA可以诱导出基于视觉的、可泛化的物理智能。

Conclusion: VAORA通过将VLM推理锚定到视觉上下文和动作引发的视觉结果，有效解决了物理推理中的幻觉和推理-行为错位问题，为构建可泛化的物理智能提供了新的奖励设计范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出VAORA奖励设计方法，通过视觉对齐奖励和视觉-动作对齐奖励来对齐VLM的推理与物理动作，属于多模态强化学习与对齐（RLHF/偏好学习）范畴，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态强化学习与对齐"（multimodal_rl_alignment）。同时论文涉及物理世界推理与动作条件生成，也与世界模型方向相关。

Importance reason: Addresses VLM hallucination in physical reasoning via novel reward design, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: VLM reasoning-action gap, visual alignment reward, physical reasoning generalization, PHYRE benchmark, hallucinated CoT suppression

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) struggle to generalize in interactive physical reasoning, particularly under unseen tasks and environments. Two key failure modes are prominent: hallucinated chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning that contradicts physical reality, and misalignment between the model's reasoning and actions. We present VAORA (Visual Action Outcome Reasoning Alignment), a novel reward design that directly addresses both issues. VAORA introduces two complementary rewards: Visual Alignment Reward, which anchors VLM reasoning to the visual context independent of the agent action itself, and Visual-Action Alignment Reward, which grounds reasoning in the visual outcome induced by the model's action. Together, these rewards suppress hallucinated CoT and reduce the gap between reasoning and behavior. To improve training stability, we further employ smooth, dense rewards by estimating success probabilities using a pre-trained in-domain expert agent. Experiments on PHYRE and Virtual Tool support our performances across novel-task and unseen-environment settings, confirming that grounded and generalizable physical intelligence can be induced through VAORA.

</details>

### [60] [Scene Graph Thinking: Reinforcing Structured Visual Reasoning for Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05716)
*Zhiwei Yang, Yuanchen Wu, Nan Zhang, Yucong Meng, Ke Yan, Shouhong Ding*

Main category: cs.CV
