TL;DR: 本文提出 Scene Graph Thinking (SaGe) 范式，通过自动化数据引擎将图像-文本数据转换为结构化场景图，构建 120K 高质量训练数据，并采用两阶段图对齐后训练（SFT + 强化微调），显著提升 MLLM 在细粒度感知与推理任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 主要关注孤立物体，忽略了结构化关系对高效目标导航的作用，在视觉密集型任务上表现受限。

Method: 1) 自动化数据引擎将平面图像-文本语料转换为结构化场景图（层次化实体为节点，视觉关系为边）；2) 从场景图中采样推理轨迹构建 120K 高质量训练数据；3) 两阶段图对齐后训练：SFT 内化结构化推理能力，强化微调使用节点代理图奖励来巩固高效图探索。

Result: 在八个多模态基准上取得显著改进，在细粒度感知与推理任务上表现优异。

Conclusion: 场景图结构推理能有效增强 MLLM 的细粒度视觉推理能力，图对齐的后训练范式是提升结构化推理的关键。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Scene Graph Thinking (SaGe) 范式，通过场景图结构增强 MLLM 的细粒度结构化视觉推理能力，属于多模态推理方向，与 [CANONICAL] 多模态推理与思维链 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Structured scene graph reasoning for MLLMs is solid but not aligned with your priority subtopics on continuous models, video generation, or world models. Signals: multimodal understanding generation, scene graph reasoning, reinforcement fine-tuning, fine-grained perception, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated strong perception and reasoning capabilities. However, most existing models focus on isolated objects and neglect structured relationships for efficient target navigation, limiting their performance on visually intensive tasks. To address this challenge, we introduce Scene Graph Thinking (SaGe), a novel paradigm that enables fine-grained and structured visual reasoning through explicit scene-graph representations. Specifically, we first introduce an automated data engine that converts flat image-text corpora into structured scene graphs, where hierarchical entities constitute the nodes and diverse visual relations define the edges. Building upon this, we construct 120K high-quality training data by sampling reasoning traces from scene graphs. Then, two-stage graph-aligned post-training paradigms are introduced, where supervised fine-tuning internalizes MLLMs with structured reasoning, and subsequent reinforcement fine-tuning proposes node-as-proxy graph rewards to consolidate efficient graph exploration. With curated data and graph-aligned training, our approach achieves significant improvements across eight multimodal benchmarks, demonstrating strong effectiveness on fine-grained perception and reasoning tasks. Code is available at .

</details>


### [61] [Segmentation before Answering: Pixel Grounding for MLLM Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05798)
*Yake Wei, Yuan Wang, Fengyun Rao, Jing Lyu, Di Hu*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 高优先级 (80.98) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SegAnswer 提出"先分割再回答"范式，用像素级分割掩码替代边界框来隔离目标区域，为 MLLM 提供更精确的视觉输入，从而提升高分辨率感知、通用感知和幻觉缓解等任务的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 在视觉推理中通常使用边界框来放大感兴趣区域，但边界框会包含冗余背景和干扰物体，影响推理精度。作者希望用更精细的像素级分割掩码替代边界框，以获取更纯净的目标区域输入。

Method: SegAnswer 将分割掩码应用于视觉输入，通过像素级掩码隔离目标区域，去除背景和干扰物。分割后的视觉输入以离散 patch 形式与 MLLM 的位置编码对齐，实现更精确的视觉 token 表示。

Result: 在多个基准上（高分辨率感知、通用感知、幻觉评测）取得一致改进，同时在分割任务上也表现出色，验证了像素级定位的可靠性。

Conclusion: 像素级分割掩码比边界框更适合作为 MLLM 视觉推理中"放大"感兴趣区域的基本单元，能有效过滤冗余信息，提升推理质量和定位能力。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 SegAnswer，通过像素级分割掩码增强 MLLM 的视觉推理能力，属于多模态推理（visual reasoning）范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 多模态推理与思维链 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Proposes pixel-level grounding for MLLM visual reasoning, improving perception and hallucination mitigation. Signals: pixel grounding, MLLM visual reasoning, segmentation before answering, high-resolution perception, hallucination mitigation

Abstract: Recent advancements in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have evolved from static perception to interleaved visual-language reasoning, often referred to as ``thinking with images''. A basic operation in this reasoning process is to zoom in on regions of interest (often represented with bounding boxes) to acquire finer visual details. In this paper, we propose \textbf{Seg}mentation before \textbf{Answer}ing (SegAnswer), which shifts the unit of zoom-in from the popular bounding box to pixel-level segmentation mask. By employing fine-grained masks to isolate the target area from cluttered environments, segmented visual input yields a more precise region of interest, effectively filtering out redundant background and interfering objects. Furthermore, the discrete patches of segmented visual input align more seamlessly with how MLLMs structure visual tokens via positional embeddings. In experiments, we evaluate SegAnswer across diverse benchmarks, including high-resolution perception, general perception, and hallucination. It achieves consistent improvements and also exhibits considerable performance on segmentation tasks, validating its capability for reliable pixel grounding.

</details>


### [62] [AVA-VLM: Adaptive Visual Attention-Vision Language Model for In-the-Wild Construction Site Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05859)
*Younggun Kim, Taeheon Kim, Youngseo Kim, Seunghee Park*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: AVA-VLM 提出了一种自适应视觉注意力机制，通过粗到细的推理策略（先低分辨率全局推理，再按需请求高分辨率局部裁剪）来提升建筑工地监控中 VLM 的可靠性、远距离识别能力和推理效率，并引入了区域感知的 Chain-of-Thought 数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有面向建筑工地的 VLM 通常直接对单张全局图像进行 QA 微调，在远距离、低分辨率等实际部署场景中可靠性不足且推理效率低下。受人类检查员先全局观察再放大细节的启发，作者希望让 VLM 具备自适应地选择何时需要高分辨率局部细节的能力。

Method: AVA-VLM 采用粗到细的两阶段推理：首先在低分辨率全局图像上进行推理，当模型判断需要细节检查时，自适应地请求高分辨率局部裁剪区域。同时引入区域感知的 Chain-of-Thought 数据集，教会模型何时需要检查、裁剪哪里以及如何利用局部证据进行判断。

Result: 实验表明，AVA-VLM 在远距离和低分辨率条件下显著提升了可靠性，同时大幅减少了视觉 token 的使用量，提高了推理效率。

Conclusion: AVA-VLM 通过模拟人类粗到细的检查策略，有效提升了 VLM 在建筑工地监控这一实际场景中的鲁棒性和效率，验证了自适应视觉注意力机制在 in-the-wild 部署中的价值。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一种粗到细的视觉推理策略，包含区域感知的 Chain-of-Thought 数据集，属于多模态推理与思维链（CoT）范畴，因此映射到 canonical 子主题 multimodal_reasoning_cot。

Importance reason: Niche construction-site monitoring application with limited transfer to core continuous multimodal or video generation priorities. Signals: Coarse-to-fine visual attention, region-aware CoT dataset, construction domain specific, no continuous token or flow matching, no video generation

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are promising for construction-site monitoring, and recent construction-tailored VLMs have primarily adapted pretrained VLMs through direct QA-style fine-tuning from a single global image. We argue that this direct paradigm remains limited for in-the-wild deployment in terms of operational range, reliability under reduced-resolution inputs, and inference efficiency. To address these challenges, we propose AVA-VLM, an Adaptive Visual Attention-Vision Language Model that follows a human-inspired coarse-to-fine reasoning strategy. AVA-VLM first reasons over a low-resolution global image and selectively requests a high-resolution local crop only when detailed inspection is needed, similar to how a human inspector zooms in on hard-to-see yet important areas. We further introduce a region-aware Chain-of-Thought dataset that teaches the model when to inspect, where to crop, and how to use local evidence. Experiments show that AVA-VLM improves reliability under long-distance and reduced-resolution conditions while substantially reducing visual-token usage.

</details>

### [63] [Narrative World Model: Narratology-Grounded Writer Memory for Long-Form Fiction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05577)
*Mohammad Saifullah, Thomas Kornmaier, Taaha Kazi, Vasu Sharma, Aditya Sanjiv Kanade, Aanand Kumar Yadav*

Main category: cs.IR, cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 值得关注 (79.71) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出叙事世界模型（NWM），一种面向长篇小说写作的作家记忆系统。NWM 结合叙事学驱动的类型化时序状态图与查询条件混合检索，在长篇小说的多跳叙事问答任务上显著优于 Graphiti/Zep、GraphRAG 等基线方法，证明了叙事学结构化表征对复杂故事状态检索的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长篇小说作家需要能够回答关于故事状态演变的多跳问题（如谁在何时知道某个秘密、事件是否先于揭示它的叙述发生等），而通用检索系统和智能体记忆系统仅表示实体和事实，缺乏叙事学结构，无法正确回答这类问题。

Method: NWM 构建一个叙事学驱动的类型化时序状态图（typed temporal-state graph），将故事中的实体、关系、事件按叙事学类型（如秘密、揭示、铺垫、回报、关系变化等）进行结构化表示，并配合查询条件混合检索（query-conditioned hybrid retrieval）来检索相关证据。所有系统通过一个固定的 Opus 4.8 阅读器仅基于各系统提供的章节级证据进行回答，以公平衡量记忆系统本身的质量。

Result: 在可复现的公开语料库和经过验证的多跳基准上，NWM 在多跳叙事 QA 任务上显著优于最强的现有时序知识图谱智能体记忆框架 Graphiti/Zep，且大幅超过 GraphRAG 和平面检索。优势源于叙事学结构化表征而非抽取质量差异——即使将 NWM 的抽取器替换给基线，优势依然保持。

Conclusion: 叙事学驱动的结构化记忆系统对于长篇小说写作中的复杂故事状态检索至关重要，NWM 证明了叙事学理论可以显著提升多跳叙事问答的性能，为 AI 辅助写作中的记忆系统设计提供了新方向。

Classification reason: 该论文构建叙事世界模型（NWM），通过叙事学驱动的时序状态图与查询条件混合检索来支持长篇小说写作中的多跳问答。核心是检索增强的记忆系统，属于多模态/文本检索范畴，映射到 canonical 子主题"多模态检索"（multimodal_retrieval）。

Importance reason: Strong narratology-grounded memory for fiction, but outside user's core priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: narrative world model, long-form fiction, multi-hop QA, temporal knowledge graph, narratology

Abstract: Long-form fiction writers need memory that answers multi-hop questions about evolving story state: who knows a secret and when they learned it, whether an event preceded the narration that revealed it, whether a setup paid off, and how a relationship shifted. General-purpose retrieval and agent-memory systems represent entities and facts but not the narratological structure these questions turn on, so they surface the wrong evidence or none at all. We introduce the Narrative World Model (NWM), a writer-memory system that pairs a narratology-grounded typed temporal-state graph with query-conditioned hybrid retrieval. To measure memory rather than the answerer, we read every system through a single held-constant Opus 4.8 reader over only that system's chapter-safe evidence, on a reproducible public corpus and a validated multi-hop benchmark, and we compare against the strongest existing temporal-knowledge-graph agent-memory framework, Graphiti/Zep (Rasmussen et al., 2025). NWM substantially and significantly outperforms this baseline on multi-hop narratological QA across both corpora, and far exceeds GraphRAG and flat retrieval. The advantage is representational rather than an artifact of extraction: it survives rebuilding the baseline with NWM's own extractor, and traces to its narratology-grounded structure and query-conditioned retrieval, not to graph size or extractor quality.

</details>


### [64] [CMDR: Contextual Multimodal Document Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05927)
*Ryota Tanaka, Taku Hasegawa, Kyosuke Nishida*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.IR, cs.CL, cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CMDR（上下文多模态文档检索）任务及CMDR-Bench基准，针对现有方法忽略文档上下文信息的问题，提出CMDR-Embed框架，通过联合编码多页面并利用共享上下文表示生成页面级嵌入，结合上下文多模态对比学习目标CMCL进行训练，显著优于非上下文嵌入方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态文档检索基准主要评估简单的词汇或语义匹配，且大多数方法独立编码页面，忽略了跨页面的上下文信息，无法处理需要聚合多页信息的查询。

Method: 提出CMDR-Embed上下文多模态嵌入框架，通过联合编码多个页面并从共享上下文表示中推导页面级嵌入，显式融入文档上下文；同时提出CMCL（上下文多模态对比学习）目标，平衡上下文建模与页面级判别性。

Result: 实验表明CMDR-Embed显著优于非上下文嵌入方法，验证了上下文感知多模态嵌入对文档检索的重要性。

Conclusion: 上下文感知的多模态嵌入对于推进文档检索至关重要，CMDR任务和CMDR-Embed框架为多模态文档检索提供了新的方向和方法。

Classification reason: 该论文提出多模态文档检索任务CMDR及上下文多模态嵌入框架CMDR-Embed，属于多模态检索范畴。现有[CANONICAL] subtopic "multimodal_retrieval（多模态检索）" 完全匹配，直接复用。

Importance reason: Focuses on document retrieval, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Multimodal document retrieval, context-aware embeddings, contrastive learning, benchmark creation, not aligned with user's core interests

Abstract: Multimodal document retrieval aims to retrieve relevant pages while preserving both textual and visual content from the original document. However, existing benchmarks primarily evaluate simple lexical or semantic matching, and most methods encode pages independently. Consequently, they overlook the contextual information in the document required to resolve queries that aggregate information across multiple pages. In this paper, we introduce CMDR and CMDR-Bench, a new multimodal document retrieval task and benchmark that require modeling document context. To address this challenge, we propose CMDR-Embed, a contextual multimodal embedding framework that explicitly incorporates document context by jointly encoding multiple pages and deriving page-level embeddings from a shared contextual representation. Furthermore, we introduce CMCL, a contextual multimodal contrastive learning objective that effectively trains CMDR-Embed by balancing contextual modeling with page-level discriminability. Experiments demonstrate that CMDR-Embed significantly outperforms non-contextual embeddings, highlighting the importance of context-aware multimodal embeddings for advancing document retrieval.

</details>


### [65] [Modality Relevance is not Modality Utility: Post-hoc Selective Modality Escalation for Cost-Aware Multimodal RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05438)
*Xue Li, Yiming Gai*

Main category: cs.IR, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 普通相关 (49.29) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出事后选择性模态升级（post-hoc selective modality escalation）策略用于多模态RAG：先使用廉价文本+表格管道生成答案，再通过验证器判断是否需要调用昂贵的VLM视觉证据，从而在保持准确率的同时大幅减少视觉调用次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态RAG系统在检索前就二选一地决定使用文本管道还是VLM管道，但模态相关性与模态效用并不等价——许多包含图像证据的问题仅凭文本和表格也能正确回答。预检索路由会过度升级，造成不必要的视觉计算开销。

Method: 提出事后选择性模态升级框架：1) 先用廉价文本+表格管道生成草稿答案；2) 设计验证器在(query, draft answer, evidence)三元组上定位缺失的模态；3) 通过校准的价值升级路由器（value-of-escalation router）判断预期准确率提升是否值得视觉成本，决定是否调用VLM。

Result: 在MultiModalQA数据集上，该方法恢复了始终使用VLM管道的准确率，同时大幅减少了视觉调用次数，并缩小了与Oracle升级率之间的大部分差距。

Conclusion: 模态相关性与模态效用不等价，事后选择性升级比预检索路由更优。该工作将路由信号层次从检索深度和推理跳数扩展到模态维度，统一在成本感知的选择性升级框架下。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多模态RAG中的模态选择与路由策略，属于多模态检索增强生成范畴，与现有[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态检索"（multimodal_retrieval）高度相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on cost-aware multimodal RAG routing, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Multimodal RAG, cost-aware routing, post-hoc verification, modality utility vs relevance, oracle headroom analysis

Abstract: Multimodal retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) grounds a generator in evidence drawn from heterogeneous modalities -- text, tables, and images. The dominant deployment choice is binary and made before the model has tried to answer: either run a cheap text(+table) pipeline, or pay for an expensive vision-language model (VLM) over every image. Recent adaptive systems improve on this by selecting the modality or fidelity pre-retrieval, from a question-conditioned predictor of which modality will be needed. We show that this is the wrong decision point. Through an oracle headroom analysis on MultiModalQA, we find that the relevance of a modality to a question is a weak predictor of whether that modality is actually needed to answer correctly: a large fraction of questions whose gold support includes an image are nonetheless answerable from text and tables alone, and a pre-retrieval router that escalates on apparent visual relevance over-escalates substantially relative to an oracle. We propose \textbf{post-hoc selective modality escalation}: answer cheaply from text and tables, run a verifier on the (query, draft answer, evidence) tuple that localizes which modality is missing, and pay for VLM evidence only there. A calibrated value-of-escalation router then decides whether the expected accuracy gain justifies the visual cost. On MultiModalQA, our router recovers the accuracy of an always-on VLM pipeline while issuing far fewer visual calls, and closes most of the gap to the oracle escalation rate. The result extends a routing-signal hierarchy established for retrieval depth and reasoning hops to a third axis -- modality -- under a single cost-aware selective-escalation view.

</details>

### [66] [WebRetriever: A Large-Scale Comprehensive Benchmark for Efficient Web Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06118)
*Wei Dong, Tianyu Fu, Zhe Yu, Hanning Wang, Anyang Su, Zhizhou Fang, Yuyang Chen, Shuo Wang, Minghui Wu, Ping Jiang, Zhen Lei, Chenxu Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.MM

TL;DR: WebRetriever是一个大规模Web Agent评测基准，涵盖800个网站和1550个任务，覆盖消费、专业和企业领域。提出了NavEval评估框架，利用丰富的交互上下文（超越视觉截图）实现与人类判断的最佳对齐。建立了三种互补评估协议：导航能力、知识辅助交互、端到端任务完成与信息提取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Web Agent评测基准存在三个关键局限：规模不足且领域多样性有限，难以评估跨域泛化能力；LLM-as-Judge评估方法无法捕捉细粒度交互语义（特别是查询表述和过滤操作）；现有基准过度强调导航成功率而忽视实际部署场景的关键需求。

Method: 构建了包含800个网站和1550个任务的大规模基准WebRetriever，覆盖消费、专业和企业领域。提出NavEval（Navigation Evaluation）框架，利用丰富的交互上下文（包括DOM结构、操作序列等）超越视觉截图进行LLM-as-Judge评估。设计了三种互补评估协议：导航能力评估、知识辅助交互评估、端到端任务完成与信息提取评估。

Result: NavEval在多个评估数据集上实现了与人类判断的最佳对齐。实验揭示了不同评估协议下性能的显著差异，证明仅靠导航成功率不足以预测实际应用效果。WebRetriever提供了细粒度的Agent能力诊断。

Conclusion: WebRetriever通过大规模、多领域覆盖和细粒度评估协议，为Web Agent研究提供了更全面的评测基础，揭示了导航成功与实际应用效果之间的差距，推动了Web Agent评估框架的发展。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了WebRetriever，一个大规模Web Agent评测基准，包含800个网站和1550个任务，并提出了NavEval评估框架。这属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）范畴，是[CANONICAL]子主题，直接匹配。

Importance reason: Strong benchmark for web agents but outside user's core focus on continuous multimodal models and generation. Signals: Large-scale web agent benchmark, NavEval evaluation framework, Multimodal understanding evaluation, No direct connection to continuous language models or video/image generation, Lacks priority subtopic keywords

Abstract: As web agents increasingly demonstrate capabilities in automated task execution, the development of robust evaluation frameworks for assessing their navigation and task completion performance has emerged as a critical research priority. However, existing benchmarks exhibit fundamental limitations. First, they suffer from insufficient scale and limited domain diversity, constraining comprehensive evaluation of cross-domain generalization. Second, prevailing LLM-as-Judge evaluation methodologies inadequately capture fine-grained interaction semantics, particularly regarding precise query formulation and filtering operations. Third, current benchmarks predominantly emphasize navigation success metrics while neglecting critical requirements for real-world deployment scenarios. To address these limitations, we introduce WebRetriever, a large-scale benchmark encompassing 800 websites and 1,550 tasks across diverse domains, including consumer, professional, and enterprise sectors, with comprehensive coverage of user intent patterns. We propose NavEval (Navigation Evaluation), a novel LLM-as-Judge framework that leverages rich interaction context beyond visual screenshots, achieving state-of-the-art alignment with human judgment across multiple evaluation datasets. Furthermore, we establish three complementary evaluation protocols that collectively provide holistic assessment of web agent capabilities: navigation proficiency, knowledge-assisted interaction, and end-to-end task completion with information extraction. Extensive experimental analysis reveals substantial performance disparities across evaluation protocols, demonstrating that navigation success alone is an insufficient predictor of real-world application effectiveness. WebRetriever delivers fine-grained diagnostic insights into agent capabilities and establishes a rigorous foundation for advancing web agent research and development.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出了 BaFCo，一个面向孟加拉语复杂表单理解的基准数据集，包含 200 份多页政府表单，定义了 26 种细粒度实体类型和 5 种粗粒度实体类型，并评测了多个主流 MLLM 在零样本和思维链提示下的表现，揭示了当前模型在低资源语言表单理解上的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在真实世界文档理解任务中日益重要，但对于孟加拉语等低资源语言，由于缺乏高质量标注数据，其应用受到严重限制。现有基准大多面向英语等高资源语言，无法评估和推动低资源语言场景下的文档理解能力。

Method: 收集了 200 份来自孟加拉国农业、教育、银行、土地管理等多个领域的多页复杂政府表单，定义了 26 种细粒度表单实体类型和 5 种粗粒度实体类型，构建了 BaFCo 基准。使用零样本和思维链提示，在低推理和高推理两种设置下，评估了 ChatGPT、Gemini、Claude、Qwen、Kimi 系列的最新 MLLM。

Result: 实验表明，当前 MLLM 在孟加拉语表单理解方面存在显著局限，尤其是在精确定位细粒度表单实体方面表现不佳。不同模型在不同推理设置下表现差异明显，整体准确率仍有较大提升空间。

Conclusion: BaFCo 填补了孟加拉语表单理解基准的空白，揭示了现有 MLLM 在低资源语言文档理解任务上的不足，为未来研究提供了有价值的评测平台和方向指引。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个孟加拉语表单理解基准数据集 BaFCo，包含文档布局分析（DLA）和关键信息提取（KIE）任务，并评测了多个 MLLM。这属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一 [CANONICAL] 子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on a low-resource language benchmark, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: low-resource language benchmark, document layout analysis, key information extraction, MLLM evaluation, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Document comprehension is a challenging yet impactful task for Multimodal Large Language Models, especially as these systems see growing adoption in real-world, human-centric applications. However, this adoption is limited for low-resource languages such as Bangla due to the scarcity of high-quality annotated data. To address this gap, we introduce BaFCo, a benchmark dataset for Bangla form comprehension with a focus on Document Layout Analysis (DLA) and Key Information Extraction (KIE). BaFCo curates 200 multi-page complex Bangladeshi government forms, sourced from across diverse sectors including agriculture, education, banking, and land management. To accurately capture the structural and contextual complexity of these forms, we define a fine-grained annotation schema comprising 26 types of form entities, along with a separate coarse form entity set consisting of 5 types. We evaluate the latest MLLMs from the ChatGPT, Gemini, Claude, Qwen, and Kimi series using zero-shot and chain-of-thought prompts under both low and high reasoning setups. Our results reveal limitations in current MLLMs' ability in comprehending Bangla forms, particularly in accurately localizing highly granular form entities. Our dataset and code is available at:

*Zhengbo Jiao, Yiming Cheng, Yilei Jiang, Kaituo Feng, Rui Huang, Tianyi Jiang, Juanxi Tian, Jiapeng li, Qunzhong Wang, Tailai Chen, Qianshan Wei, Chuan Xiao, Shanyu Rong, Yangfu Li, Yanhan Zhou, Yunpu Ma, Yifan Zhang, Xiangyu Yue*

TL;DR: SearchEyes 提出以类型化知识图谱为骨干的模拟搜索世界，统一了多模态搜索智能体的训练数据、搜索环境和奖励信号构建。通过感知-知识链（PKC）采样多跳路径并保留跳级元数据，以及跳锚策略优化（HaPO）实现步骤级信用分配，在六个多模态知识密集型基准上达到开源最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态搜索智能体的训练数据、搜索环境和奖励信号三者独立构建，导致结构化元数据被丢弃、环境依赖不可复现的外部引擎、RL奖励在轨迹层面稀疏。需要一种统一框架来弥合这一结构性断裂。

Method: 1) 以类型化知识图谱（Wikidata5M）为骨干构建模拟搜索世界，统一数据、环境和奖励。2) 提出感知-知识链（PKC），在视觉-知识交叉区域采样约束多跳路径，保留跳级实体元数据。3) 提出跳锚策略优化（HaPO），利用PKC中的跳级锚点进行步骤级信用分配，无需单独训练过程奖励模型。

Result: 在六个多模态知识密集型基准上，SearchEyes-27B 相比最强开源基线平均提升6.2分，达到开源多模态搜索智能体的SOTA性能。

Conclusion: SearchEyes 通过模拟搜索世界统一了多模态搜索智能体的三个关键组件，验证了知识图谱驱动的搜索环境设计和步骤级奖励分配的有效性，为多模态深度搜索智能体提供了新的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出多模态搜索智能体 SearchEyes，在多个多模态知识密集型基准上进行评测并达到SOTA，核心贡献涉及多模态搜索的评测与基准，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）。

Importance reason: Strong on multimodal understanding and search, but lacks direct connection to priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: multimodal search agent, typed knowledge graph, multi-hop reasoning, step-level credit assignment, open-source SOTA

Abstract: Training multimodal search agents to perform multi-hop reasoning remains challenging due to a fundamental structural disconnect: existing pipelines construct training data, search environments, and reward signals independently, causing synthesized structural metadata to be discarded, environments to rely on irreproducible external engines, and RL rewards to remain sparse at the trajectory level. We present \textbf{SearchEyes}, which uses a typed knowledge graph as the backbone of a \emph{simulated search world} that unifies all three components. We propose \textbf{Perception-Knowledge Chains (PKC)} to sample constrained multi-hop paths over the visual-knowledge intersection of Wikidata5M, retaining hop-level entity metadata that simultaneously defines a self-contained search world and step-level reward anchors. We further propose \textbf{Hop-Anchored Policy Optimization (HaPO)}, which reuses these anchors for step-level credit assignment without a separately trained process reward model. Experiments on six multimodal knowledge-intensive benchmarks show that SearchEyes achieves state-of-the-art performance among open-source multimodal search agents, with SearchEyes-27B improving over the strongest open-source baseline by 6.2 points on average.%

</details>

TL;DR: PluraMath 将数学推理评测从高资源语言扩展到 18 种低资源语言，通过人工验证的翻译流程构建数据集，并评测了 27 个推理 LLM，揭示了高资源与低资源语言之间的数学推理性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数学推理基准（如 PolyMath）严重偏向英语和中文等高资源语言，低资源语言的数学推理能力评估几乎空白，无法全面反映 LLM 在多语言环境下的真实推理能力。

Method: 基于 PolyMath 数据集，通过人工策划的翻译流水线将数学问题翻译为 18 种低资源语言（涵盖 6 个语系），由母语者验证翻译质量。使用该数据集对 27 个推理 LLM（涵盖小、中、大和闭源模型四个规模）进行多语言数学推理能力基准测试。

Result: 高资源语言与低资源语言之间的数学推理性能存在持续差距；更强的指令跟随能力与更好的多语言推理表现相关；开源了数据集、数据获取流水线和评估框架。

Conclusion: 多语言数学推理评测仍存在显著的语言偏差，PluraMath 为低资源语言的数学推理评估提供了重要资源，有助于推动更公平的多语言 LLM 评测。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 PluraMath，一个面向多语言数学推理评测的基准数据集，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）子主题。虽然论文主要关注文本数学推理而非视觉模态，但数学推理评测是多模态推理评测的重要组成部分，且该子主题涵盖评测基准的构建与评估协议。

Importance reason: Focuses on multilingual math reasoning evaluation, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: Multilingual evaluation, low-resource languages, math reasoning, benchmark extension, not aligned with user's core interests

Abstract: Mathematical reasoning has become a central task for evaluating and tuning reasoning Large Language Models (LLMs), yet existing benchmarks remain heavily biased toward high-resource languages, with English and Chinese dominating both pre-training corpora and evaluation suites. The recently released PolyMath (Wang et al., 2025) dataset represents a significant step forward, yet its coverage is still limited to 18 only high-resource languages. To address this gap, we introduce PluraMath, an extension of PolyMath to 18 additional {underrepresented languages spanning 6 language families -- ranging from mid-resource to extreme low-resource settings. We constructed the dataset through a human-curated pipeline, where native speakers thoroughly validated pre-computed translations. Using PluraMath, we then benchmark 27 reasoning LLMs across four model scales -- small, mid-size, large, and closed-source ensembles -- probing the multilingual mathematical reasoning capabilities of state-of-the-art models under diverse linguistic conditions. Our fine-grained analysis confirms a persistent gap in mathematical reasoning performance between high-resource and underrepresented languages, with stronger results largely associated with better instruction-following ability. We fully open-source our dataset, data acquisition pipeline, and evaluation framework, with the goal of lowering the barrier to multilingual benchmark development for underrepresented communities.

</details>


### [70] [Pluralis v0.1: Towards a Multicultural, Multimodal, Multilingual Benchmark for AI Risk and Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06196)
*Alicia Parrish, Rajat Shinde, Sanket Badhe, Xinyi Bai, Sree Bhargavi Balija, Hua-Rong Chu, Emilio Ferrara, Armstrong Foundjem, Rajat Ghosh, Aakash Gupta, Xuanli He, Ong Chen Hui, Minji Jung, Madhangi Karimanal, Faiza Khan Khattak, Boryoung Kim, Eugenia Kim, Liliya Lavitas, Seok Min Lim, Victor Lu, Jim Moirangthem, Dhivya Nagasubramanian, Deepak Pandita, Sita Rajagopal, Geetha Raju, Evgeniia Razumovskaia, Aravind Reddy, Federico Ricciuti, Nobin Sarwar, Sungpil Shin, Sunayana Sitaram, Snehal Thorat, Tharindu Cyril Weerasooriya, Jasmijn Bastings, Joachim Baumann, Kongtao Chen, Murali Emani, Mariya Hendriksen, Jiho Jin, Jun Seong Kim, Younghoon Ko, Alicja Kwasniewska, Minjae Lee, Tom Wei-cyuan Lin Kashyap Ramanandula Manjusha, Junho Myung, Junyeong Park, Roma Patel, Shyam Ratan, Sudarsun Santhiappan, Priyanka Suresh, Tuesday, Ksheeraj Sai Vepuri Laura Amortegui-Ordonez, Claire Dennis, Minsuk Kahng, Chris Knotz, Alice Oh, Balaraman Ravindran, Soojung Ryu William Bartholomew, Hiwot Tesfaye, Lora Aroyo*

TL;DR: Pluralis v0.1 是一个从文化优先视角构建的多模态、多区域、多语言AI安全与可靠性评测数据集，涵盖6个亚太国家和8种语言的6,448条提示，通过文本与图像的协同作用触发特定法律或文化违规，并提出了Judge-Pluralis评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI安全评估和基准测试框架主要依赖西方中心的文化无关默认设置，忽视了关键的区域法律、社会语言细微差别和文化禁忌，导致视觉语言模型（VLM）在全球部署中存在漏洞。

Method: 从文化优先视角构建多模态数据集Pluralis，涵盖6个亚太国家和8种语言，原生收集本地化安全风险而非改编西方数据集。引入多模态评估范式：用户文本和图像单独看都无害，但协同触发特定法律或文化违规。提出Judge-Pluralis，一个基于LLM-as-a-Judge的集成评估模型，在经验推导的文化分类学示例上训练。

Result: 在Pluralis子集上观察VLM行为，发现了反复出现的、区域特定的失败模式，如图像误识别导致下游危害、遗漏物品-上下文-地区交互、以及不充分的拒绝。这些失败模式在不同地区和语言间系统性地变化，暴露了全局平均指标所掩盖的盲点。

Conclusion: Pluralis不是作为已解决的评估框架呈现，而是作为推动多语言、多文化评估科学发展的第一步和催化剂，呼吁研究社区利用这一基础来更好地支持AI文化对齐的全球部署。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个面向多文化、多区域、多语言的多模态AI安全与可靠性评测基准Pluralis，属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一[CANONICAL]子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on cultural safety evaluation, not on core continuous multimodal or generation models. Signals: Multicultural benchmark, Multimodal safety, VLM evaluation, Cultural alignment, Judge-Pluralis

Abstract: Current AI safety evaluation and benchmarking frameworks predominantly rely on Western-centric culture-agnostic defaults that mask critical regional laws, socio-linguistic nuances, and cultural taboos, leaving Vision-Language Models (VLMs) vulnerable in global deployments. We introduce Pluralis v0.1: a novel multimodal, multi-regional, and multilingual dataset built from a culture-first perspective. Spanning 6,448 prompts across six Asia-Pacific countries (Bangladesh, India, Korea, Pakistan, Singapore, Taiwan) and eight languages, Pluralis diverges from prior work by natively sourcing localized safety hazards rather than adapting Western datasets. Crucially, it introduces a multimodal evaluation paradigm: user text (e.g., "Should I gift this?") and an image referring to "this" (e.g., a clock) - both innocuous in isolation, but synergistically triggering specific legal or cultural violations. Pluralis disentangles universal safety violations from localized cultural appropriateness, establishing the latter as a first-class evaluation axis. To operationalize this, we present Judge-Pluralis, an agreement-gated LLM-as-a-Judge ensemble trained on examples classified in an empirically derived cultural taxonomy. Observing VLM behavior on a subset of the Pluralis surfaces recurring, locale-specific failure modes such as image misidentifications with downstream harm, missed item-context-locale interactions, and inadequate refusals. These failure modes vary systematically across locales and languages, exposing blind spots that globally averaged metrics conceal. Ultimately, Pluralis is not presented as a solved evaluation framework for cultural alignment, but rather as a first step and catalyst for future innovation. We call upon the research community to utilize this foundation to advance the science of multilingual, multicultural evaluation to better support AI cultural alignment globally.

</details>

*So Hasegawa, Shailaja Keyur Sampat, Lei Liu, Wei-Peng Chen*

TL;DR: 本文提出DataGovBench，一个基于政府开放数据的LLM数据分析基准，包含Table QA（复杂可分解问题回答与可视化）和Table Insight（探索性数据分析生成专家级发现）两个任务。实验表明当前LLM在真实世界数据分析场景中仍存在显著性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM数据分析评测基准多聚焦于小表格的事实检索，未能反映真实世界中大规模多表格数据集、外部知识整合和探索性洞察发现等复杂场景，缺乏能衡量LLM实际数据分析能力的评测基准。

Method: 从政府开放数据构建DataGovBench基准，包含两个任务：(1) Table QA——需要解决复杂可分解问题并生成文本答案或可视化；(2) Table Insight——评估模型通过探索性数据分析生成专家级发现的能力。使用多种SOTA LLM（含/不含智能体框架）进行全面实验。

Result: 实验显示当前LLM在Table QA和Table Insight两个任务上均存在显著性能差距，表明现有LLM系统远未满足真实世界数据分析的需求。

Conclusion: DataGovBench为推进LLM在数据分析领域的研究提供了具有挑战性的基准，揭示了当前模型在真实复杂数据分析场景中的不足，为未来研究指明了方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了DataGovBench，一个基于政府开放数据的LLM数据分析评测基准，包含Table QA和Table Insight两个任务。这属于多模态理解与生成方向下的多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）[CANONICAL]子主题，因为该子主题涵盖新基准的构建与评测协议。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM data analysis benchmarking, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: Benchmark for real-world data analysis, Table QA and Table Insight tasks, Government open data, Performance gaps in current LLMs, Not aligned with user's priority subtopics

Abstract: Current benchmarks for evaluating Large Language Models (LLMs) in data analysis often fail to reflect real-world settings. They typically focus on fact retrieval from small tables and overlook the challenges of large multi-tabular datasets, external knowledge integration, and exploratory insight discovery. We introduce DataGovBench, a benchmark derived from governmental open data designed to evaluate LLMs in practical scenarios. The benchmark includes two tasks: Table QA that requires solving complex decomposable questions and producing textual answers or visualizations, and Table Insight that evaluates the ability of models to generate expert-level findings through exploratory data analysis. Comprehensive experiments with state-of-the-art LLMs, both with and without agentic frameworks, reveal significant performance gaps across both tasks. These results suggest that current LLM-based systems remain far from satisfying the demands of real-world data analytics. DataGovBench provides a challenging benchmark for advancing research on LLMs capable of both answering analytical queries and discovering insights from data. Code and sample data are available at .

</details>

*Tianyuan Zhang, Xianglong Liu, Aishan Liu, Lu Wang, Yitong Zhang, Peng Yue, Mingchuan Zhang, Siyuan Liang, Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

TL;DR: 本文提出LanEvil++，首个评估自动驾驶车道感知在环境幻觉（阴影、反射、轮胎痕迹等14种）下鲁棒性的基准。基于CARLA模拟器生成94个高保真3D场景，包含90,292张标注图像、1,596个视频片段和41,855个VQA对。实验表明环境幻觉显著降低车道检测和ADVLM性能，阴影影响最大。提出多模态幻觉防御方法MIDA，提升鲁棒性约4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实驾驶环境中自然存在的环境幻觉（如阴影、反射、轮胎痕迹）会干扰视觉感知，导致场景误判，对自动驾驶系统构成严重安全风险。然而现有研究很大程度上忽视了这些现象，缺乏系统性的评估基准。

Method: 1) 构建LanEvil++基准：定义14种环境幻觉类型，使用CARLA模拟器生成94个高保真可控3D场景，产出90,292张图像、1,596个视频片段和41,855个VQA对。2) 评估传统车道检测（LD）模型和视觉语言模型（ADVLM）在幻觉下的性能。3) 提出多模态幻觉防御方法MIDA，通过多模态融合增强鲁棒性。

Result: LD模型在幻觉下Accuracy平均下降5.27%，F1-score下降10.49%；ADVLM的GPT-score下降2.03%，Language-score下降0.75%。阴影是最具破坏性的幻觉类型，Accuracy下降高达7.20%。闭环仿真显示幻觉可导致错误驾驶决策。MIDA在LD模型上提升鲁棒性4.23%，在ADVLM上提升3.82%。

Conclusion: 环境幻觉对自动驾驶车道感知构成严重威胁，现有模型对此鲁棒性不足。LanEvil++为评估和提升自动驾驶系统在环境幻觉下的鲁棒性提供了系统化基准，MIDA方法展示了多模态防御的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了LanEvil++基准，用于评估自动驾驶车道感知在环境幻觉（阴影、反射等）下的鲁棒性，涉及传统车道检测和VLM-based系统。核心贡献是构建了一个新的多模态评测基准，属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一规范主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on autonomous driving robustness, which is outside your core priority subtopics and lacks direct connection to continuous language/multimodal models or video generation. Signals: autonomous driving, lane perception, environmental illusions, robustness benchmark, multimodal defense

Abstract: Environmental illusions (eg., shadows, reflections, and tire marks) are naturally existing yet overlooked phenomena in real-world driving environments. They can disturb visual perception, leading to misinterpretation of the scene and posing serious safety risks to autonomous driving (AD) systems. However, existing researches largely overlook these phenomena, leaving a critical gap. To address this issue, we study AD robustness through the lane perception perspective, a fundamental task supporting core functions like cruise control and lane centering. We focus on two representative models: conventional lane detection (LD) and vision-language model-based systems (ADVLMs). In this work, we introduce the first benchmark, LanEvil++, for evaluating the robustness of lane perception under environmental illusions. LanEvil++ encompasses 14 types of illusions and leverages the CARLA simulator to generate 94 high-fidelity, fully controllable 3D scenes, yielding a dataset of 90,292 annotated images, 1,596 video clips, and 41,855 visual question answering pairs. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that environmental illusions substantially degrade the performance of state-of-the-art LD methods. On average, LD models experience a 5.27% drop in Accuracy and a 10.49% decline in F1-score, while ADVLMs show a 2.03% reduction in GPT-score and a 0.75% drop in Language-score. Among all illusions, shadows emerge as the most disruptive factor, reducing accuracy by up to 7.20%. Furthermore, closed-loop simulations reveal that these illusions can lead to incorrect driving decisions. Complementary real-world case studies highlight safety-critical failures in actual traffic scenes. To enhance robustness, we propose the Multimodal Illusion Defense Approach (MIDA). MIDA achieves substantial gains under challenging conditions, boosting robustness by 4.23% on LD models and 3.82% on ADVLMs.

</details>


### [73] [HoloCount: A Holistic Visual Counting Benchmark for MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06420)
*Jinhong Deng, Limeng Qiao, Guanglu Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (75.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: HoloCount 是一个针对多模态大语言模型（MLLM）视觉计数能力的全面评测基准，包含三层层次化分类体系（语义计数、分析计数、鲁棒性测试），对20多个SOTA MLLM进行了系统评估，揭示了模型在从感知到复杂推理和对抗场景下的显著性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计数基准主要关注简化场景下的基础感知，无法捕捉逻辑约束或对抗条件下出现的复杂失败模式（如数值幻觉）。为了系统评估MLLM的计数能力并揭示其瓶颈，作者提出了更全面、更具诊断性的基准。

Method: 构建 HoloCount 基准，采用三层层次化分类体系：(1) 语义计数——原子计数和属性计数；(2) 分析计数——空间推理和集合推理的逻辑组合；(3) 鲁棒性测试——高密度场景、语言偏差等对抗性场景。对20多个SOTA MLLM进行系统评估。

Result: 即使是最先进的MLLM，在从感知任务过渡到复杂分析推理和对抗场景时，性能显著下降。揭示了当前MLLM在定量精度方面的关键瓶颈。

Conclusion: HoloCount 系统性地揭示了当前MLLM计数能力的现状，为开发更可靠、更扎实的多模态系统提供了路线图。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 HoloCount，一个针对 MLLM 视觉计数能力的全面评测基准，属于多模态评测与基准方向，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准" (multimodal_benchmark)。

Importance reason: Good diagnostic benchmark for MLLM counting, but not aligned with user's priority on continuous/autoregressive models and world models. Signals: MLLM evaluation, visual counting benchmark, hierarchical taxonomy, reveals perception-to-reasoning gap, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Visual counting is a fundamental pillar of multimodal intelligence, requiring a seamless integration of fine-grained grounding and spatial reasoning. While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved remarkable success in qualitative scene understanding, their quantitative precision remains a significant bottleneck, often characterized by persistent numerical hallucinations. Existing counting benchmarks primarily focus on basic perception in simplified contexts, failing to capture the complex failure modes that emerge under logical constraints or adversarial conditions. To address these limitations, we introduce HoloCount, a holistic and diagnostically rich benchmark structured around a three-level hierarchical taxonomy. HoloCount evaluates MLLMs across: (1) Semantic Counting, focusing on atomic and property-based enumeration; (2) Analytical Counting, assessing logical composition through spatial and set-based reasoning; and (3) Robustness Testing, probing model integrity against adverse scenarios and grounded counter-priors, such as high-density scenes and linguistic biases. Through an exhaustive evaluation of over 20 state-of-the-art MLLMs, we reveal a critical performance gap: even top-tier models degrade significantly as tasks transition from perception to complex analytical reasoning and adverse scenarios. Our findings provide a systematic landscape of current MLLM counting capabilities and offer a roadmap for developing more grounded and reliable multimodal systems. The dataset is available at .

</details>

TL;DR: EgoPolice 是一个基于真实执法记录仪视频的第一视角视频理解基准数据集，包含细粒度的警民交互行为标注，用于评测模型在高运动、密集交互场景下的视频理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频理解数据集缺乏对真实、高动态、高风险的第一视角场景（如执法记录仪视频）的覆盖，而这类场景对警务行为研究和社会治理具有重要意义。作者旨在填补这一空白，提供一个具有挑战性的基准来推动运动鲁棒和上下文感知的第一视角感知研究。

Method: 从公开的执法记录仪视频中筛选并标注了警民交互行为标签（秒级粒度），构建了 EgoPolice 数据集。设计了两个任务：动作分类（多标签分类）和多项选择问答（MCQA）。在多个开源和闭源视频模型（包括 Gemini 2.5 Pro 等）上进行了基准测试。

Result: 即使是最佳的视频模型（如 Gemini 2.5 Pro）在高风险动作（如"武器出鞘"）的预测上仍然表现不佳，表明该基准具有挑战性。论文报告了各模型在分类和 MCQA 任务上的性能对比。

Conclusion: EgoPolice 为第一视角视频理解提供了一个具有挑战性的新基准，揭示了现有模型在真实高动态场景下的局限性，并为大规模执法记录仪视频的自动化分析奠定了基础。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 EgoPolice 数据集，用于第一视角（egocentric）视频理解，包含分类和多选题问答任务，并对多种视频模型进行了评测。这属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一 [CANONICAL] 子主题。

Importance reason: The paper focuses on a specialized egocentric video benchmark for police body-worn cameras, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and lacks alignment with their configured direction weights. Signals: Egocentric video benchmark, Police body-worn camera, High-stakes action recognition, Multimodal understanding evaluation, Low personal relevance to continuous language models or video generation

Abstract: We introduce EgoPolice, a carefully curated dataset of real, egocentric police-civilian interactions, sourced from publicly available body-worn camera videos. We select police-civilian action labels that are critical for police behavioral research and annotate them at a second-by-second granularity. The videos feature rapid and irregular camera motion, dense human interactions, and rare high-stakes events, making the dataset a challenging benchmark for motion-robust and context-aware egocentric perception. We provide two different tasks, classification and multiple-choice question-answering, and benchmark both open-source and closed-source models. We find that even the best video models like Gemini 2.5 Pro still struggle to accurately predict high-risk actions such as "Weapon Out". Beyond serving as a benchmark, EgoPolice provides a foundation for developing models capable of identifying events of interest in large-scale body-worn camera video repositories, enabling more efficient downstream human review.

</details>

### [75] [Multi-Teacher Contrastive Distillation for Edge-Efficient Pathology Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05533)
*Tim Lenz, Maurice Heide, Marco Gustav, Nic G. Reitsam, Jakob Nikolas Kather*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 普通相关 (45.94) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: MuCoDi 是一种多教师对比蒸馏框架，将多个病理基础模型（Virchow2、UNI2、H-Optimus-1）的冻结 tile embedding 蒸馏到轻量级 MobileOne/RepViT 编码器中，在保持接近教师模型性能的同时将模型大小降低数个数量级，并在树莓派 5 上实现高达 605 倍的单 tile 加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 病理基础模型（PFM）在全切片图像分析中表现出色，但其巨大的模型尺寸和推理成本阻碍了在病理科室本地部署。现有蒸馏方法通常只使用单一教师或采用回归损失，未能充分利用多个互补教师的知识。因此，需要一种高效的多教师蒸馏框架，在保持 PFM 级别表征质量的同时实现边缘端部署。

Method: 提出 MuCoDi 框架，采用 MoCo v3 风格的对比蒸馏目标：将多个冻结的 PFM（Virchow2、UNI2、H-Optimus-1）的 tile embedding 缓存作为对比学习中的动量编码器键，训练轻量级学生编码器（MobileOne 和 RepViT）。学生通过对比损失学习与多个教师 embedding 对齐，而非直接回归单个教师特征。在 14.3M TCGA tile（来自 11.8K WSI）上预训练，并在 23 个临床下游分类任务上评估冻结编码器。

Result: RepViT 学生模型 MuCoEdge-R2.3 和 R1.5 达到 71.0% 外部 AUROC，仅比最佳教师 Virchow2（71.8%）低 0.8 个百分点；MuCoEdge-R2.3 获得最佳外部 F1（51.8%）和次优 AUPRC（53.3%）。MuCoEdge-R1.0 仅 6.4M 参数和 1.12 GFLOPs 即达 70.9% AUROC。在树莓派 5 上，百万参数以下的 MobileOne 学生实现高达 605 倍单 tile 加速，同时保持 66.5%-66.9% 外部 AUROC。

Conclusion: MuCoDi 框架证明了通过多教师对比蒸馏，可以将病理基础模型级别的表征质量迁移到轻量级边缘端编码器中，在模型大小和推理速度上实现数个数量级的提升，同时性能损失极小。这为病理 AI 的本地化部署提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出多教师对比蒸馏框架 MuCoDi，将多个病理基础模型（PFM）的知识蒸馏到轻量级边缘端编码器（MobileOne/RepViT），显著降低模型参数量和推理成本。这属于多模态模型的高效化与轻量化方向，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态高效化与轻量化"（efficient_multimodal）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on pathology foundation model distillation, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and negative keywords include medical diagnosis. Signals: Multi-teacher distillation, edge deployment, pathology foundation models, contrastive learning, model compression

Abstract: Computational pathology foundation models (PFMs) have advanced whole-slide image analysis. However, their size and inference cost hinder local deployment in pathology departments. We propose MuCoDi, a pretraining framework that distills frozen tile embeddings from multiple PFMs into compact edge-oriented encoders. Instead of regressing individual teacher features, MuCoDi trains lightweight MobileOne and RepViT students with a contrastive distillation objective adapted from MoCo v3, where cached Virchow2, UNI2, and H-Optimus-1 embeddings replace momentum-encoder keys. We pretrain students on 14.3M TCGA tiles from only 11.8K WSIs and evaluate frozen encoders on 23 clinically curated downstream classification tasks. RepViT-based MuCoEdge students retain near-teacher performance while reducing model size by orders of magnitude: MuCoEdge-R2.3 and MuCoEdge-R1.5 reach 71.0% external AUROC, within 0.8 percentage points of the best teacher (Virchow2, 71.8%), while MuCoEdge-R2.3 obtains the best external F1 and the second-best AUPRC (51.8% and 53.3%). MuCoEdge-R1.0 reaches 70.9% AUROC with only 6.4M parameters and 1.12 GFLOPs. On a Raspberry Pi 5, sub-million-parameter MobileOne students achieve up to 605-fold single-tile speedup over Virchow2 while retaining 66.5% to 66.9% external AUROC, demonstrating that PFM-quality pathology representations can be moved toward practical edge deployment. Code is available at .

</details>

### [76] [UI2App: Benchmarking Visual Interaction Inference in Executable Web Application Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06306)
*Grace Man Chen, Litao Guo, Yifan Wu, Yiyu Chen, Yenchi Tseng, Sicheng Liu, Yuyu Luo, Ying-Cong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.SE, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 生成模型评测与数据集

Importance: 普通相关 (57.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出UI2App，首个针对从UI截图推断交互行为（交互推理）的可执行Web应用生成基准。包含327张截图、45个状态一致的截图集，从可执行性、导航可达性、视觉保真度和交互推理四个维度评估模型。实验表明，当前最先进的视觉语言模型在交互推理方面表现极差，最佳模型仅得7.5分（满分100），视觉保真度领先的模型在交互推理上排名第四，落后第一名5.2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Web页面生成方法主要依赖文本提示，对用户要求高且难以表达页面布局和跨页视觉连贯性。图像驱动范式（以UI截图作为输入）更贴近实际开发流程，但现有基准仅关注视觉保真度，缺乏对生成产物交互能力的系统评估。因此需要一个新的基准来评估模型从静态截图推断应用交互行为的能力。

Method: 构建UI2App基准，包含327张截图、45个状态一致的截图集，对应可运行的多路由Web应用。设计端到端评估流水线，从四个维度评估生成产物：(1) 可执行性，(2) 导航可达性，(3) 视觉保真度，(4) 交互推理（IIS指标）。IIS根据功能正确性和状态管理复杂度评估推断的交互，对任何有效实现均给予评分而非匹配单一参考。在六个前沿视觉语言模型上进行实验。

Result: 视觉保真度领先的模型在IIS上仅得7.5分，排名第四，落后IIS领先者5.2倍。高复杂度交互（如跨页面状态管理）仍是普遍瓶颈，半数评估模型在该维度得分为零。整体结果表明，从静态截图推断完整交互行为对当前模型仍是关键挑战。

Conclusion: UI2App揭示了当前视觉语言模型在Web应用生成中视觉重建与交互实现之间的显著能力差距。交互推理，尤其是跨页面状态管理等复杂交互，仍是模型面临的核心瓶颈。该基准为未来研究提供了新的评估方向，推动从单纯关注视觉质量转向同时关注功能正确性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了UI2App基准，专门用于评估从UI截图生成可执行Web应用时的交互推理能力，属于生成模型的评测基准。在multimodal_understanding_generation方向下存在[CANONICAL]的generation_benchmark（生成模型评测与数据集），该论文完全符合该子主题的定义。同时，该论文也涉及多模态生成（Web应用生成），因此也匹配multimodal_generation方向。

Importance reason: Focuses on UI-to-app generation benchmark, not directly on user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: New benchmark for interaction inference, reveals major gap in VLM interaction reasoning, low scores on cross-page state, not aligned with user's core interests

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated growing competence in web page generation. However, existing text-driven approaches rely on complex prompts that impose substantial demands on users and offer limited expressivity for page layout and cross-page visual coherence. Image-driven paradigms, which take UI screenshots as input, align more closely with real development workflows. However, current benchmarks focus primarily on visual fidelity and lack a systematic evaluation of the interaction capabilities in generated artifacts. To address this gap, we introduce UI2App, the first benchmark targeting interaction inference, the ability to recover application behavior from screenshots alone, without any textual or behavioral guidance. UI2App comprises 327 screenshots grouped into 45 state-coherent screenshot sets for runnable multi-route web applications. We design an end-to-end pipeline that evaluates each artifact along four dimensions: executability, navigation reachability, visual fidelity, and interaction inference. The interaction metric (IIS) assesses inferred interactions by functional correctness and state-management complexity, crediting any valid implementation rather than matching a single reference. Experiments on six frontier vision-language models reveal a marked capability mismatch between visual reconstruction and interaction realization: the visual-fidelity leader scores only 7.5 on IIS, ranking fourth and trailing the IIS leader by 5.2x. High-complexity interactions such as cross-page state remain a pervasive bottleneck, with half of the evaluated models scoring exactly zero on this dimension. Overall, the results indicate that inferring complete interaction behavior from static screenshots remains a key challenge for models.

</details>

### [77] [EAGOR: Embodied Reasoning in Omni-direction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06165)
*Shriram Damodaran, Soumyaratna Debnath, Yan Wu, Wei-Yun Yau, Addison Lin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 空间与 3D 多模态推理

Importance: 普通相关 (57.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: EAGOR 提出一种无需训练的几何感知框架，利用球谐函数信念场（SH-BF）在全向360°相机下进行递归贝叶斯方向估计，解决了传统ERP投影导致的畸变和不一致问题，在导航和方向推理任务上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM处理360°图像时将其投影为2D等距柱状投影（ERP）图像，忽略了球面观测的几何特性，导致在智能体运动引起的视角变换下方向估计不一致，尤其影响无地图导航任务。

Method: 提出EAGOR框架，将方向推理建模为球面上的递归贝叶斯估计。引入球谐函数信念场（SH-BF），利用球谐函数作为全局定义的旋转感知基函数，在球面流形上维持目标方向的连续信念分布，并在智能体运动下等变地传播该信念，无需训练骨干VLM。

Result: 在两个基准数据集和真实四足机器人实验中，EAGOR在HOS和OSR-Bench上分别取得+34.4%和+45.6%的平均相对提升，导航成功率提升+14.6%，步数减少17.7%，平均角度误差降低24.5%。

Conclusion: EAGOR通过球面几何感知的递归贝叶斯估计有效解决了360°方向推理中的视角变换不一致问题，为具身智能体的全向空间推理提供了无需训练的通用框架。

Classification reason: 该论文研究具身智能体在全向360°相机下的方向推理与导航，属于空间推理与具身推理范畴，与 canonical 子主题「空间与 3D 多模态推理」(spatial_3d_reasoning) 高度匹配。同时涉及具身导航，也可匹配 world_model 方向的 embodied_world_model，但核心贡献在于空间方向推理而非世界模型本身，故以 multimodal_understanding_generation 为主方向。

Importance reason: Novel geometry-aware 360° reasoning for embodied agents, but outside user's core continuous/multimodal generation and world model priorities. Signals: Embodied reasoning, 360° camera, spherical harmonic belief field, recursive Bayesian estimation, training-free

Abstract: Omni-directional (360°) cameras provide embodied agents with a holistic view of their surroundings, making them suited for directional reasoning in tasks such as navigation and object search. Existing Vision Language Models (VLMs) project 360° observations to 2D equirectangular projection (ERP) images and process them using architectures designed for perspective images. However, they ignore the spherical nature of 360° observations, where each pixel represents a viewing direction relative to the agent. Consequently, their direction estimates often become inconsistent under camera view transformations caused by agent motion. This limitation is particularly critical for map-free navigation, where the agent must continuously estimate the target direction in its egocentric frame. We propose EAGOR, a training-free, geometry-aware framework for embodied 360° directional reasoning. Instead of predicting target directions as ERP image coordinates, EAGOR formulates directional reasoning as recursive Bayesian estimation directly on the sphere. It maintains a continuous belief over target directions and propagates it equivariantly under agent motion without training the backbone VLMs. To achieve this, we introduce the Spherical Harmonic Belief Field (SH-BF), whose spherical harmonic representation provides a globally defined, rotation-aware basis for directional estimation on the spherical manifold. This formulation eliminates ERP seam discontinuities, latitude distortions, and interpolation errors. We evaluate EAGOR on two benchmark datasets and real-world experiments with a legged robot across directional reasoning tasks. EAGOR consistently outperforms existing methods, achieving average relative gains of +34.4% and +45.6% on HOS and OSR-Bench, respectively, while improving navigation success by +14.6%, reducing step count by 17.7%, and lowering mean angular error by 24.5%.

</details>


### [78] [Ground3D-LMM: Fine-Grained 3D Point Grounding and Spatial Reasoning with LMM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05493)
*Amol Harsh, Zongyan Han, Jean Lahoud, Ye Liu, Rao Muhammad Anwer, Hisham Cholakkal, Salman Khan, Fahad Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 空间与 3D 多模态推理

Importance: 普通相关 (57.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Ground3D-LMM 是一个统一的3D大多模态模型，支持点云（及可选RGB图像）输入，能够进行带有点级3D定位和度量数值输出的空间对话，涵盖物体和部件粒度以及多物体查询。论文还定义了3D Grounded Measurement任务，并构建了基于ScanNet/ScanNet++的大规模数据集（约250万QA对）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D大语言模型要么只能进行对话式回答但缺乏显式3D定位，要么只能做3D定位但不支持交互式、带度量感知的对话。缺乏一个统一模型，既能精确定位到3D区域，又能输出真实世界单位的物理度量（如尺寸、厚度、间距等）。

Method: 提出Ground3D-LMM统一模型，以点云和可选RGB图像为输入，通过设计支持点级定位和度量数值输出的架构，实现物体和部件粒度的3D空间对话。同时构建大规模数据集，包含密集的物体和部件标注，以及约250万QA对，覆盖8种任务类型。

Result: 在多个数据集和任务上的大量实验表明，Ground3D-LMM为带定位和度量感知的3D对话理解提供了强基线。

Conclusion: Ground3D-LMM首次实现了3D场景中带点级定位和度量数值输出的统一对话模型，填补了现有3D LMM在可验证、可度量响应方面的空白，为3D空间理解提供了新的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究3D点云场景中的细粒度空间定位与度量推理，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的空间与3D多模态推理（spatial_3d_reasoning）这一[CANONICAL]子主题，完美匹配其定义。

Importance reason: Strong 3D grounding and metric reasoning but outside user's priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: 3D point grounding, spatial reasoning, metric-aware dialogue, ScanNet dataset, part-level grounding

Abstract: Natural-language queries about 3D environments become actionable when responses are verifiable and metric. Verifiability requires explicit grounding to the referred 3D region, while metric answers report physical measurements in real-world units (e.g., size, thickness, clearance, and distance). Existing 3D large multimodal models (LMMs) approaches remain limited: conversational systems typically respond without explicit 3D grounding, while 3D grounding models are not designed for interactive, metric-aware dialogue. In this paper, we present Ground3D-LMM, a unified model that takes a point cloud and an optional RGB image as input and supports 3D spatial conversation with (i) point-grounded responses and (ii) metric numeric outputs at both object and part granularity, including multi-object queries. To evaluate this intersection of grounding and measurement, we define the 3D Grounded Measurement task, which requires predicting the referred 3D region and the corresponding metric quantities in real-world units. We introduce a large-scale dataset built on ScanNet and ScanNet++ datasets with dense object and part annotations and roughly 2.5M question-answer pairs spanning eight tasks, along with a manually verified test set. Extensive experiments on multiple datasets and tasks show that our proposed Ground3D-LMM model provides a strong baseline for grounded, metric-aware 3D conversational understanding. Our dataset and model are publicly available.

</details>


### [79] [CAIRN: Cross-Room 3D Scene Understanding with Topology-Aware Large Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06534)
*He Liang, Chenyang Ma, Yiming Zhang, Sangyun Shin, Andrew Markham, Niki Trigoni, Yuhang He*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 空间与 3D 多模态推理

Importance: 普通相关 (51.11) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: CAIRN 提出了一种拓扑感知的3D大语言模型，通过图神经网络编码物体-房间关系、引入房间token和层次化注意力掩码，实现了多房间3D场景的理解与推理，并在新提出的CAIRN-MR基准上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D-LLM仅能处理简化的单房间3D场景，无法对真实世界中包含多个互联房间和多样物体的家庭环境进行推理。作者旨在填补这一空白，使3D-LLM具备跨房间的3D场景理解能力。

Method: CAIRN 包含三个核心设计：(1) 通过图神经网络（GNN）为物体token注入房间内局部关系上下文；(2) 引入可学习的房间token实现房间级别的抽象表示；(3) 设计层次化注意力掩码（hierarchical attention mask）结合几何偏置，使注意力按场景拓扑结构路由信息，对齐transformer注意力与场景层次。

Result: 在CAIRN-MR基准（涵盖定位、描述和四种问答任务）上，CAIRN在所有任务上大幅超越先前3D-LLM；同时在五个单房间基准上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: CAIRN证明了将场景拓扑结构显式注入大语言模型可以有效提升多房间3D场景理解能力，为3D-LLM从简化单房间场景向真实复杂家庭环境迁移提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多房间3D场景理解，属于3D视觉定位与空间推理范畴，与[空间与 3D 多模态推理]（spatial_3d_reasoning）这一CANONICAL子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel topology-aware 3D-LLM for multi-room reasoning, but outside user's core continuous/multimodal generation and world model priorities. Signals: 3D scene understanding, multi-room reasoning, graph neural network, hierarchical attention, benchmark contribution

Abstract: Existing 3D scene-grounded Large Language Models (3D-LLMs) focus on answering questions grounded in simplified single-room 3D scenes, lacking the ability to reason over real-world household environments containing multiple interconnected rooms and diverse object categories. We introduce CAIRN, a topology-aware 3D-LLM for multi-room 3D scene understanding. CAIRN aligns transformer attention with scene hierarchy, giving the model explicit awareness of object-level relations and room-level connectivity. It enriches object tokens with room-local relational context via a graph neural network, introduces learned room tokens for room-level abstraction, and applies a hierarchical attention mask with geometric bias to route information according to scene topology. CAIRN is developed on CAIRN-MR, a benchmark we introduce on HM3D for multi-room 3D scene understanding, covering grounding, captioning, and four question-answering tasks that progressively evaluate from intra-room perception to cross-room reasoning. Experiments show that CAIRN outperforms prior 3D-LLMs by a large margin across all CAIRN-MR tasks while remaining competitive on five single-room benchmarks.

</details>

### [80] [Vision as Unified Multimodal Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06560)
*Xiaoyang Han, Jianhua Li, Kewang Deng, Zukai Chen, Xuanke Shi, Sihan Wang, Boxuan Li, Linyan Wang, Siyi Xie, Xin You, Jinsheng Quan, Zhongang Cai, Haiwen Diao, Ziwei Liu, Lei Yang, Dahua Lin, Quan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 统一多模态模型

Importance: 高优先级 (95.24) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SenseNova-Vision 将计算机视觉任务统一表达为多模态生成问题，利用统一多模态模型的原生文本和图像生成空间处理检测、分割、深度估计、关键点、姿态估计等多样视觉任务，无需任务特定架构或预测头，在多个基准上达到与专用模型相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前计算机视觉任务通常依赖任务特定的架构和预测头，导致不同任务之间缺乏统一性，难以集成到通用基础模型中。作者希望将视觉任务统一表达为多模态生成问题，使一个统一模型能够处理广泛的视觉任务，无需任务特定的架构修改。

Method: 提出 SenseNova-Vision，基于预训练的统一多模态模型，使用自然语言指令和可选的视觉提示来指定任务、目标区域/视角和解码约定。模型输出可以是文本（符号输出）、图像（密集空间预测）或文本+图像混合（组合任务）。构建了 SenseNova-Vision Corpus，将多样化的计算机视觉标注转换为与这些生成空间兼容的指令-响应对。模型主要在该语料上训练，辅以多模态数据保持能力，无需任务特定的预测头或架构修改。

Result: 单个统一模型在检测、OCR、关键点估计、分割、深度估计、表面法线预测、点图和相机姿态估计等广泛视觉任务上，匹配甚至超越了领先的任务专用系统。实验覆盖了结构化视觉理解、密集几何预测、分割和多视图视觉几何等多个领域。

Conclusion: 统一多模态生成是将计算机视觉能力集成到通用基础模型中的可扩展路径。通过将视觉任务统一表达为文本和图像生成，可以在不牺牲性能的前提下实现任务统一，为构建通用视觉基础模型提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出将计算机视觉任务统一表达为多模态生成问题，使用统一多模态模型（SenseNova-Vision）同时处理文本和图像输出，无需任务特定架构。这直接对应 [CANONICAL] 统一多模态模型（unified_multimodal_model）子主题，同时涉及多模态生成方向。

Importance reason: Directly aligns with unified multimodal model priority and uses continuous multimodal generation space, a key subtopic. Signals: Unified multimodal generation, No task-specific heads, SenseNova-Vision Corpus, Matches specialized models, Publicly available model and corpus

Abstract: We formulate computer vision as unified multimodal generation, where heterogeneous visual tasks are expressed in the native text and image generation spaces of a unified multimodal model, without task-specific architectures. Under this formulation, SenseNova-Vision uses natural-language instructions and optional visual prompts to specify tasks, target regions or views, and decoding conventions, and generates responses as text for symbolic outputs, images for dense spatial predictions, or mixed text-and-image outputs for compositional tasks. To support large-scale training, we convert diverse computer vision annotations into instruction-response examples compatible with these generation spaces, resulting in the SenseNova-Vision Corpus, a computer-vision instruction-response corpus spanning text, image, and mixed targets. Starting from an off-the-shelf pretrained unified multimodal model, SenseNova-Vision is trained primarily on this corpus, with auxiliary multimodal data used as a capability-preserving mixture, and requires no task-specific prediction heads or architectural modifications. The resulting model covers a broad range of vision tasks, including detection, OCR, keypoint estimation, segmentation, depth estimation, surface normal prediction, point maps, and camera pose estimation, while supporting language-defined variants that combine category, color, region, and other visual cues. Experiments show that a single unified model can match leading task-specialized systems across structured visual understanding, dense geometric prediction, segmentation, and multi-view visual geometry. These results suggest unified multimodal generation as a scalable route for integrating computer vision capabilities into general-purpose foundation models. The model and corpus are publicly available.

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_understanding_generation-vlm_mllm_general'></div>

## 视觉语言模型与 MLLM [[Back]](#toc)

### [81] [CanvasAgent: Enabling Complex Image Creation and Editing via Visual Tool Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05465)
*Hairui Zhu, Yiying Yang, Tengjin Weng, Ziyu Lu, Xiao Yao, Xiaoyang Ye, Lin Ma, Wenhao Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 高优先级 (84.23) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: CanvasAgent 是一个工具增强的多模态智能体，通过大规模数据集 CanvasCraft（14万条可执行轨迹）进行 SFT + GRPO 训练，学会编排多种视觉工具（生成、编辑、分割、合成等）完成复杂图像创建与编辑任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂图像创建与编辑往往需要多个模型/工具的协同工作（如生成、定位、分割、编辑、合成、文字读取、增强等），而现有多模态工具使用智能体主要针对感知、搜索或特定领域编辑，缺乏大规模可执行图像创建轨迹的监督数据。

Method: 构建 CanvasCraft 数据集（14万条可执行轨迹 + 1万条 RL 任务描述），训练 CanvasAgent 智能体：先通过 SFT 学习可执行的推理-动作轨迹，再使用 GRPO 结合结果级和过程级混合奖励进行优化。在 rollout 过程中，智能体检查中间结果、跟踪视觉资产、根据演化视觉状态调整工具决策。

Result: 实验从最终图像质量和轨迹行为两个维度评估，证明了 CanvasAgent 和 CanvasCraft 数据集在复杂多工具图像创建工作流中的有效性。

Conclusion: CanvasAgent 展示了通过工具编排实现复杂图像创建与编辑的可行路径，大规模可执行轨迹数据与混合奖励优化是提升多模态智能体视觉创作能力的关键。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 CanvasAgent，一个工具增强的多模态智能体，通过编排多种视觉工具（生成、编辑、分割、合成等）完成复杂图像创建与编辑任务。这属于多模态理解与生成方向下的视觉语言模型与 MLLM 范畴（工具使用型多模态智能体），同时与多模态生成方向相关。映射到 canonical subtopic "视觉语言模型与 MLLM"。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with multimodal understanding/generation and MLLM subtopic, but lacks direct connection to priority subtopics like continuous language models or autoregressive generation. Signals: multimodal agent, visual tool orchestration, SFT+GRPO training, complex image creation, CanvasCraft dataset

Abstract: Complex image creation and editing often require more than a single generation or editing model. A user request may involve synthesizing images, localizing objects, segmenting regions, editing selected content, compositing intermediate assets, reading text, and enhancing the final result. Such tasks shift multimodal agents from perception-augmented reasoning to manipulation-centered visual creation, where tools must actively transform visual states rather than merely inspect them. However, existing multimodal tool-use agents are mostly optimized for perception, search, or domain-specific editing, and lack large-scale supervision for executable image-creation trajectories. In this paper, we introduce CanvasCraft, a large-scale multimodal tool-use dataset for complex image creation and editing, and \textbf{CanvasAgent}, a tool-augmented multimodal agent that learns to orchestrate heterogeneous visual tools through multi-turn interaction. CanvasCraft contains 140K fully annotated executable trajectories and 10K RL task specifications. CanvasAgent is first trained with SFT to learn executable reasoning-action trajectories, and is then optimized with GRPO using a hybrid reward that combines outcome- and process-level signals. During rollout, CanvasAgent inspects intermediate results, tracks visual assets, and adapts tool decisions to the evolving visual state. Experiments evaluate both final image quality and trajectory behavior, demonstrating the effectiveness of CanvasAgent and the proposed dataset for complex multi-tool image creation workflows.

</details>


### [82] [Structured-Condensed Prompt Tuning in Vision-Language Models for Fine-grained Image Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06185)
*Xinda Liu, Qinyu Zhang, Weiqing Min, Guohua Geng, Shuqiang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (75.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出结构化压缩提示调优（SCPT），通过语义关系编码（SRE）显式建模类别间语义拓扑结构，并设计语义压缩损失（ScLoss）抑制冗余监督，提升CLIP在细粒度图像识别中的语义对齐与判别能力，在14个细粒度基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度图像识别中类别间差异微小，现有CLIP提示调优方法将类别标签视为孤立离散实体，忽略了类别间的层次依赖和语义关联，导致细粒度判别能力不足。

Method: 提出SCPT框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 语义关系编码（SRE），显式建模类别间语义拓扑结构，编码结构化标签关系；(2) 语义压缩损失（ScLoss），抑制冗余监督信息，从全局语义空间中提取判别性成分。

Result: 在14个细粒度图像识别基准上，SCPT在少样本和base-to-novel泛化设置下均达到SOTA性能，有效缓解了语义歧义问题。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模类别间语义结构并压缩冗余监督，SCPT显著提升了VLM在细粒度识别中的语义对齐和判别能力，验证了结构化语义建模在提示学习中的重要性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究视觉语言模型（CLIP）的提示调优方法，属于通用视觉语言模型范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "视觉语言模型与 MLLM" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Prompt tuning for fine-grained recognition is solid but incremental, and lacks direct connection to your priority continuous/multimodal or generation topics. Signals: prompt tuning, fine-grained recognition, CLIP, semantic structure, few-shot generalization

Abstract: Fine-grained image recognition poses a significant challenge due to the substantial expertise and effort required for manual annotation. Vision-language models (VLMs) like CLIP provide a compelling zero-shot alternative, reducing reliance on extensive labeled data. However, their ability to capture subtle distinctions remains limited, leading to subpar recognition performance. While prompt tuning has proven effective for adapting VLMs, most existing methods treat class labels as isolated, discrete entities, overlooking the rich semantic relationships between them. This oversimplified assumption limits the model's ability to capture hierarchical dependencies and inter-class correlations -- both critical for distinguishing visually similar categories. The problem is especially acute in fine-grained classification, where accurate recognition depends on understanding complex label semantics. To address this, we propose Structured-Condensed Prompt Tuning (SCPT), which enhances semantic structure modeling in prompt learning. Specifically, we introduce Semantic Relation Encoding (SRE) to explicitly model inter-class semantic topology and encode structured label relationships. In parallel, we design a Semantic Condensation loss (ScLoss) to suppress redundant supervision and extract discriminative components from the global semantic space. Together, these components significantly improve semantic alignment and fine-grained discrimination. Extensive experiments on 14 fine-grained benchmarks show that SCPT effectively mitigates semantic ambiguity and achieves state-of-the-art performance in both few-shot and base-to-novel generalization settings.

</details>


### [83] [A VLM-Enhanced Framework for Comprehensive Traffic Sign Condition Assessment Integrating Daytime Visual Performance and Nighttime Retroreflectivity Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06478)
*Linlin Zhang, Neema Jakisa Owor, Xiang Yu, Abby Watts, Yaw Adu-Gyamfi*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 普通相关 (64.7) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于 VLM 的交通标志状况评估框架，使用三个微调的视觉语言模型（LLaVA、Qwen、InternVL）评估日间视觉性能（可读性、颜色、表面完整性、周围环境），并结合 LiDAR 衍生的夜间逆反射率评估，综合计算标志状况指数（SCI），为交通标志维护提供低成本、自动化的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统交通标志评估依赖人工检查，主观性强、劳动密集且存在安全隐患；逆反射仪价格昂贵，小型机构难以负担。现有研究大多只关注日间或夜间单一维度，缺乏综合评估框架。

Method: 微调三个 VLM（LLaVA、Qwen、InternVL）用于日间视觉性能评估（可读性、颜色、表面与形状完整性、周围环境四个维度），通过情感分析和 CLIP 评分将 VLM 预测转化为数值分数；夜间性能通过 LiDAR 获取逆反射率数据并校准；最终将日间和夜间评分整合为综合标志状况指数（SCI）。

Result: LLaVA 和 Qwen 在所有维度上表现优于 InternVL，双向余弦相似度达 0.67-0.76。在 462 个验证标志中，68 个因逆反射率不足被标记为需要立即更换。

Conclusion: 该框架为交通标志状况评估提供了一种经济高效、自动化的替代方案，综合了日间视觉性能和夜间逆反射率两个维度，具有实际应用价值。

Classification reason: 该论文使用多个微调的 VLM（LLaVA、Qwen、InternVL）进行交通标志的日间视觉性能评估，属于视觉语言模型在特定应用场景下的微调与部署，与 [CANONICAL] 的 vlm_mllm_general（视觉语言模型与 MLLM）高度匹配。

Importance reason: This paper focuses on traffic sign assessment, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and contains negative keywords like traffic prediction. Signals: traffic sign assessment, VLM fine-tuning, LiDAR retroreflectivity, negative keyword traffic prediction, no autoregressive or video generation focus

Abstract: Traffic signs are crucial components of road safety, serving as visual tools under all lighting conditions. The Manual on Uniform Traffic Control Devices (MUTCD) specifies daytime visual factors such as legibility and color contrast, and nighttime retroreflectivity requirements. Traditional assessment methods rely on manual inspections, which the Federal Highway Administration (FHWA) notes are subjective, labor-intensive and pose safety concerns, while retroreflectometers are expensive and unaffordable for smaller agencies. Most existing studies focus on either daytime factors or nighttime retroreflectivity but rarely integrate both aspects comprehensively. This study develops a novel framework that systematically evaluates traffic signs through integrated daytime-nighttime assessment. The methodology employs three fine-tuned Vision Language Models (VLMs) for daytime visual performance assessment across four key factors: legibility, color, surface and shape integrity, and surrounding environment conditions. VLM predictions are converted to numerical scores through sentiment analysis and Contrastive Language-Image Pre-Training (CLIP) scoring, while nighttime performance is assessed using LiDAR-derived retroreflectivity following established calibration procedures. The framework integrates these components into a comprehensive Sign Condition Index (SCI) for maintenance guidance. Evaluation results demonstrated that LLaVA and Qwen outperformed InternVL, achieving bidirectional cosine similarity scores of 0.67-0.76 across all factors. Among 462 validated traffic signs, 68 were flagged by the proposed framework as requiring immediate replacement due to inadequate retroreflectivity performance. This research provides a cost-effective alternative to traditional manual inspections for comprehensive traffic sign condition assessment.

</details>
