### [84] [LEGATO 2: Toward Multimodal Sheet Music Recognition and Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05769)
*Guang Yang, Brian Siyuan Zheng, Victoria Ebert, Noah A. Smith*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (55.14) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Legato 2 提出了一种新的乐谱光学识别（OMR）流水线，首次采用逐系统（system-by-system）顺序处理方式，沿水平谱线方向读取乐谱，而非将整页视为无差别图像，从而更好地扩展到任意长输入。它也是首个能够生成包含嵌入文本内容（如标题和注释）的符号转录的 OMR 模型。该模型在多个数据集上超越先前最优方法，且符号转录能增强大语言模型对密集音乐文档的理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 OMR 方法通常将整页乐谱作为整体处理，难以扩展到长乐谱，且无法处理乐谱中嵌入的文本内容（如标题、注释）。同时，大语言模型对密集音乐文档的理解能力有限，需要更好的符号转录作为补充输入。

Method: Legato 2 流水线包含两个阶段：首先使用系统级分割（system-level segmentation）将乐谱图像按水平谱线系统切分；然后使用自回归视觉语言模型（autoregressive vision-LM）逐系统地生成符号转录，同时捕获局部符号细节和整体乐谱结构。

Result: 在多个 OMR 数据集上，Legato 2 一致超越先前最优方法。此外，实验表明，将符号转录作为视觉输入的补充，能显著提升前沿语言模型对密集音乐文档的理解能力。

Conclusion: Legato 2 在 OMR 和下游乐谱理解任务上均建立了新的最优性能，证明了逐系统顺序处理策略和符号转录与视觉输入互补的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究乐谱图像识别与理解（OMR），属于文档理解范畴。乐谱是一种特殊的文档格式，论文涉及从图像中提取符号标注和语义知识，与多模态文档理解高度相关，因此映射到 canonical 子主题"视觉问答与文档理解"（multimodal_vqa_doc）。

Importance reason: Specialized OMR paper with no direct alignment to priority subtopics or boost keywords. Signals: specialized domain, no continuous/multimodal language model, no video/image generation, no world model, no efficient inference

Abstract: We propose a novel pipeline, Legato 2, for extracting symbolic notation and semantic knowledge from images of sheet music. Legato 2 features the first large-scale neural model for optical music recognition (OMR) to operate sequentially on a system-by-system basis, following the horizontal lines of notation as they are read on the page, rather than treating the page as an undifferentiated image, enabling better scaling to arbitrarily long inputs. It is also the first OMR model capable of generating symbolic transcriptions that include embedded textual content, such as titles and annotations. The pipeline combines system-level segmentation with an autoregressive vision-LM to capture both local notation details and score structure. Across multiple datasets, Legato 2 consistently outperforms prior state of the art. We also show that symbolic transcriptions complement visual inputs for frontier language models, improving their interpretation of dense musical documents. Legato 2 establishes new state-of-the-art performance in both OMR and downstream sheet music understanding.

</details>


### [85] [Revisiting Scene Graph Generation from the Perspective of Detector-Conditioned Reachability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06176)
*Runfeng Qu, Pia K Bideau, Ole Hall, Julie Ouerfelli-Ethier, Klaus Obermayer, Olaf Hellwich*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (47.36) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文从检测器条件可达性的视角重新审视场景图生成（SGG），发现基于检测器的方法和基于查询的方法在预测行为上存在互补性。作者提出 Dual-SGG 方法，通过双查询设计融合两种推理机制，在 Visual Genome、Open Images v6 和 GQA-200 数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有场景图生成方法可分为基于检测器（detector-based）和基于查询（query-based）两类，但两者预测行为的差异尚未被系统分析。作者希望从检测器条件可达性的角度揭示这种差异，并利用互补性提升 SGG 性能。

Method: 设计受控实验从检测器条件可达性视角分析两类方法的预测差异，发现它们提供互补线索。基于此观察，提出 Dual-SGG 方法，采用双查询设计（dual-query design）融合基于检测器和基于查询两种推理机制，以利用互补的预测行为。

Result: 在 Visual Genome、Open Images v6 和 GQA-200 三个数据集上的大量实验表明，所提出的 Dual-SGG 方法有效提升了场景图生成的性能。

Conclusion: 检测器条件可达性视角揭示了基于检测器和基于查询的 SGG 方法在预测行为上的互补性，融合两种机制的双查询设计能够有效提升场景图生成效果。

Classification reason: 该论文研究场景图生成（Scene Graph Generation），属于视觉场景理解任务，与视觉问答（VQA）和场景理解密切相关。场景图生成是视觉理解的重要子领域，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 的 multimodal_vqa_doc（视觉问答与文档理解）最为合适。

Importance reason: Scene graph generation is a niche subtask with no direct alignment to your priority subtopics or boost keywords. Signals: scene graph generation, detector-based vs query-based, Visual Genome, complementary reasoning, no priority subtopic match

Abstract: Scene graph generation (SGG) approaches can be broadly classified into detector-based and query-based methods according to their underlying reasoning mechanisms. However, the discrepancy in their predictive behaviors, induced by these distinct mechanisms, has not been systematically analyzed. In this work, we design a controlled experimental setup to examine prediction discrepancies from the perspective of detector-conditioned reachability. The results suggest clear complementary clues. Motivated by this observation, we introduce a Dual-SGG method that consolidates both reasoning mechanisms via a dual-query design, thereby leveraging the complementary predictive behaviors of both detector-based and query-based methods. Extensive experiments on the Visual Genome, Open Images v6, and GQA-200 datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [86] [VaseMuseum: Digital Intelligent Museum for Ancient Greek Pottery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06374)
*Jiazi Wang, Nonghai Zhang, Qiushi Xie, Zeyu Zhang, Yufeng Chen, Yang Zhao, Ling Shao, Hao Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: VaseMuseum 是一个轻量级模块化多模态智能体框架，面向古希腊陶器数字博物馆，通过结合2D/3D多模态感知、外部知识检索、源级与响应级可靠性控制以及无训练的GRPO式选择机制，提升了VLM在知识密集型文化遗产问答中的引用有效性、减少幻觉并生成更中立的答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在文化遗产领域（如古希腊陶器），VLM面临两大挑战：1）开放解释需要细粒度2D/3D视觉证据与专业知识结合，但检索可能引入弱来源和不可验证的引用；2）证据不完整或模糊时，VLM常给出自信但无依据的回答，缺乏校准的不确定性表达。

Method: 提出VaseMuseum框架，包含交互式虚拟博物馆和VaseAgent智能体。VaseAgent通过多模态感知（2D图像+3D文物）、3D感知推理、外部知识检索（权威网站和博物馆知识源）和推理时可靠性控制来工作。具体包括：源级控制选择多样且可验证的证据；响应级控制将生成声明与证据池比对，在证据不足时鼓励中立、受证据约束的回答；此外采用无训练的GRPO式选择机制，在不更新VLM主干的情况下优先选择具有有效引用和校准置信度的响应。

Result: 在逼真的数字博物馆模拟实验中，与启用搜索的VLM基线相比，VaseMuseum在引用有效性、减少知识密集型查询的幻觉以及模糊情况下生成更中立答案方面均有提升。

Conclusion: VaseMuseum通过模块化的可靠性控制机制，有效提升了VLM在文化遗产数字博物馆场景中的可信度，为将VLM应用于专业知识密集型领域提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文构建了一个面向古希腊陶器数字博物馆的多模态智能体框架，涉及基于VLM的视觉问答、知识检索与证据推理，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的视觉问答与文档理解（multimodal_vqa_doc）范畴。虽然应用领域是文化遗产，但核心任务是VLM驱动的知识密集型视觉问答与证据验证，与现有canonical subtopic高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on a specialized cultural heritage application with limited methodological novelty for core continuous multimodal or video generation priorities. Signals: domain-specific museum agent, lightweight modular framework, GRPO-style selection without training, citation validity improvement, no direct connection to continuous language/video generation/world model priorities

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have made interactive digital museums increasingly feasible by connecting 3D digitization with natural-language artifact exploration. However, in cultural heritage domains such as ancient Greek pottery, reliable VLM assistance is limited by two challenges. First, open-ended interpretation requires grounding fine-grained 2D/3D visual evidence in specialized curatorial knowledge, yet the retrieval process may introduce weak sources and unverifiable references. Second, when the available evidence is incomplete, noisy, or ambiguous, VLMs often produce confident but unsupported answers instead of calibrated uncertainty. To address these challenges, we propose VaseMuseum, a lightweight and modular multimodal agent framework for intelligent digital museums of ancient Greek pottery. VaseMuseum combines an interactive virtual museum with VaseAgent, which supports both 2D images and 3D artifacts through multimodal perception, 3D-aware reasoning, external knowledge retrieval, and inference-time reliability control. Specifically, VaseAgent retrieves evidence from authoritative web and museum knowledge sources, and source-level control selects diverse and verifiable evidence before generation. Meanwhile, response-level control checks generated claims against the evidence pool and encourages neutral, evidence-bounded answers when support is insufficient or conflicting. Moreover, a training-free GRPO-style selection mechanism favors responses with valid references and calibrated confidence without updating the VLM backbone. Experiments in a realistic digital museum simulation show that VaseMuseum improves citation validity, reduces hallucinations on knowledge-intensive queries, and produces more neutral answers under ambiguity compared with search-enabled VLM baselines.

</details>


### [87] [MonoIR-RS: Infrared Remote Sensing Vision-Language Learning with CLIP and VLM Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06552)
*Jiaju Han, Ma Yaqi, Yahui Chai, Xuemeng Sun, Xin Li, Qike Zhang, Yingying Zhao, Xiang Chen, Luwei Yang, Chengyin Hu, Jiahuan Long*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (44.11) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: MonoIR-RS 是一个大规模红外遥感视觉-语言数据集与基准，通过合成红外图像、CLIP对比适应和VLM指令微调，将红外遥感证据与语言对齐，显著提升了红外模态的检索和描述能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥感视觉-语言资源主要关注可见光波段，红外遥感图像（具有强度结构、目标-背景对比度、光照不变性等独特信息）的视觉-语言理解尚未被充分探索。

Method: 从FusionRS同源数据池构建MonoIR-RS数据集，包含60万张合成红外图像和59,032条红外感知描述记录；对5个CLIP骨干和6个VLM骨干进行红外感知微调，将红外模态与RGB双模态学习解耦。

Result: 红外感知适应使CLIP平均召回率提升最多12.8个点，VLM描述的红外线索覆盖率达到100%，RGB颜色泄漏降至接近零；合成红外图像在AVIID基准上比灰度转换更接近真实热成像。

Conclusion: MonoIR-RS为红外遥感证据与语言对齐提供了可控、可复现的测试平台，证明了红外感知数据构建与模型适应策略的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文构建红外遥感视觉-语言数据集，进行CLIP对比学习和VLM指令微调，属于遥感领域的视觉问答与理解任务，映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "视觉问答与文档理解"（multimodal_vqa_doc）。

Importance reason: Focuses on infrared remote sensing, which is a negative keyword, and does not align with priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Infrared remote sensing, negative keyword match, no overlap with priority subtopics, low personal relevance

Abstract: Infrared remote-sensing imagery captures intensity structure, object-background contrast, and illumination-invariant cues often invisible in RGB imagery. Yet, most remote-sensing vision-language resources and models focus on visible-band semantics, leaving infrared vision-language understanding underexplored. We introduce MonoIR-RS, a large-scale infrared remote-sensing vision-language dataset and benchmark that couples IR-aware data construction with CLIP-style contrastive adaptation and VLM instruction tuning. Built from the same source pool and split as FusionRS, MonoIR-RS retains the infrared image as the model-facing modality, yielding 600,000 synthesized infrared images and 59,032 retained IR-aware caption records. The model experiments use this retained language-supervision subset, whose captions rewrite supervision around grayscale structure and infrared-style contrast instead of RGB appearance. We show that the synthesized infrared imagery is markedly closer to real thermal imagery than a grayscale conversion on the AVIID benchmark. We fine-tune five CLIP backbones and six VLM backbones, and calibrate them against zero-shot behavior: IR-aware adaptation lifts CLIP mean recall by up to 12.8 points and drives VLM captioning IR-cue coverage to 100% while reducing residual RGB-color leakage to near zero. By isolating the infrared modality from RGB-IR dual-modal learning, MonoIR-RS offers a controlled, reproducible testbed for aligning infrared remote-sensing evidence with language.

</details>

### [88] [Light-Omni: Reflex over Reasoning in Agentic Video Understanding with Long-Term Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05511)
*Chang Nie, Jiaju Wei, Junlan Feng, Chaoyou Fu, Caifeng Shan*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视频理解与时序推理

Importance: 高优先级 (84.23) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Light-Omni 提出了一种轻量级多模态智能体框架，通过双上下文状态（全局多模态脚本 + 参数化潜状态）实现单次前向传播的反射式视频理解，避免了迭代推理带来的高延迟和计算开销，在多个视频基准上取得更优的精度-效率平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频智能体依赖"侦探式"迭代推理（如搜索、证据聚合）来补偿全局上下文缺失和检索语义不对齐的问题，导致高昂的计算成本和延迟。作者希望设计一种更轻量、反射式的视频理解框架，避免冗长的迭代推理。

Method: Light-Omni 设计了双上下文状态：(1) 全局状态——一个有限大小的多模态脚本，通过层次化合并从情景记忆中持续整合，保留近期细节同时总结过去事件；(2) 参数化潜状态——以全局状态为条件生成，直接驱动自主行动并产生检索嵌入，实现语义对齐的检索和反射式响应，无需迭代推理。

Result: 在多个视频基准上，Light-Omni 相比 M3-Agent 平均准确率提升 2.4%，速度提升 12.1 倍，GPU 内存效率提升 2.6 倍。同时可作为记忆系统提升现有 MLLM 的性能和效率。

Conclusion: Light-Omni 验证了通过精心设计的双上下文状态，可以在避免迭代推理的前提下实现高效、准确的智能体视频理解，为长时序视频理解提供了一种轻量级且可扩展的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Light-Omni，一个面向智能体视频理解的多模态框架，核心贡献在于长时序视频理解中的长期记忆与高效推理。这与 [CANONICAL] 视频理解与时序推理 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Proposes a lightweight agentic video understanding framework with dual-context memory that achieves strong accuracy-efficiency trade-offs, aligning with user's interest in efficient inference and multimodal understanding. Signals: efficient inference, multimodal agent, video understanding, long-term memory, reflex over reasoning

Abstract: Agentic video understanding equips models with long-term memory to autonomously process and respond to continuous, long-horizon multimodal streams. However, advanced video agents often rely on ``detective-style'' iterative reasoning for action control (e.g., $\mathtt{search}$) and evidence aggregation, incurring prohibitive costs and latency. We argue that such heavy reasoning primarily compensates for the lack of global context and semantic misalignment in retrieval. This paper introduces Light-Omni, a multimodal agent framework for reflexive and lightweight video understanding. It achieves this through dual contextual states that instantly build the required context in a single forward pass. First, we maintain a global state, a finite-sized multimodal script continuously consolidated from episodic memory, serving as the global context for Light-Omni. Through hierarchical merging, it preserves recent details while summarizing past events. Second, conditioned on this global context, we generate a parametric latent state that directly drives autonomous actions and produces retrieval embeddings, with minimal latency. Benefiting from this coupled design, Light-Omni achieves semantically aligned retrieval and reflexive responses while avoiding iterative reasoning. Extensive experiments validate the effectiveness of Light-Omni across multiple video benchmarks. Notably, it outperforms M3-Agent with an average 2.4% accuracy gain, a 12.1$\times$ speedup, and a 2.6$\times$ improvement in GPU memory efficiency. Furthermore, it serves as a memory system to enhance both the performance and efficiency of existing MLLMs. Project page: .
